Phase 1 is below as code to paste into the chat. It’s untested because I can’t run your app from here, so the check at the end matters.

You don’t need to create any image files. The icons are generated by code (step 3, src/app/icons/[name]/route.tsx).

HTTPS for the phone. Chrome only offers “Install” over HTTPS, so for the phone test, deploy the repo to Vercel with the same env vars as .env.local. On your PC, localhost works for testing.

Step 3: new files

supabase/migrations/202611080017_profile_timezone_valid.sql. Also paste it into the Supabase SQL editor and run it.

sql
-- Reject unknown time zones so a typo can never break log date calculation.
create or replace function public.validate_profile_timezone()
returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from pg_timezone_names where name = new.timezone) then
    raise exception 'Unknown time zone: %', new.timezone using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;

drop trigger if exists profiles_validate_timezone on public.profiles;
create trigger profiles_validate_timezone
  before insert or update of timezone on public.profiles
  for each row execute function public.validate_profile_timezone();

src/lib/dates.ts

ts
export const DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE = "Asia/Kolkata";

export function isValidTimeZone(timeZone: string): boolean {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** The calendar date (YYYY-MM-DD) of an instant in the given time zone. */
export function localDate(timeZone: string = DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE, instant: Date = new Date()): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" }).format(instant);
}

export function shiftDate(date: string, days: number): string {
  const value = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
  value.setUTCDate(value.getUTCDate() + days);
  return value.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

src/lib/dates.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isValidTimeZone, localDate, shiftDate } from "./dates";

describe("dates", () => {
  it("uses the profile time zone, not the server's, around midnight", () => {
    expect(localDate("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:20:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-08"); // 11:50 pm IST
    expect(localDate("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:40:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-09"); // 12:10 am IST
    expect(localDate("UTC", new Date("2026-11-08T18:40:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-08");
  });
  it("shifts calendar dates across month and leap boundaries", () => {
    expect(shiftDate("2026-03-01", -1)).toBe("2026-02-28");
    expect(shiftDate("2028-03-01", -1)).toBe("2028-02-29");
    expect(shiftDate("2026-12-31", 1)).toBe("2027-01-01");
  });
  it("validates time zone names", () => {
    expect(isValidTimeZone("Asia/Kolkata")).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTimeZone("Mars/Base")).toBe(false);
  });
});

src/lib/api.ts (one helper for all authenticated calls; later phases use it)

ts
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, readonly status: number) {
    super(message);
  }
}

type Options = Omit<RequestInit, "body"> & { json?: unknown };

export async function apiFetch<T = unknown>(path: string, options: Options = {}): Promise<T> {
  const { json, headers: initHeaders, ...rest } = options;
  const { data } = await supabase().auth.getSession();
  const headers = new Headers(initHeaders);
  if (data.session) headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${data.session.access_token}`);
  let body: string | undefined;
  if (json !== undefined) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
    body = JSON.stringify(json);
  }
  const response = await fetch(path, { ...rest, headers, body });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
    try {
      const payload = (await response.json()) as { error?: string };
      if (payload.error) message = payload.error;
    } catch {
      /* keep the generic message */
    }
    throw new ApiError(message, response.status);
  }
  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  return (await response.json()) as T;
}

src/lib/supabase-server.ts

ts
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export async function createSupabaseServerClient() {
  const store = await cookies();
  return createServerClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!, {
    cookies: {
      getAll: () => store.getAll(),
      setAll: (list) => {
        try {
          list.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => store.set(name, value, options));
        } catch {
          /* Called from a Server Component; the middleware refreshes the session instead. */
        }
      },
    },
  });
}

src/lib/pwa-install.ts (keeps Chrome’s one-time install event so any screen can use it)

ts
export type BeforeInstallPromptEvent = Event & {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: "accepted" | "dismissed" }>;
};

let deferred: BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null = null;
let installed = false;
let started = false;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const notify = () => listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());

export function startInstallCapture() {
  if (started || typeof window === "undefined") return;
  started = true;
  window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    deferred = event as BeforeInstallPromptEvent;
    notify();
  });
  window.addEventListener("appinstalled", () => {
    deferred = null;
    installed = true;
    notify();
  });
}

export function subscribeInstall(listener: () => void) {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}
export const getDeferredPrompt = () => deferred;
export const installedThisSession = () => installed;

export async function runInstallPrompt(): Promise<"accepted" | "dismissed" | "unavailable"> {
  const event = deferred;
  if (!event) return "unavailable";
  deferred = null; // the browser allows each event to be used once
  notify();
  await event.prompt();
  return (await event.userChoice).outcome;
}

src/components/pwa/PwaRegister.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { startInstallCapture } from "@/lib/pwa-install";

export function PwaRegister() {
  useEffect(() => {
    startInstallCapture();
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" || !("serviceWorker" in navigator)) return;
    navigator.serviceWorker.register("/sw.js", { scope: "/" }).catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  return null;
}

src/components/pwa/InstallPrompt.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useReducer, useState } from "react";
import { getDeferredPrompt, installedThisSession, runInstallPrompt, startInstallCapture, subscribeInstall } from "@/lib/pwa-install";

const DISMISSED_KEY = "ahara.install.dismissedAt";
const SNOOZE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function isStandalone() {
  return window.matchMedia("(display-mode: standalone)").matches || (navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
}
function dismissedRecently() {
  try {
    const at = Number(window.localStorage.getItem(DISMISSED_KEY));
    return at > 0 && Date.now() - at < SNOOZE_MS;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
function rememberDismissal() {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(DISMISSED_KEY, String(Date.now()));
  } catch {
    /* storage unavailable: the card simply shows again next visit */
  }
}

export function InstallPrompt({ variant }: { variant: "hero" | "card" }) {
  const [, refresh] = useReducer((n: number) => n + 1, 0);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [standalone, setStandalone] = useState(false);
  const [snoozed, setSnoozed] = useState(false);
  const [android, setAndroid] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    startInstallCapture();
    setStandalone(isStandalone());
    setSnoozed(dismissedRecently());
    setAndroid(/android/i.test(navigator.userAgent));
    setReady(true);
    return subscribeInstall(refresh);
  }, []);

  const event = getDeferredPrompt();
  if (!ready || standalone || installedThisSession()) return null;
  if (variant === "card" && (snoozed || !event)) return null;
  if (variant === "hero" && !event && !android) return null;

  async function install() {
    const outcome = await runInstallPrompt();
    if (outcome === "dismissed" && variant === "card") {
      rememberDismissal();
      setSnoozed(true);
    }
  }
  function notNow() {
    rememberDismissal();
    setSnoozed(true);
  }

  return (
    <section className={variant === "hero" ? "install-hero" : "install-card"} aria-label="Install Ahara">
      <div>
        <p className="install-title">Install Ahara on your phone</p>
        <p className="install-text">Open it full-screen from your home screen, like any other app.</p>
        {!event && <p className="install-text">In Chrome, tap the ⋮ menu, then “Install app” (or “Add to Home screen”).</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="install-actions">
        {event && <button className="button primary" onClick={() => void install()}>Install app</button>}
        {variant === "card" && <button className="button secondary" onClick={notNow}>Not now</button>}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

public/sw.js

js
// Ahara service worker (P1): lets Chrome treat Ahara as an installable app.
// Offline caching and the "update available" notice arrive in P10.
self.addEventListener("install", () => {});
self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => event.waitUntil(self.clients.claim()));
self.addEventListener("fetch", () => {});

src/app/icons/[name]/route.tsx (draws the app icons: an orange tile with an “A”)

tsx
import { ImageResponse } from "next/og";

export const dynamic = "force-static";

const variants = {
  "icon-192.png": { size: 192, maskable: false },
  "icon-512.png": { size: 512, maskable: false },
  "icon-maskable-512.png": { size: 512, maskable: true },
  "apple-touch-icon.png": { size: 180, maskable: false },
} as const;

export function generateStaticParams() {
  return Object.keys(variants).map((name) => ({ name }));
}

export async function GET(_request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ name: string }> }) {
  const { name } = await params;
  const variant = variants[name as keyof typeof variants];
  if (!variant) return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
  const { size, maskable } = variant;
  const tile = maskable ? size : Math.round(size * 0.84);
  return new ImageResponse(
    (
      <div style={{ display: "flex", width: size, height: size, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", background: "#090909" }}>
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            width: tile,
            height: tile,
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "center",
            background: "#ff6a00",
            borderRadius: maskable ? 0 : Math.round(size * 0.2),
            color: "#090909",
            fontSize: Math.round(size * (maskable ? 0.44 : 0.56)),
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          A
        </div>
      </div>
    ),
    { width: size, height: size },
  );
}

src/middleware.ts

ts
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

const PUBLIC_PATHS = new Set(["/", "/login", "/auth-error"]);

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({ request });
  const supabase = createServerClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!, {
    cookies: {
      getAll: () => request.cookies.getAll(),
      setAll: (list) => {
        list.forEach(({ name, value }) => request.cookies.set(name, value));
        response = NextResponse.next({ request });
        list.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => response.cookies.set(name, value, options));
      },
    },
  });
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const isPublic = PUBLIC_PATHS.has(pathname) || pathname.startsWith("/auth/");

  if (!user && !isPublic) {
    const url = request.nextUrl.clone();
    url.pathname = "/login";
    url.search = "";
    return NextResponse.redirect(url);
  }
  if (user && (pathname === "/" || pathname === "/login")) {
    const url = request.nextUrl.clone();
    url.pathname = "/today";
    url.search = "";
    return NextResponse.redirect(url);
  }
  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/((?!api/|_next/|icons/|sw\\.js|manifest\\.webmanifest|favicon\\.ico|.*\\.(?:png|jpg|jpeg|svg|ico|webp|txt|js|css)$).*)"],
};

src/app/auth/callback/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase-server";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const { searchParams, origin } = new URL(request.url);
  const code = searchParams.get("code");
  if (code) {
    const supabase = await createSupabaseServerClient();
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.exchangeCodeForSession(code);
    if (!error) return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}/today`);
  }
  return NextResponse.redirect(`${origin}/auth-error`);
}

src/components/app-shell/SessionProvider.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from "react";

export type SessionInfo = { userId: string; email: string | null; displayName: string | null; timeZone: string };

const SessionContext = createContext<SessionInfo | null>(null);

export function SessionProvider({ value, children }: { value: SessionInfo; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <SessionContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</SessionContext.Provider>;
}
export function useSession(): SessionInfo | null {
  return useContext(SessionContext);
}

src/components/app-shell/SignOutButton.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export function SignOutButton({ className = "button secondary" }: { className?: string }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function signOut() {
    setBusy(true);
    await supabase().auth.signOut();
    router.replace("/login");
    router.refresh();
  }
  return (
    <button className={className} onClick={() => void signOut()} disabled={busy}>
      {busy ? "Signing out…" : "Sign out"}
    </button>
  );
}

next.config.ts

ts
import type { NextConfig } from "next";
import path from "node:path";

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  outputFileTracingRoot: path.join(process.cwd()),
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        // The service worker file must never be cached, or updates would not be noticed.
        source: "/sw.js",
        headers: [
          { key: "Cache-Control", value: "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate" },
          { key: "Service-Worker-Allowed", value: "/" },
        ],
      },
    ];
  },
};
export default nextConfig;

src/app/(app)/layout.tsx

tsx
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/app-shell/AppShell";
import { SessionProvider } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE } from "@/lib/dates";
import { createSupabaseServerClient } from "@/lib/supabase-server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function AppLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  const supabase = await createSupabaseServerClient();
  const {
    data: { user },
  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect("/login");
  const { data: profile } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("display_name,timezone").eq("id", user.id).maybeSingle();
  const value = {
    userId: user.id,
    email: user.email ?? null,
    displayName: (profile?.display_name as string | null | undefined) ?? null,
    timeZone: (profile?.timezone as string | undefined) ?? DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE,
  };
  return (
    <SessionProvider value={value}>
      <AppShell>{children}</AppShell>
    </SessionProvider>
  );
}

src/components/app-shell/AppShell.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { InstallPrompt } from "@/components/pwa/InstallPrompt";

const primary = [
  { href: "/today", label: "Today", icon: "◉", match: ["/today", "/log"] },
  { href: "/insights", label: "Insights", icon: "⌁", match: ["/insights"] },
  { href: "/add", label: "Add", icon: "+", match: ["/add"] },
  { href: "/activity", label: "Activity", icon: "↗", match: ["/activity"] },
  { href: "/settings", label: "Profile", icon: "◎", match: ["/settings", "/goals", "/favourites", "/export"] },
] as const;
const secondary = [
  { href: "/favourites", label: "Favourites" },
  { href: "/goals", label: "Goals" },
] as const;

const matches = (pathname: string, prefixes: readonly string[]) =>
  prefixes.some((prefix) => pathname === prefix || pathname.startsWith(`${prefix}/`));

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className="desktop-sidebar" aria-label="Primary navigation">
        <Link className="brand" href="/today"><span className="brand-mark">A</span><span>Ahara</span></Link>
        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          {primary.map((item) => (
            <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} className={matches(pathname, item.match) ? "nav-link active" : "nav-link"}>
              <span aria-hidden="true">{item.icon}</span>{item.label}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="sidebar-divider" />
        <nav className="sidebar-nav secondary">
          {secondary.map((item) => (
            <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} className={matches(pathname, [item.href]) ? "nav-link active" : "nav-link"}>{item.label}</Link>
          ))}
        </nav>
      </aside>
      <div className="app-frame">
        <header className="mobile-topbar">
          <Link className="brand" href="/today"><span className="brand-mark">A</span><span>Ahara</span></Link>
        </header>
        <main className="page-main">
          {pathname === "/today" && <InstallPrompt variant="card" />}
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
      <nav className="mobile-bottom-nav" aria-label="Primary navigation">
        {primary.map((item) => (
          <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} className={matches(pathname, item.match) ? "mobile-nav-link active" : "mobile-nav-link"}>
            <span aria-hidden="true">{item.icon}</span><span>{item.label}</span>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

src/components/RouteStates.tsx (stops the sign-in flash)

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";

export function SignInRequired({ detail = "Sign in to view and save your private Ahara data." }: { detail?: string }) {
  const session = useSession();
  // Signed-in users briefly reach here while a page loads its own data: show a skeleton, never a sign-in card.
  if (session) return <div className="skeleton-stack" aria-hidden="true"><div /><div /></div>;
  return (
    <section className="state-card">
      <p className="state-icon" aria-hidden="true">◎</p>
      <h2>Keep your data private</h2>
      <p>{detail}</p>
      <Link className="button primary" href="/login">Sign in with email</Link>
    </section>
  );
}

export function CapabilityState({ title, detail, href, action }: { title: string; detail: string; href?: string; action?: string }) {
  return (
    <section className="state-card">
      <p className="state-icon" aria-hidden="true">○</p>
      <h2>{title}</h2>
      <p>{detail}</p>
      {href && action && <Link className="button secondary" href={href}>{action}</Link>}
    </section>
  );
}

src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx

src/app/(auth)/login/page.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const db = useMemo(supabase, []);
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [code, setCode] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"idle" | "pending" | "sent" | "verifying" | "error">("idle");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function sendLink(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(email)) {
      setStatus("error");
      setMessage("Enter a valid email address.");
      return;
    }
    setStatus("pending");
    const { error } = await db.auth.signInWithOtp({ email, options: { emailRedirectTo: `${location.origin}/auth/callback` } });
    if (error) {
      setStatus("error");
      setMessage(error.message);
    } else {
      setStatus("sent");
      setMessage("Check your email. Tap the sign-in link, or enter the code from the email below.");
    }
  }

  async function verifyCode(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!/^\d{6,10}$/.test(code.trim())) {
      setStatus("error");
      setMessage("Enter the numeric code from your email.");
      return;
    }
    setStatus("verifying");
    const { error } = await db.auth.verifyOtp({ email, token: code.trim(), type: "email" });
    if (error) {
      setStatus("error");
      setMessage("That code did not work. It may have expired; request a new one.");
      return;
    }
    router.replace("/today");
    router.refresh();
  }

  const emailSent = status === "sent" || status === "verifying" || (status === "error" && message.includes("code"));

  return (
    <section className="auth-card">
      <Link className="brand" href="/"><span className="brand-mark">A</span>Ahara</Link>
      <p className="eyebrow">Welcome</p>
      <h1>Sign in to your private diary</h1>
      <p className="muted">We’ll email you a one-time link. No password is needed.</p>
      <form onSubmit={sendLink}>
        <label>Email address
          <input type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" />
        </label>
        <button className="button primary full-width" disabled={status === "pending" || status === "verifying"}>
          {status === "pending" ? "Sending…" : emailSent ? "Send a new link" : "Email me a sign-in link"}
        </button>
      </form>
      {emailSent && (
        <form onSubmit={verifyCode}>
          <label>Code from the email
            <input inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="one-time-code" value={code} onChange={(event) => setCode(event.target.value)} placeholder="123456" />
          </label>
          <button className="button secondary full-width" disabled={status === "verifying"}>{status === "verifying" ? "Checking…" : "Sign in with code"}</button>
        </form>
      )}
      {status === "sent" && <p className="success-note" role="status">{message}</p>}
      {status === "error" && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{message}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}

src/components/goals/GoalSetupForm.tsx (targets are optional now)

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";

type Goal = { kind: string; value: number };

function parse(text: string): number | null | "invalid" {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return null;
  const value = Number(trimmed);
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 && value <= 10000 ? value : "invalid";
}

export function GoalSetupForm({ compact = true }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  const [intake, setIntake] = useState("");
  const [activity, setActivity] = useState("");
  const [state, setState] = useState<"idle" | "saving" | "saved" | "error">("idle");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch<{ goals: Goal[] }>("/api/goals")
      .then(({ goals }) => {
        setIntake(String(goals.find((goal) => goal.kind === "intake_kcal")?.value ?? ""));
        setActivity(String(goals.find((goal) => goal.kind === "activity_kcal")?.value ?? ""));
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  async function save(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const intakeKcal = parse(intake);
    const activityKcal = parse(activity);
    if (intakeKcal === "invalid" || activityKcal === "invalid") {
      setState("error");
      setMessage("Enter a number between 1 and 10,000, or leave the field empty.");
      return;
    }
    if (intakeKcal === null && activityKcal === null) {
      setState("error");
      setMessage("Enter at least one target to save.");
      return;
    }
    setState("saving");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/goals", { method: "POST", json: { intakeKcal, activityKcal } });
      setState("saved");
      setMessage("Your targets were saved. Earlier days keep the target they had.");
    } catch (error) {
      setState("error");
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Your targets could not be saved.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className={compact ? "form-card goal-form compact" : "form-card goal-form"} onSubmit={save}>
      <div>
        <p className="eyebrow">Personal targets</p>
        <h2>Your daily targets</h2>
        <p className="muted">Both are optional. They are personal targets, not medical advice.</p>
      </div>
      <label>Daily food-energy target (kcal)
        <input value={intake} onChange={(event) => setIntake(event.target.value)} inputMode="numeric" placeholder="For example, 2000" />
      </label>
      <label>Daily activity-energy target (kcal)
        <input value={activity} onChange={(event) => setActivity(event.target.value)} inputMode="numeric" placeholder="For example, 300" />
      </label>
      <p className="goal-note">Ahara never subtracts activity from food or shows “net calories”.</p>
      {state === "error" && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{message}</p>}
      {state === "saved" && <p className="success-note" role="status">{message}</p>}
      <button className="button primary full-width" disabled={state === "saving"}>{state === "saving" ? "Saving…" : "Save targets"}</button>
    </form>
  );
}

src/app/(app)/settings/profile/page.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE, isValidTimeZone } from "@/lib/dates";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

const COMMON_ZONES = ["Asia/Kolkata", "Asia/Dubai", "Asia/Singapore", "Europe/London", "America/New_York", "America/Los_Angeles", "Australia/Sydney"];

export default function ProfileSettingsPage() {
  const session = useSession();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState(session?.displayName ?? "");
  const [timeZone, setTimeZone] = useState(session?.timeZone ?? DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"idle" | "saving" | "saved" | "error">("idle");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function save(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!session) return;
    const zone = timeZone.trim();
    if (name.trim().length > 60) {
      setState("error");
      setMessage("Keep your name to 60 characters or fewer.");
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidTimeZone(zone)) {
      setState("error");
      setMessage("That time zone was not recognised. Pick one from the list, such as Asia/Kolkata.");
      return;
    }
    setState("saving");
    const { error } = await supabase()
      .from("profiles")
      .upsert({ id: session.userId, display_name: name.trim() || null, timezone: zone }, { onConflict: "id" });
    if (error) {
      setState("error");
      setMessage(error.message);
      return;
    }
    setState("saved");
    setMessage("Profile saved.");
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Settings" title="Profile" description="Your time zone decides which day a new entry belongs to." />
      <form className="form-card" onSubmit={save}>
        <label>Email
          <input value={session?.email ?? ""} readOnly aria-readonly="true" />
        </label>
        <label>Display name
          <input value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} maxLength={60} placeholder="Optional" />
        </label>
        <label>Time zone
          <input list="time-zones" value={timeZone} onChange={(event) => setTimeZone(event.target.value)} />
          <datalist id="time-zones">{COMMON_ZONES.map((zone) => <option key={zone} value={zone} />)}</datalist>
        </label>
        <p className="muted">Changing it affects new entries only. Past entries keep the day they were saved on.</p>
        {state === "error" && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{message}</p>}
        {state === "saved" && <p className="success-note" role="status">{message}</p>}
        <button className="button primary" disabled={state === "saving"}>{state === "saving" ? "Saving…" : "Save profile"}</button>
      </form>
    </>
  );
}

src/app/(app)/settings/privacy/page.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { SignOutButton } from "@/components/app-shell/SignOutButton";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function PrivacySettingsPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function deleteAccount() {
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/account", { method: "DELETE" });
      await supabase().auth.signOut();
      router.replace("/");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Your account could not be deleted.");
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Settings" title="Privacy & data" description="Your personal data stays separate from the shared food catalogue." />
      <section className="form-card">
        <h2>Your data</h2>
        <p className="muted">Download everything Ahara holds about you as a JSON file.</p>
        <Link className="button secondary" href="/export">Export my data</Link>
      </section>
      <section className="form-card">
        <h2>Sign out</h2>
        <SignOutButton />
      </section>
      <section className="form-card">
        <h2>Delete account</h2>
        <p className="muted">This permanently deletes your account and all your entries. It cannot be undone. Type “delete” to confirm.</p>
        <label>Confirmation
          <input value={confirm} onChange={(event) => setConfirm(event.target.value)} autoComplete="off" />
        </label>
        {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
        <button className="button secondary" disabled={busy || confirm.trim().toLowerCase() !== "delete"} onClick={() => void deleteAccount()}>
          {busy ? "Deleting…" : "Delete my account"}
        </button>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

P2: numbers and dates
SQL: run in the Supabase SQL editor, and save as supabase/migrations/202611080018_copy_day.sql
sql
-- Copies one day's entries to another empty day in a single transaction.
create or replace function public.copy_day(source_date date, target_date date)
returns integer
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
  uid uuid := auth.uid();
  tz_name text;
  copied integer := 0;
  src record;
  new_id uuid;
begin
  if uid is null then
    raise exception 'Not signed in' using errcode = '28000';
  end if;
  if source_date = target_date then
    raise exception 'Choose a different day to copy from.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from food_logs where owner_id = uid and local_date = target_date) then
    raise exception 'That day already has entries.' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  select timezone into tz_name from profiles where id = uid;
  tz_name := coalesce(tz_name, 'Asia/Kolkata');

  for src in
    select * from food_logs where owner_id = uid and local_date = source_date order by consumed_at
  loop
    new_id := gen_random_uuid();
    insert into food_logs (id, owner_id, consumed_at, local_date, meal_type, food_id, quantity, unit,
                           name_snapshot, serving_name_snapshot, source_kind, notes)
    values (new_id, uid,
            (target_date + (src.consumed_at at time zone tz_name)::time) at time zone tz_name,
            target_date, src.meal_type, src.food_id, src.quantity, src.unit,
            src.name_snapshot, src.serving_name_snapshot, src.source_kind, src.notes);

    insert into food_log_nutrients (log_id, base_snapshot, final_totals, completeness)
      select new_id, base_snapshot, final_totals, completeness from food_log_nutrients where log_id = src.id;

    insert into food_log_adjustments (log_id, kind, accuracy_class, label, nutrient_changes, assumption, note,
                                      position, basis, scales_with_quantity)
      select new_id, kind, accuracy_class, label, nutrient_changes, assumption, note,
             position, basis, scales_with_quantity
      from food_log_adjustments where log_id = src.id;

    insert into food_log_tags (log_id, tag, source)
      select new_id, tag, source from food_log_tags where log_id = src.id;

    copied := copied + 1;
  end loop;
  return copied;
end
$$;
New: src/lib/totals.ts
ts
export type KcalTotal = { total: number | null; partial: boolean; knownCount: number; unknownCount: number };

/** Sums calories without ever treating unknown as zero. */
export function sumKcal(values: ReadonlyArray<number | null | undefined>): KcalTotal {
  let total = 0;
  let knownCount = 0;
  for (const value of values) {
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
      total += value;
      knownCount += 1;
    }
  }
  const unknownCount = values.length - knownCount;
  return {
    total: values.length === 0 ? 0 : knownCount === 0 ? null : total,
    partial: knownCount > 0 && unknownCount > 0,
    knownCount,
    unknownCount,
  };
}

/** Reads final calories from a food_log_nutrients embed, which may arrive as an object or a one-item array. */
export function finalKcal(embed: unknown): number | null {
  const row = (Array.isArray(embed) ? embed[0] : embed) as { final_totals?: { kcal?: unknown } } | null | undefined;
  const kcal = row?.final_totals?.kcal;
  return typeof kcal === "number" && Number.isFinite(kcal) ? kcal : null;
}
New: src/lib/totals.test.ts
ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { finalKcal, sumKcal } from "./totals";

describe("sumKcal", () => {
  it("is zero for an empty day, not unknown", () => {
    expect(sumKcal([])).toMatchObject({ total: 0, partial: false });
  });
  it("never counts unknown as zero", () => {
    expect(sumKcal([null, null])).toMatchObject({ total: null, partial: false, unknownCount: 2 });
  });
  it("marks a total partial when some entries are unknown", () => {
    expect(sumKcal([138, null, 275])).toMatchObject({ total: 413, partial: true, unknownCount: 1 });
  });
  it("keeps a real zero", () => {
    expect(sumKcal([0, 100])).toMatchObject({ total: 100, partial: false });
  });
});

describe("finalKcal", () => {
  it("reads both embed shapes and returns null for unknown", () => {
    expect(finalKcal({ final_totals: { kcal: 120 } })).toBe(120);
    expect(finalKcal([{ final_totals: { kcal: 120 } }])).toBe(120);
    expect(finalKcal({ final_totals: { kcal: null } })).toBeNull();
    expect(finalKcal(undefined)).toBeNull();
  });
});
New: src/lib/meals.ts
ts
export const MEALS = ["breakfast", "lunch", "snack", "dinner"] as const;
export type Meal = (typeof MEALS)[number];

export function mealForHour(hour: number): Meal {
  if (hour < 11) return "breakfast";
  if (hour < 15) return "lunch";
  if (hour < 19) return "snack";
  return "dinner";
}
Replace: src/app/api/logs/copy-yesterday/route.ts
ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const { fromDate, toDate } = (await request.json()) as { fromDate?: string; toDate?: string };
  if (!fromDate || !toDate || !DATE.test(fromDate) || !DATE.test(toDate)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Valid source and destination dates are required." }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const { data, error } = await auth.client.rpc("copy_day", { source_date: fromDate, target_date: toDate });
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  return NextResponse.json({ copied: data as number }, { status: 201 });
}
Replace: src/app/api/insights/route.ts
ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE, localDate, shiftDate } from "@/lib/dates";
import { finalKcal } from "@/lib/totals";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const DAYS = { week: 7, month: 30, year: 365 } as const;
type Period = keyof typeof DAYS;
type LogRow = { local_date: string; name_snapshot: string; food_log_nutrients: unknown };

async function fetchLogs(client: SupabaseClient, start: string, end: string): Promise<LogRow[]> {
  const rows: LogRow[] = [];
  const PAGE = 1000; // the API returns at most 1000 rows per request
  for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE) {
    const { data, error } = await client
      .from("food_logs")
      .select("local_date,name_snapshot,food_log_nutrients(final_totals)")
      .gte("local_date", start)
      .lte("local_date", end)
      .order("consumed_at")
      .range(from, from + PAGE - 1);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    const page = (data ?? []) as unknown as LogRow[];
    rows.push(...page);
    if (page.length < PAGE) break;
  }
  return rows;
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const period = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("period") ?? "week") as Period;
  if (!(period in DAYS)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid period." }, { status: 400 });

  const { data: profile } = await auth.client.from("profiles").select("timezone").eq("id", auth.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const timeZone = (profile?.timezone as string | undefined) ?? DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE;
  const end = localDate(timeZone);
  const start = shiftDate(end, -(DAYS[period] - 1));

  let logs: LogRow[];
  try {
    logs = await fetchLogs(auth.client, start, end);
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load logs." }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const days = new Map<string, { kcal: number; known: number; unknown: number }>();
  const foods = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const log of logs) {
    const day = days.get(log.local_date) ?? { kcal: 0, known: 0, unknown: 0 };
    const kcal = finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients);
    if (kcal === null) {
      day.unknown += 1;
    } else {
      day.known += 1;
      day.kcal += kcal;
      foods.set(log.name_snapshot, (foods.get(log.name_snapshot) ?? 0) + kcal);
    }
    days.set(log.local_date, day);
  }

  const daily = [...days.entries()]
    .filter(([, day]) => day.known > 0)
    .map(([date, day]) => ({ date, kcal: day.kcal, partial: day.unknown > 0 }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));

  return NextResponse.json({
    period,
    start,
    end,
    loggedDays: days.size,
    knownDays: daily.length,
    partialDays: daily.filter((day) => day.partial).length,
    unknownOnlyDays: days.size - daily.length,
    daily,
    topFoods: [...foods.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 5).map(([name, kcal]) => ({ name, kcal })),
    note: "Days you didn’t log are left out, never counted as zero. Entries with unknown calories are left out of the totals.",
  });
}
Replace: src/app/(app)/insights/page.tsx
tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";

type Insight = {
  start: string;
  end: string;
  loggedDays: number;
  knownDays: number;
  partialDays: number;
  unknownOnlyDays: number;
  daily: { date: string; kcal: number; partial: boolean }[];
  topFoods: { name: string; kcal: number }[];
  note: string;
};
type Period = "week" | "month" | "year";
const RANGES: [Period, string][] = [["week", "7 days"], ["month", "30 days"], ["year", "Year"]];
const MAX_BARS = 31;

export default function InsightsPage() {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<Period>("week");
  const [data, setData] = useState<Insight | null>(null);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ready" | "error">("loading");

  const load = useCallback(
    async (signal?: AbortSignal) => {
      setState("loading");
      try {
        setData(await apiFetch<Insight>(`/api/insights?period=${period}`, { signal }));
        setState("ready");
      } catch {
        if (!signal?.aborted) setState("error");
      }
    },
    [period],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    void load(controller.signal);
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [load]);

  const bars = data ? data.daily.slice(-MAX_BARS) : [];
  const max = Math.max(...bars.map((day) => day.kcal), 1);
  const average = data && data.daily.length ? data.daily.reduce((sum, day) => sum + day.kcal, 0) / data.daily.length : null;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Patterns, not judgment" title="Insights" description="Built only from the days you actually logged." />
      <section className="segmented" aria-label="Insight date range">
        {RANGES.map(([value, label]) => (
          <button className={period === value ? "selected" : ""} key={value} onClick={() => setPeriod(value)}>{label}</button>
        ))}
      </section>

      {state === "loading" ? (
        <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /></div>
      ) : state === "error" ? (
        <section className="state-card">
          <h2>Your insights didn’t load</h2>
          <p>Your saved data is safe. Check your connection and try again.</p>
          <button className="button primary" onClick={() => void load()}>Try again</button>
        </section>
      ) : !data || data.loggedDays === 0 ? (
        <section className="state-card">
          <h2>Not enough data yet</h2>
          <p>Log food on a few days to see your personal patterns here.</p>
        </section>
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="insight-summary">
            <div><span>Days logged</span><strong>{data.loggedDays}</strong></div>
            <div><span>Average per logged day</span><strong>{average === null ? "Unknown" : `${Math.round(average)} kcal`}</strong></div>
            <div><span>Range</span><strong>{data.start} to {data.end}</strong></div>
          </section>
          {(data.partialDays > 0 || data.unknownOnlyDays > 0) && (
            <p className="muted" role="note">
              {data.partialDays > 0 && `${data.partialDays} day(s) include entries with unknown calories, so their real totals may be higher. `}
              {data.unknownOnlyDays > 0 && `${data.unknownOnlyDays} day(s) have only unknown-calorie entries and are left out.`}
            </p>
          )}
          <section className="chart-card">
            <p className="eyebrow">Nutrition</p>
            <h2>Daily calories</h2>
            <div className="bar-chart" role="img" aria-label={`Calories on ${bars.length} logged days.`}>
              {bars.map((day) => (
                <div className="bar-column" key={day.date}>
                  <span>{day.partial ? "≥" : ""}{Math.round(day.kcal)}</span>
                  <div className="bar-track"><div style={{ height: `${Math.max(4, (day.kcal / max) * 100)}%` }} /></div>
                  <small>{day.date.slice(5)}</small>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            {data.daily.length > MAX_BARS && <p className="muted">Showing the latest {MAX_BARS} logged days. A fuller long-range view comes in P9.</p>}
            <p className="muted">{data.note}</p>
          </section>
          <section className="chart-card">
            <p className="eyebrow">Food habits</p>
            <h2>Most logged foods</h2>
            {data.topFoods.map((food) => (
              <div className="food-row" key={food.name}><span>{food.name}</span><strong>{Math.round(food.kcal)} kcal</strong></div>
            ))}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
Replace: src/components/dashboard/TodayDashboard.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { CapabilityState } from "@/components/RouteStates";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localDate, shiftDate } from "@/lib/dates";
import { MEALS } from "@/lib/meals";
import { intakeStatus } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { finalKcal, sumKcal } from "@/lib/totals";

type Log = { id: string; name_snapshot: string; quantity: number; unit: string; meal_type: string; food_log_nutrients: unknown };

function quantityText(log: Log) {
  const amount = Number(log.quantity);
  return log.unit === "g" ? `${amount} g` : `${amount} ${log.unit}`;
}

export function TodayDashboard() {
  const session = useSession();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";
  const db = useMemo(supabase, []);
  const today = localDate(timeZone);
  const [date, setDate] = useState(today);
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<Log[]>([]);
  const [intakeGoal, setIntakeGoal] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [activityGoal, setActivityGoal] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [previousCount, setPreviousCount] = useState(0);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"loading" | "ready" | "error">("loading");
  const [copying, setCopying] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setStatus("loading");
    const [goalsResult, logsResult, previousResult] = await Promise.all([
      db.from("goal_versions").select("kind,value").lte("starts_at", date).or(`ends_at.is.null,ends_at.gt.${date}`),
      db.from("food_logs").select("id,name_snapshot,quantity,unit,meal_type,food_log_nutrients(final_totals)").eq("local_date", date).order("consumed_at"),
      db.from("food_logs").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("local_date", shiftDate(date, -1)),
    ]);
    if (goalsResult.error || logsResult.error) {
      setStatus("error");
      return;
    }
    const goals = (goalsResult.data ?? []) as { kind: string; value: number }[];
    const find = (kind: string) => {
      const row = goals.find((goal) => goal.kind === kind);
      return row ? Number(row.value) : null;
    };
    setIntakeGoal(find("intake_kcal"));
    setActivityGoal(find("activity_kcal"));
    setLogs((logsResult.data ?? []) as unknown as Log[]);
    setPreviousCount(previousResult.count ?? 0);
    setStatus("ready");
  }, [db, date]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  async function copyPrevious() {
    setCopying(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/logs/copy-yesterday", { method: "POST", json: { fromDate: shiftDate(date, -1), toDate: date } });
      await load();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Could not copy those entries.");
    } finally {
      setCopying(false);
    }
  }

  const total = sumKcal(logs.map((log) => finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients)));
  const progress = intakeGoal && total.total !== null ? Math.min(100, Math.round((total.total / intakeGoal) * 100)) : 0;

  function statusLine(): string {
    if (intakeGoal === null) return "No daily food target set.";
    if (total.total === null) return "Calories for these entries are unknown.";
    const result = intakeStatus(total.total, intakeGoal);
    if (total.partial) {
      return result.state === "above"
        ? `At least ${Math.round(result.difference!)} kcal above your target.`
        : "Some entries have unknown calories, so the real total may be higher.";
    }
    if (result.state === "at") return "At your target.";
    if (result.state === "below") return `${Math.round(-result.difference!)} kcal remaining.`;
    return `${Math.round(result.difference!)} kcal above your target.`;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Daily dashboard" title={date === today ? "Today" : date} description="Review your day and choose the next small action." action={<Link className="button primary" href="/add">Add food</Link>} />
      <div className="date-row">
        <button onClick={() => setDate(shiftDate(date, -1))} aria-label="Previous day">‹</button>
        <input id="today-date" type="date" value={date} max={today} aria-label="Date" onChange={(event) => event.target.value && setDate(event.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={() => setDate(shiftDate(date, 1))} disabled={date >= today} aria-label="Next day">›</button>
      </div>

      {status === "loading" ? (
        <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /><div /></div>
      ) : status === "error" ? (
        <CapabilityState title="Today could not load" detail="Your diary was not changed. Check your connection and try again." action="Try again" href="/today" />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="summary-card">
            <div>
              <p className="eyebrow">Intake</p>
              <p className="big-number">
                {total.total === null ? "Unknown" : `${total.partial ? "≥ " : ""}${Math.round(total.total)}`} <span>{total.total === null ? "" : "kcal"}</span>
              </p>
              <p>{statusLine()}</p>
              {total.partial && <p className="muted">{total.unknownCount} entr{total.unknownCount === 1 ? "y has" : "ies have"} unknown calories.</p>}
            </div>
            <div className="progress-wrap">
              <div className="progress-ring" style={{ "--progress": `${progress}%` } as React.CSSProperties}>
                <span>{intakeGoal ? `${progress}%` : "—"}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="muted">{intakeGoal ? `${intakeGoal} kcal target` : "No target"}</span>
            </div>
          </section>

          {intakeGoal === null && activityGoal === null ? (
            <section className="state-card">
              <h2>Set your daily targets</h2>
              <p>Add a food target and an activity target. Both are optional and personal.</p>
              <Link className="button secondary" href="/goals">Set targets</Link>
            </section>
          ) : (
            activityGoal !== null && <p className="muted">Activity target: {activityGoal} kcal. Activity tracking arrives in P8, and it never changes your food target.</p>
          )}

          {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}

          <section className="content-section">
            <div className="section-heading"><h2>Meals</h2><Link href="/log">View diary</Link></div>
            {logs.length === 0 ? (
              <>
                <CapabilityState title="Nothing logged yet" detail="Add your first food when you are ready." href="/add" action="Add food" />
                {previousCount > 0 && (
                  <button className="button secondary" disabled={copying} onClick={() => void copyPrevious()}>
                    {copying ? "Copying…" : `Copy ${previousCount} entr${previousCount === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} from ${shiftDate(date, -1)}`}
                  </button>
                )}
              </>
            ) : (
              MEALS.map((meal) => {
                const entries = logs.filter((log) => log.meal_type === meal);
                if (!entries.length) return null;
                return (
                  <section className="meal-card" key={meal}>
                    <h3>{meal}</h3>
                    {entries.map((log) => {
                      const kcal = finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients);
                      return (
                        <div className="food-row" key={log.id}>
                          <span><b>{log.name_snapshot}</b><small>{quantityText(log)}</small></span>
                          <strong>{kcal === null ? "Calories unknown" : `${Math.round(kcal)} kcal`}</strong>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </section>
                );
              })
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

sql
-- Ranked food search: exact name, then name starts with, then a word starts with, then contains.
create or replace function public.search_foods(q text, max_results integer default 30)
returns table (
  found_food_id uuid,
  match_rank integer,
  matched_alias text,
  match_quality public.alias_quality,
  alias_verified boolean
)
language sql
stable
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
  with cleaned as (
    select lower(trim(q)) as term,
           replace(replace(replace(lower(trim(q)), '\', '\\'), '%', '\%'), '_', '\_') as pattern
  ),
  hits as (
    select a.food_id,
           a.alias,
           a.match_quality as quality,
           a.verified,
           f.owner_id,
           case
             when lower(a.alias) = c.term then 1
             when lower(a.alias) like c.pattern || '%' then 2
             when lower(a.alias) like '% ' || c.pattern || '%' then 3
             else 4
           end as rank_value
    from cleaned c
    join public.food_aliases a on length(c.term) >= 2 and lower(a.alias) like '%' || c.pattern || '%'
    join public.foods f on f.id = a.food_id
  ),
  best as (
    select distinct on (food_id) *
    from hits
    order by food_id, rank_value, verified desc, length(alias)
  )
  select b.food_id, b.rank_value, b.alias, b.quality, b.verified
  from best b
  order by b.rank_value, (b.owner_id is null), length(b.alias), b.alias
  limit least(greatest(max_results, 1), 50);
$$;

ts
/** Current wall-clock time (HH:MM, 24-hour) in the given time zone. */
export function localTime(timeZone: string = DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE, instant: Date = new Date()): string {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone, hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", hourCycle: "h23" }).formatToParts(instant);
  const get = (type: string) => parts.find((part) => part.type === type)?.value ?? "00";
  return `${get("hour")}:${get("minute")}`;
}

function zoneOffsetMs(timeZone: string, instant: Date): number {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone, hourCycle: "h23", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
  }).formatToParts(instant);
  const get = (type: string) => Number(parts.find((part) => part.type === type)?.value);
  const asUtc = Date.UTC(get("year"), get("month") - 1, get("day"), get("hour"), get("minute"), get("second"));
  return asUtc - Math.floor(instant.getTime() / 1000) * 1000;
}

/** Converts a local date (YYYY-MM-DD) and time (HH:MM) in a time zone into the exact instant. */
export function zonedDateTimeToInstant(date: string, time: string, timeZone: string): Date {
  const [year, month, day] = date.split("-").map(Number);
  const [hour, minute] = time.split(":").map(Number);
  const guess = Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, hour, minute);
  const firstOffset = zoneOffsetMs(timeZone, new Date(guess));
  let result = guess - firstOffset;
  const secondOffset = zoneOffsetMs(timeZone, new Date(result));
  if (secondOffset !== firstOffset) result = guess - secondOffset; // daylight-saving edge
  return new Date(result);
}
Replace: src/lib/dates.test.ts
ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isValidTimeZone, localDate, localTime, shiftDate, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "./dates";

describe("dates", () => {
  it("uses the profile time zone, not the server's, around midnight", () => {
    expect(localDate("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:20:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-08"); // 11:50 pm IST
    expect(localDate("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:40:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-09"); // 12:10 am IST
    expect(localDate("UTC", new Date("2026-11-08T18:40:00Z"))).toBe("2026-11-08");
  });
  it("shifts calendar dates across month and leap boundaries", () => {
    expect(shiftDate("2026-03-01", -1)).toBe("2026-02-28");
    expect(shiftDate("2028-03-01", -1)).toBe("2028-02-29");
    expect(shiftDate("2026-12-31", 1)).toBe("2027-01-01");
  });
  it("validates time zone names", () => {
    expect(isValidTimeZone("Asia/Kolkata")).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidTimeZone("Mars/Base")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("formats the local time", () => {
    expect(localTime("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:20:00Z"))).toBe("23:50");
    expect(localTime("Asia/Kolkata", new Date("2026-11-08T18:40:00Z"))).toBe("00:10");
  });
  it("turns a local date and time into the right instant", () => {
    expect(zonedDateTimeToInstant("2026-11-08", "23:50", "Asia/Kolkata").toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-08T18:20:00.000Z");
    expect(zonedDateTimeToInstant("2026-11-09", "00:10", "Asia/Kolkata").toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-08T18:40:00.000Z");
    expect(zonedDateTimeToInstant("2026-07-01", "12:00", "Europe/London").toISOString()).toBe("2026-07-01T11:00:00.000Z");
  });
});

New: src/lib/units.ts
ts
import { resolveQuantity, type Nutrients, type QuantityResult } from "@/lib/nutrition";

export type PortionUnit = "serving" | "g";
export type NutrientRow = {
  basis: "per_serving" | "per_100g";
  serving_id?: string | null;
  kcal: number | null;
  protein_g: number | null;
  carbs_g: number | null;
  fat_g: number | null;
  sat_fat_g: number | null;
  fibre_g: number | null;
  free_sugars_g: number | null;
  total_sugars_g: number | null;
  sodium_mg: number | null;
};
export type ServingRow = { id: string; name: string; unit_class: string; grams: number | null };
export type FoodSummary = {
  id: string;
  canonical_name: string;
  serving_mode: "count" | "household" | "grams_only";
  owner_id?: string | null;
  food_servings?: ServingRow[];
  food_nutrients?: NutrientRow[];
  matched_alias?: string;
  match_quality?: "exact" | "approximate";
};

export function rowToNutrients(row: NutrientRow): Nutrients {
  return {
    kcal: row.kcal, proteinG: row.protein_g, carbsG: row.carbs_g, fatG: row.fat_g, satFatG: row.sat_fat_g,
    fibreG: row.fibre_g, freeSugarsG: row.free_sugars_g, totalSugarsG: row.total_sugars_g, addedSugarsG: null, sodiumMg: row.sodium_mg,
  };
}

const perServingRow = (food: FoodSummary) => food.food_nutrients?.find((row) => row.basis === "per_serving");
const per100gRow = (food: FoodSummary) => food.food_nutrients?.find((row) => row.basis === "per_100g");

/** Counted foods never allow grams; other foods offer whatever their data supports. */
export function availableUnits(food: FoodSummary): PortionUnit[] {
  const hasServing = Boolean(perServingRow(food));
  const hasGrams = Boolean(per100gRow(food));
  if (food.serving_mode === "count") return hasServing ? ["serving"] : [];
  if (food.serving_mode === "household") return [...(hasServing ? (["serving"] as const) : []), ...(hasGrams ? (["g"] as const) : [])];
  return hasGrams ? ["g"] : [];
}

export function servingNameFor(food: FoodSummary): string {
  const row = perServingRow(food);
  const match = row?.serving_id ? food.food_servings?.find((serving) => serving.id === row.serving_id) : undefined;
  return match?.name ?? food.food_servings?.[0]?.name ?? (food.serving_mode === "count" ? "piece" : "serving");
}

export const quantityChips = (unit: PortionUnit): number[] => (unit === "g" ? [50, 100, 150, 200, 250] : [0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 3]);
export const quantityStep = (unit: PortionUnit) => (unit === "g" ? 10 : 0.25);
export const minQuantity = (unit: PortionUnit) => (unit === "g" ? 1 : 0.25);

export function parseQuantity(text: string): number | null {
  const value = Number(text.trim().replace(",", "."));
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 && value <= 10000 ? value : null;
}

export function formatQuantity(value: number): string {
  const whole = Math.floor(value + 1e-9);
  const fraction = Math.round((value - whole) * 100) / 100;
  if (fraction === 0) return String(whole);
  const glyph = ({ 0.25: "¼", 0.5: "½", 0.75: "¾" } as Record<number, string>)[fraction];
  if (glyph) return whole === 0 ? glyph : `${whole}${glyph}`;
  return String(Math.round(value * 100) / 100);
}

export function previewPortion(food: FoodSummary, unit: PortionUnit, quantity: number): QuantityResult {
  const perServing = perServingRow(food);
  const per100g = per100gRow(food);
  return resolveQuantity(
    { unitClass: food.serving_mode, perServing: perServing ? rowToNutrients(perServing) : undefined, per100g: per100g ? rowToNutrients(per100g) : undefined },
    quantity,
    unit,
  );
}

/** One-line description for search results, such as "piece · 138 kcal". */
export function defaultKcalLabel(food: FoodSummary): string {
  const serving = perServingRow(food);
  if (serving) return serving.kcal === null ? `${servingNameFor(food)} · calories unavailable` : `${servingNameFor(food)} · ${Math.round(serving.kcal)} kcal`;
  const grams = per100gRow(food);
  if (grams) return grams.kcal === null ? "per 100 g · calories unavailable" : `per 100 g · ${Math.round(grams.kcal)} kcal`;
  return "Nutrition unavailable";
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { availableUnits, formatQuantity, parseQuantity, previewPortion, type FoodSummary } from "./units";

// Fixture numbers only exercise the rules; they are not nutrition data.
const row = (basis: "per_serving" | "per_100g", kcal: number | null) => ({
  basis, serving_id: basis === "per_serving" ? "s1" : null, kcal,
  protein_g: null, carbs_g: null, fat_g: null, sat_fat_g: null, fibre_g: null, free_sugars_g: null, total_sugars_g: null, sodium_mg: null,
});
const idli: FoodSummary = { id: "1", canonical_name: "Idli", serving_mode: "count", food_servings: [{ id: "s1", name: "piece", unit_class: "count", grams: null }], food_nutrients: [row("per_serving", 100), row("per_100g", 200)] };
const rice: FoodSummary = { id: "2", canonical_name: "Rice", serving_mode: "household", food_nutrients: [row("per_serving", 100), row("per_100g", 200)] };
const curdOnlyGrams: FoodSummary = { id: "3", canonical_name: "Curd", serving_mode: "household", food_nutrients: [row("per_100g", 60)] };

describe("units", () => {
  it("never offers grams for counted foods", () => {
    expect(availableUnits(idli)).toEqual(["serving"]);
  });
  it("offers what the data supports for household foods", () => {
    expect(availableUnits(rice)).toEqual(["serving", "g"]);
    expect(availableUnits(curdOnlyGrams)).toEqual(["g"]);
  });
  it("scales fractions and keeps unknown nutrients unknown", () => {
    const result = previewPortion(idli, "serving", 1.5);
    expect(result.ok && result.nutrients.kcal).toBe(150);
    expect(result.ok && result.nutrients.proteinG).toBeNull();
  });
  it("blocks grams for a counted food", () => {
    expect(previewPortion(idli, "g", 100)).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "grams_not_allowed" });
  });
  it("parses and formats quantities", () => {
    expect(parseQuantity("1,5")).toBe(1.5);
    expect(parseQuantity("0")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseQuantity("abc")).toBeNull();
    expect(formatQuantity(1.5)).toBe("1½");
    expect(formatQuantity(0.25)).toBe("¼");
    expect(formatQuantity(2)).toBe("2");
  });
});
New: src/lib/server/logging.ts
ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { nutrientKeys, resolveQuantity, type Nutrients, type QuantityError, type QuantityResult, type UnitClass } from "@/lib/nutrition";

type NutrientRow = {
  basis: "per_serving" | "per_100g";
  serving_id: string | null;
  kcal: number | null; protein_g: number | null; carbs_g: number | null; fat_g: number | null; sat_fat_g: number | null;
  fibre_g: number | null; free_sugars_g: number | null; total_sugars_g: number | null; sodium_mg: number | null;
  extra?: Nutrients["extra"];
};
export type LoggableFood = {
  id: string;
  canonical_name: string;
  serving_mode: UnitClass;
  source_kind: string;
  food_servings: { id: string; name: string }[];
  food_nutrients: NutrientRow[];
};

const SELECT =
  "id,canonical_name,serving_mode,source_kind,food_servings(id,name)," +
  "food_nutrients(basis,serving_id,kcal,protein_g,carbs_g,fat_g,sat_fat_g,fibre_g,free_sugars_g,total_sugars_g,sodium_mg,extra)";

export async function loadLoggableFood(client: SupabaseClient, foodId: string): Promise<LoggableFood | null> {
  const { data, error } = await client.from("foods").select(SELECT).eq("id", foodId).single();
  return error || !data ? null : (data as unknown as LoggableFood);
}

function toNutrients(row: NutrientRow): Nutrients {
  return {
    kcal: row.kcal, proteinG: row.protein_g, carbsG: row.carbs_g, fatG: row.fat_g, satFatG: row.sat_fat_g,
    fibreG: row.fibre_g, freeSugarsG: row.free_sugars_g, totalSugarsG: row.total_sugars_g, addedSugarsG: null,
    sodiumMg: row.sodium_mg, extra: row.extra,
  };
}

export function resolveEntry(food: LoggableFood, quantity: number, unit: "serving" | "g"): { result: QuantityResult; servingName: string | null } {
  const perServing = food.food_nutrients.find((row) => row.basis === "per_serving");
  const per100g = food.food_nutrients.find((row) => row.basis === "per_100g");
  const result = resolveQuantity(
    { unitClass: food.serving_mode, perServing: perServing ? toNutrients(perServing) : undefined, per100g: per100g ? toNutrients(per100g) : undefined },
    quantity,
    unit,
  );
  const serving = perServing ? food.food_servings.find((item) => item.id === perServing.serving_id) ?? food.food_servings[0] : undefined;
  return { result, servingName: unit === "g" ? null : serving?.name ?? null };
}

export function completenessOf(values: Nutrients): Record<string, "complete" | "unknown"> {
  return Object.fromEntries(nutrientKeys.map((key) => [key, values[key] === null ? "unknown" : "complete"]));
}

export const QUANTITY_MESSAGES: Record<QuantityError, string> = {
  invalid_quantity: "Enter an amount above zero.",
  grams_not_allowed: "This food is counted in pieces, so grams aren’t available.",
  unknown_serving: "This food has no nutrition data for that unit.",
};

const MEALS = new Set(["breakfast", "lunch", "snack", "dinner"]);
export type EntryInput = { quantity: number; unit: "serving" | "g"; mealType: string; consumedAt: Date };

/** Validates the fields shared by creating and editing a log. Returns an error message or the clean values. */
export function parseEntry(body: Record<string, unknown>): EntryInput | string {
  const quantity = Number(body.quantity);
  if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0 || quantity > 10000) return "Enter an amount above zero.";
  if (body.unit !== "serving" && body.unit !== "g") return "Choose a unit.";
  if (typeof body.mealType !== "string" || !MEALS.has(body.mealType)) return "Choose a meal.";
  const consumedAt = new Date(String(body.consumedAt));
  if (Number.isNaN(consumedAt.getTime())) return "Choose a valid date and time.";
  if (consumedAt.getTime() > Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000) return "That time is in the future.";
  return { quantity, unit: body.unit, mealType: body.mealType, consumedAt };
}
Replace: src/app/api/logs/route.ts
ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { calculateAdjusted, type Adjustment, type NutrientKey } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { completenessOf, loadLoggableFood, parseEntry, QUANTITY_MESSAGES, resolveEntry } from "@/lib/server/logging";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const bad = (message: string, status = 400) => NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status });
async function readBody(request: Request): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null> {
  try {
    return (await request.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const body = await readBody(request);
  if (!body || typeof body.id !== "string" || typeof body.foodId !== "string") return bad("Invalid log input");
  const entry = parseEntry(body);
  if (typeof entry === "string") return bad(entry);

  const food = await loadLoggableFood(auth.client, body.foodId);
  if (!food) return bad("Food not found", 404);
  const { result, servingName } = resolveEntry(food, entry.quantity, entry.unit);
  if (!result.ok) return bad(QUANTITY_MESSAGES[result.error]);

  const { error: logError } = await auth.client.from("food_logs").insert({
    id: body.id,
    owner_id: auth.user.id,
    consumed_at: entry.consumedAt.toISOString(),
    local_date: "2000-01-01", // placeholder: a database trigger sets the real local date from the profile time zone
    meal_type: entry.mealType,
    food_id: food.id,
    quantity: entry.quantity,
    unit: entry.unit === "g" ? "g" : servingName ?? "serving",
    name_snapshot: food.canonical_name,
    serving_name_snapshot: servingName,
    source_kind: food.source_kind,
  });
  if (logError?.code === "23505") return NextResponse.json({ duplicate: true });
  if (logError) return bad(logError.message);

  const { error: nutrientError } = await auth.client.from("food_log_nutrients").insert({
    log_id: body.id,
    base_snapshot: result.nutrients,
    final_totals: result.nutrients,
    completeness: completenessOf(result.nutrients),
  });
  if (nutrientError) {
    await auth.client.from("food_logs").delete().eq("id", body.id); // never leave an entry without its snapshot
    return bad(nutrientError.message);
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ id: body.id }, { status: 201 });
}

type AdjustmentRow = {
  kind: Adjustment["kind"];
  accuracy_class: "measured" | "estimate" | "manual";
  label: string;
  nutrient_changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>>;
  assumption: string | null;
  basis: "per_entry" | "per_unit";
  scales_with_quantity: boolean;
};

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const body = await readBody(request);
  if (!body || typeof body.id !== "string") return bad("Invalid log input");
  const entry = parseEntry(body);
  if (typeof entry === "string") return bad(entry);

  const { data: existing } = await auth.client.from("food_logs").select("food_id").eq("id", body.id).single();
  if (!existing?.food_id) return bad("Log not found, or its food was removed.", 404);
  const food = await loadLoggableFood(auth.client, existing.food_id as string);
  if (!food) return bad("Food is no longer available", 404);
  const { result, servingName } = resolveEntry(food, entry.quantity, entry.unit);
  if (!result.ok) return bad(QUANTITY_MESSAGES[result.error]);

  // Re-apply the entry's saved adjustments to the new amount so the numbers stay consistent.
  const { data: adjustmentRows } = await auth.client
    .from("food_log_adjustments")
    .select("kind,accuracy_class,label,nutrient_changes,assumption,basis,scales_with_quantity")
    .eq("log_id", body.id)
    .order("position");
  const adjustments: Adjustment[] = ((adjustmentRows ?? []) as AdjustmentRow[]).map((row) => ({
    kind: row.kind,
    label: row.label,
    changes: row.nutrient_changes,
    basis: row.basis,
    scalesWithQuantity: row.scales_with_quantity,
    accuracyClass: row.accuracy_class,
    assumption: row.assumption ?? undefined,
  }));
  const adjusted = calculateAdjusted(result.nutrients, entry.quantity, adjustments);

  const { error: updateError } = await auth.client
    .from("food_logs")
    .update({
      consumed_at: entry.consumedAt.toISOString(),
      meal_type: entry.mealType,
      quantity: entry.quantity,
      unit: entry.unit === "g" ? "g" : servingName ?? "serving",
      serving_name_snapshot: servingName,
    })
    .eq("id", body.id);
  if (updateError) return bad(updateError.message);

  const { data: snapshot } = await auth.client.from("food_log_nutrients").select("snapshot_version").eq("log_id", body.id).single();
  const { error: snapshotError } = await auth.client
    .from("food_log_nutrients")
    .update({
      base_snapshot: result.nutrients,
      final_totals: adjusted.totals,
      completeness: { ...completenessOf(adjusted.totals), ...adjusted.completeness },
      snapshot_version: ((snapshot?.snapshot_version as number | undefined) ?? 0) + 1,
    })
    .eq("log_id", body.id);
  if (snapshotError) return bad(snapshotError.message);
  return NextResponse.json({ id: body.id });
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const id = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("id");
  if (!id) return bad("A log id is required.");
  const { error } = await auth.client.from("food_logs").delete().eq("id", id).eq("owner_id", auth.user.id);
  return error ? bad(error.message) : new NextResponse(null, { status: 204 });
}
Replace: src/app/api/search/route.ts
ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

type Hit = { found_food_id: string; matched_alias: string; match_quality: "exact" | "approximate" };

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const query = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("q")?.trim();
  if (!query || query.length < 2 || query.length > 80) return NextResponse.json({ foods: [] });

  const { data: hits, error } = await auth.client.rpc("search_foods", { q: query, max_results: 30 });
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  const list = (hits ?? []) as Hit[];
  if (!list.length) return NextResponse.json({ foods: [] });

  const { data: foods, error: foodsError } = await auth.client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).in("id", list.map((hit) => hit.found_food_id));
  if (foodsError) return NextResponse.json({ error: foodsError.message }, { status: 400 });
  const byId = new Map(((foods ?? []) as unknown as { id: string }[]).map((food) => [food.id, food]));
  return NextResponse.json({
    foods: list.flatMap((hit) => {
      const food = byId.get(hit.found_food_id);
      return food ? [{ ...food, matched_alias: hit.matched_alias, match_quality: hit.match_quality }] : [];
    }),
  });
}
New: src/app/api/foods/quick/route.ts
ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const [recentResult, favouriteResult] = await Promise.all([
    auth.client.from("food_logs").select("food_id,quantity,unit").not("food_id", "is", null).order("consumed_at", { ascending: false }).limit(60),
    auth.client.from("favourite_foods").select("food_id").order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(20),
  ]);
  if (recentResult.error || favouriteResult.error) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Could not load your quick list." }, { status: 400 });

  const recentByFood = new Map<string, { quantity: number; unit: "serving" | "g" }>();
  for (const row of recentResult.data ?? []) {
    const id = row.food_id as string;
    if (recentByFood.size >= 8 && !recentByFood.has(id)) continue;
    if (!recentByFood.has(id)) recentByFood.set(id, { quantity: Number(row.quantity), unit: row.unit === "g" ? "g" : "serving" });
  }
  const favouriteIds = (favouriteResult.data ?? []).map((row) => row.food_id as string);
  const ids = [...new Set([...recentByFood.keys(), ...favouriteIds])];
  if (!ids.length) return NextResponse.json({ favourites: [], recents: [] });

  const { data: foods, error } = await auth.client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).in("id", ids);
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  const byId = new Map(((foods ?? []) as unknown as { id: string }[]).map((food) => [food.id, food]));
  return NextResponse.json({
    favourites: favouriteIds.flatMap((id) => (byId.get(id) ? [{ food: byId.get(id) }] : [])),
    recents: [...recentByFood.entries()].flatMap(([id, last]) => (byId.get(id) ? [{ food: byId.get(id), ...last }] : [])),
  });
}
Replace: src/components/food/AddFoodExperience.tsx
