"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AddOwnFoodPanel } from "@/components/AddOwnFoodPanel";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localDate, localTime, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "@/lib/dates";
import { MEALS, mealForHour, type Meal } from "@/lib/meals";
import {
  availableUnits, defaultKcalLabel, formatQuantity, minQuantity, parseQuantity, previewPortion,
  quantityChips, quantityStep, servingNameFor, type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit,
} from "@/lib/units";

type Recent = { food: FoodSummary; quantity: number; unit: PortionUnit };
type Quick = { favourites: { food: FoodSummary }[]; recents: Recent[] };
const EMPTY_QUICK: Quick = { favourites: [], recents: [] };

export function AddFoodExperience() {
  const session = useSession();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<FoodSummary[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [quick, setQuick] = useState<Quick>(EMPTY_QUICK);
  const [food, setFood] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<PortionUnit>("serving");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("1");
  const [meal, setMeal] = useState<Meal>("breakfast");
  const [date, setDate] = useState("");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [showWhen, setShowWhen] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [lastLogged, setLastLogged] = useState<{ id: string; name: string; meal: Meal } | null>(null);
  const [own, setOwn] = useState(false);

  const today = date ? localDate(timeZone) : "";

  // Defaults are set after mount so the server and browser never disagree about "now".
  useEffect(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    setDate(localDate(timeZone, now));
    setTime(localTime(timeZone, now));
    setMeal(mealForHour(Number(localTime(timeZone, now).slice(0, 2))));
  }, [timeZone]);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick").then(setQuick).catch(() => setQuick(EMPTY_QUICK));
  }, [lastLogged, own]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setSearching(false);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      setSearching(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch<{ foods: FoodSummary[] }>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`, { signal: controller.signal });
        setResults(data.foods);
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setError("We couldn’t search right now. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setSearching(false);
      }
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query]);

  const favouriteIds = new Set(quick.favourites.map((item) => item.food.id));

  function choose(next: FoodSummary, preset?: { unit: PortionUnit; quantity: number }) {
    const units = availableUnits(next);
    const usePreset = preset && units.includes(preset.unit);
    const startUnit = usePreset ? preset.unit : units[0] ?? "serving";
    setFood(next);
    setUnit(startUnit);
    setAmount(String(usePreset ? preset.quantity : startUnit === "g" ? 100 : 1));
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    setLastLogged(null);
  }

  async function logEntry(target: FoodSummary, logUnit: PortionUnit, quantity: number): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!date || !time) return false;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const id = crypto.randomUUID();
      const consumedAt = zonedDateTimeToInstant(date, time, timeZone);
      await apiFetch("/api/logs", { method: "POST", json: { id, foodId: target.id, quantity, unit: logUnit, mealType: meal, consumedAt: consumedAt.toISOString() } });
      setLastLogged({ id, name: target.canonical_name, meal });
      setNotice("");
      return true;
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That entry couldn’t be saved. Your choices are still here.");
      return false;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveFromSheet() {
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    if (!food || quantity === null) return;
    if (await logEntry(food, unit, quantity)) {
      setFood(null);
      setQuery("");
    }
  }

  async function undo() {
    if (!lastLogged) return;
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/logs?id=${encodeURIComponent(lastLogged.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice(`${lastLogged.name} was removed.`);
      setLastLogged(null);
    } catch {
      setError("That entry couldn’t be removed. You can remove it from your diary.");
    }
  }

  async function toggleFavourite(target: FoodSummary) {
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/favourites", { method: "POST", json: { foodId: target.id, favourite: !favouriteIds.has(target.id) } });
      setQuick(await apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick"));
    } catch {
      setError("That favourite couldn’t be saved.");
    }
  }

  function nudge(direction: 1 | -1) {
    const current = parseQuantity(amount) ?? 0;
    const next = Math.max(minQuantity(unit), Math.round((current + direction * quantityStep(unit)) * 100) / 100);
    setAmount(String(next));
  }

  const whenBar = (
    <section className="ax-when" aria-label="When did you have it?">
      <div className="ax-chips">
        {MEALS.map((item) => (
          <button key={item} className={`ax-chip ${meal === item ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={meal === item} onClick={() => setMeal(item)}>{item}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="muted">
        {date === today ? "Today" : date} at {time}{" "}
        <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setShowWhen(!showWhen)}>{showWhen ? "Done" : "Change date or time"}</button>
      </p>
      {showWhen && (
        <div className="ax-when-fields">
          <label>Date<input type="date" value={date} max={today} onChange={(event) => setDate(event.target.value)} /></label>
          <label>Time<input type="time" value={time} onChange={(event) => setTime(event.target.value)} /></label>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );

  if (own) {
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Personal food" title="Add a food that isn’t listed" description="Add only what you know. Anything you leave blank stays unknown." />
        <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setOwn(false)}>← Back to search</button>
        <AddOwnFoodPanel initialName={query} onDone={(message) => { setNotice(message); setOwn(false); }} />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (food) {
    const units = availableUnits(food);
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    const preview = quantity ? previewPortion(food, unit, quantity) : null;
    const favourite = favouriteIds.has(food.id);
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="How much?" />
        <section className="portion-editor">
          <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setFood(null)}>← Change food</button>
          <div className="selected-food-heading">
            <div>
              <p className="eyebrow">Selected food</p>
              <h2>{food.canonical_name}</h2>
              {food.match_quality === "approximate" && <p className="ax-note">Closest match, not the same dish.</p>}
            </div>
            <button className="favourite-button" aria-pressed={favourite} onClick={() => void toggleFavourite(food)}>{favourite ? "★ Saved" : "☆ Save"}</button>
          </div>

          {units.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="nutrition-note">This food has no usable nutrition data, so it can’t be logged.</p>
          ) : (
            <>
              {units.length > 1 && (
                <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Unit">
                  {units.map((option) => (
                    <button
                      key={option}
                      className={`ax-chip ${unit === option ? "selected" : ""}`}
                      aria-pressed={unit === option}
                      onClick={() => { setUnit(option); setAmount(option === "g" ? "100" : "1"); }}
                    >
                      {option === "g" ? "grams" : servingNameFor(food)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              <fieldset>
                <legend>{unit === "g" ? "Amount (g)" : `How many ${servingNameFor(food)}?`}</legend>
                <div className="ax-chips">
                  {quantityChips(unit).map((value) => (
                    <button key={value} className={`ax-chip ${quantity === value ? "selected" : ""}`} onClick={() => setAmount(String(value))}>
                      {unit === "g" ? `${value} g` : formatQuantity(value)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <div className="quantity-stepper">
                  <button onClick={() => nudge(-1)} aria-label="Decrease amount">−</button>
                  <input value={amount} inputMode="decimal" onChange={(event) => setAmount(event.target.value)} aria-label="Amount" />
                  <button onClick={() => nudge(1)} aria-label="Increase amount">+</button>
                </div>
              </fieldset>

              {whenBar}

              {preview?.ok ? (
                <section className="nutrition-preview" aria-live="polite">
                  <h3>Nutrition for this portion</h3>
                  <div className="nutrition-grid">
                    {([["Calories", preview.nutrients.kcal, "kcal"], ["Protein", preview.nutrients.proteinG, "g"], ["Carbs", preview.nutrients.carbsG, "g"], ["Fat", preview.nutrients.fatG, "g"]] as const).map(([name, value, suffix]) => (
                      <div key={name}><span>{name}</span><strong>{value === null ? "—" : `${Math.round(value * 10) / 10}${suffix}`}</strong></div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  {preview.nutrients.kcal === null && <p className="muted">Calories aren’t available for this food. It will be logged as unknown, not zero.</p>}
                </section>
              ) : (
                <p className="nutrition-note">{quantity ? "Nutrition isn’t available for this amount." : "Enter an amount above zero."}</p>
              )}
              {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
              <button className="button primary full-width" disabled={saving || quantity === null || !date || !time} onClick={() => void saveFromSheet()}>
                {saving ? "Adding…" : `Add to ${meal}`}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </section>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="What did you eat?" description="Search dishes or ingredients, then choose the portion." />
      {lastLogged && (
        <div className="post-save" role="status">
          <p>{lastLogged.name} added to {lastLogged.meal}.</p>
          <Link href="/today">View today</Link>
          <button onClick={() => void undo()}>Undo</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {notice && <p className="success-note" role="status">{notice}</p>}
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {whenBar}

      <section className="search-panel">
        <label htmlFor="food-search">Search dishes or ingredients</label>
        <div className="search-input">
          <input id="food-search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Try poha, dosa, idli…" autoComplete="off" />
          <button onClick={() => setQuery("")} aria-label="Clear search">×</button>
        </div>
        <p className="search-status" role="status">{searching ? "Searching…" : query.trim().length >= 2 ? `${results.length} options found` : ""}</p>

        {query.trim().length >= 2 ? (
          <>
            {!searching && !results.length && !error && <p className="muted">We couldn’t find that dish. Try another name, or add your own.</p>}
            <div className="search-results">
              {results.map((result) => {
                const aliasDiffers = result.matched_alias && result.matched_alias.toLowerCase() !== result.canonical_name.toLowerCase();
                return (
                  <button className="search-result" key={result.id} onClick={() => choose(result)}>
                    <span>
                      <b>{result.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(result)}{result.owner_id ? " · Your food" : ""}</small>
                      {(aliasDiffers || result.match_quality === "approximate") && (
                        <small className="ax-note">
                          {aliasDiffers && `Matched “${result.matched_alias}”`}
                          {aliasDiffers && result.match_quality === "approximate" && " · "}
                          {result.match_quality === "approximate" && "Closest match, not the same dish"}
                        </small>
                      )}
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {quick.recents.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Recent</h2>
                {quick.recents.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food, { unit: item.unit, quantity: item.quantity })}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>Last: {formatQuantity(item.quantity)} {item.unit === "g" ? "g" : servingNameFor(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                    <button className="button secondary" disabled={saving || !date} onClick={() => void logEntry(item.food, item.unit, item.quantity)}>Log again</button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
            {quick.favourites.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Favourites</h2>
                {quick.favourites.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food)}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        <button className="text-link quiet-link" onClick={() => setOwn(true)}>Add a food that isn’t listed</button>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

Also save it as supabase/migrations/202611090020_own_foods.sql.

sql
-- Own foods can be archived (hidden from search) without touching past entries.
alter table public.foods add column if not exists archived_at timestamptz;
create index if not exists foods_owner_active_idx on public.foods(owner_id) where archived_at is null;

-- Same ranked search as before, now skipping archived foods.
create or replace function public.search_foods(q text, max_results integer default 30)
returns table (
  found_food_id uuid,
  match_rank integer,
  matched_alias text,
  match_quality public.alias_quality,
  alias_verified boolean
)
language sql
stable
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
  with cleaned as (
    select lower(trim(q)) as term,
           replace(replace(replace(lower(trim(q)), '\', '\\'), '%', '\%'), '_', '\_') as pattern
  ),
  hits as (
    select a.food_id,
           a.alias,
           a.match_quality as quality,
           a.verified,
           f.owner_id,
           case
             when lower(a.alias) = c.term then 1
             when lower(a.alias) like c.pattern || '%' then 2
             when lower(a.alias) like '% ' || c.pattern || '%' then 3
             else 4
           end as rank_value
    from cleaned c
    join public.food_aliases a on length(c.term) >= 2 and lower(a.alias) like '%' || c.pattern || '%'
    join public.foods f on f.id = a.food_id and f.archived_at is null
  ),
  best as (
    select distinct on (food_id) *
    from hits
    order by food_id, rank_value, verified desc, length(alias)
  )
  select b.food_id, b.rank_value, b.alias, b.quality, b.verified
  from best b
  order by b.rank_value, (b.owner_id is null), length(b.alias), b.alias
  limit least(greatest(max_results, 1), 50);
$$;

Replace src/lib/totals.ts

ts
import type { NutrientKey } from "@/lib/nutrition";

export type KcalTotal = { total: number | null; partial: boolean; knownCount: number; unknownCount: number };

/** Sums numbers without ever treating unknown as zero. Works for calories and any other nutrient. */
export function sumKnown(values: ReadonlyArray<number | null | undefined>): KcalTotal {
  let total = 0;
  let knownCount = 0;
  for (const value of values) {
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
      total += value;
      knownCount += 1;
    }
  }
  const unknownCount = values.length - knownCount;
  return {
    total: values.length === 0 ? 0 : knownCount === 0 ? null : total,
    partial: knownCount > 0 && unknownCount > 0,
    knownCount,
    unknownCount,
  };
}
export const sumKcal = sumKnown;

/** Reads one final nutrient from a food_log_nutrients embed, which may arrive as an object or a one-item array. */
export function finalValue(embed: unknown, key: NutrientKey): number | null {
  const row = (Array.isArray(embed) ? embed[0] : embed) as { final_totals?: Record<string, unknown> } | null | undefined;
  const value = row?.final_totals?.[key];
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}
export const finalKcal = (embed: unknown): number | null => finalValue(embed, "kcal");

New src/lib/day-status.ts

ts
import { intakeStatus } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import type { KcalTotal } from "@/lib/totals";

export type IntakeState = "no-goal" | "unknown" | "uncertain" | "below" | "at" | "above";
export type IntakeView = { state: IntakeState; text: string; icon: string };

/** Neutral wording only: it describes where the day stands and never judges it. */
export function describeIntake(total: KcalTotal, goal: number | null): IntakeView {
  if (goal === null) return { state: "no-goal", text: "No daily food target set.", icon: "○" };
  if (total.total === null) return { state: "unknown", text: "Calories for these entries are unknown.", icon: "?" };
  const result = intakeStatus(total.total, goal);
  const difference = result.difference ?? 0;
  if (total.partial) {
    return result.state === "above"
      ? { state: "above", text: `At least ${Math.round(difference)} kcal above your target.`, icon: "▲" }
      : { state: "uncertain", text: "Some entries have unknown calories, so the real total may be higher.", icon: "≈" };
  }
  if (result.state === "at") return { state: "at", text: "At your target.", icon: "●" };
  if (result.state === "below") return { state: "below", text: `${Math.round(-difference)} kcal remaining.`, icon: "▽" };
  return { state: "above", text: `${Math.round(difference)} kcal above your target.`, icon: "▲" };
}

New src/lib/day-status.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { describeIntake } from "./day-status";
import { sumKnown } from "./totals";

describe("describeIntake", () => {
  it("handles no target and unknown calories", () => {
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([100]), null).state).toBe("no-goal");
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([null]), 2000).state).toBe("unknown");
  });
  it("describes below, at and above with the 25 kcal tolerance", () => {
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([1500]), 2000)).toMatchObject({ state: "below", text: "500 kcal remaining." });
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([1990]), 2000).state).toBe("at");
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([2300]), 2000)).toMatchObject({ state: "above", text: "300 kcal above your target." });
  });
  it("never claims 'at target' when something is unknown", () => {
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([1990, null]), 2000).state).toBe("uncertain");
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([2300, null]), 2000).text).toContain("At least 300");
  });
  it("shows the whole target remaining on an empty day", () => {
    expect(describeIntake(sumKnown([]), 2000).text).toBe("2000 kcal remaining.");
  });
});

New src/lib/use-undo-delete.ts

ts
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

/**
 * Holds a delete for a few seconds so it can be undone. The caller removes the item from the screen
 * straight away; the delete is only sent when the time runs out, a new delete starts, or the screen closes.
 */
export function useUndoableDelete<T>(commit: (item: T) => Promise<void>, delayMs = 6000) {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const pendingRef = useRef<T | null>(null);
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const commitRef = useRef(commit);

  useEffect(() => {
    commitRef.current = commit;
  }, [commit]);

  const flush = useCallback(async () => {
    if (timerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
    const item = pendingRef.current;
    pendingRef.current = null;
    setPending(null);
    if (item !== null) await commitRef.current(item);
  }, []);

  const start = useCallback(
    (item: T) => {
      void flush();
      pendingRef.current = item;
      setPending(item);
      timerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        void flush();
      }, delayMs);
    },
    [flush, delayMs],
  );

  const undo = useCallback((): T | null => {
    if (timerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
    const item = pendingRef.current;
    pendingRef.current = null;
    setPending(null);
    return item;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    void flush();
  }, [flush]);

  return { pending, start, undo };
}

If you close the whole browser tab during those 6 seconds, the delete never happens and the entry stays. That’s the safe failure.

New src/lib/own-food.ts

ts
export type UnitKind = "count" | "household";
export type OwnFoodInput = {
  name: string;
  unitKind: UnitKind;
  servingName: string;
  kcal: number;
  proteinG: number | null;
  carbsG: number | null;
  fatG: number | null;
  sugarG: number | null;
  sodiumMg: number | null;
  note: string | null;
};
export type OwnFoodFormValues = {
  name: string;
  unitKind: UnitKind;
  servingName: string;
  mode: "portion" | "batch";
  servingsMade: string;
  kcal: string;
  protein: string;
  carbs: string;
  fat: string;
  sugar: string;
  sodium: string;
  note: string;
};

const LIMITS = { kcal: 5000, grams: 1000, sodiumMg: 20000 };
const round2 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;

/** Blank means unknown (null). Anything that is not a number of 0 or more is invalid. */
export function parseNumber(text: string): number | null | "invalid" {
  const trimmed = text.trim().replace(",", ".");
  if (trimmed === "") return null;
  const value = Number(trimmed);
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 ? value : "invalid";
}

type Parsed = { value: number | null } | { error: string };
function readNumber(raw: unknown, max: number, label: string, required: boolean): Parsed {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === "") return required ? { error: `${label} is required.` } : { value: null };
  const value = Number(raw);
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > max) return { error: `${label} must be a number from 0 to ${max}.` };
  return { value };
}

/** Validates a per-portion food. Used by both the form and the server. Returns an error message or the clean values. */
export function validateOwnFoodInput(raw: Record<string, unknown>): OwnFoodInput | string {
  const name = typeof raw.name === "string" ? raw.name.trim() : "";
  if (!name || name.length > 120) return "Enter a name of up to 120 characters.";
  if (raw.unitKind !== "count" && raw.unitKind !== "household") return "Choose how this food is counted.";
  const servingName = typeof raw.servingName === "string" ? raw.servingName.trim() : "";
  if (!servingName || servingName.length > 40) return "Name the portion, such as piece or bowl.";
  const note = typeof raw.note === "string" && raw.note.trim() ? raw.note.trim() : null;
  if (note && note.length > 500) return "Keep the note to 500 characters.";

  const kcal = readNumber(raw.kcal, LIMITS.kcal, "Calories", true);
  const protein = readNumber(raw.proteinG, LIMITS.grams, "Protein", false);
  const carbs = readNumber(raw.carbsG, LIMITS.grams, "Carbs", false);
  const fat = readNumber(raw.fatG, LIMITS.grams, "Fat", false);
  const sugar = readNumber(raw.sugarG, LIMITS.grams, "Sugar", false);
  const sodium = readNumber(raw.sodiumMg, LIMITS.sodiumMg, "Sodium", false);
  for (const field of [kcal, protein, carbs, fat, sugar, sodium]) if ("error" in field) return field.error;
  const value = (field: Parsed) => (field as { value: number | null }).value;

  return {
    name,
    unitKind: raw.unitKind,
    servingName,
    kcal: value(kcal) as number,
    proteinG: value(protein),
    carbsG: value(carbs),
    fatG: value(fat),
    sugarG: value(sugar),
    sodiumMg: value(sodium),
    note,
  };
}

/** Turns the form into per-portion values, dividing by servings when the user entered a whole batch. */
export function buildOwnFood(form: OwnFoodFormValues): { ok: true; value: OwnFoodInput } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  let divisor = 1;
  if (form.mode === "batch") {
    const servings = parseNumber(form.servingsMade);
    if (servings === null || servings === "invalid" || servings <= 0) return { ok: false, error: "Enter how many servings the batch makes." };
    divisor = servings;
  }
  const field = (text: string): number | null | "invalid" => {
    const parsed = parseNumber(text);
    return parsed === "invalid" ? "invalid" : parsed === null ? null : round2(parsed / divisor);
  };
  const values = { kcal: field(form.kcal), proteinG: field(form.protein), carbsG: field(form.carbs), fatG: field(form.fat), sugarG: field(form.sugar), sodiumMg: field(form.sodium) };
  if (Object.values(values).includes("invalid")) return { ok: false, error: "Use numbers only (0 or more), or leave a field blank if you don’t know it." };
  const checked = validateOwnFoodInput({ name: form.name, unitKind: form.unitKind, servingName: form.servingName, note: form.note, ...values });
  return typeof checked === "string" ? { ok: false, error: checked } : { ok: true, value: checked };
}

New src/lib/own-food.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildOwnFood, parseNumber, validateOwnFoodInput, type OwnFoodFormValues } from "./own-food";

const base: OwnFoodFormValues = {
  name: "Amma’s pesarattu", unitKind: "count", servingName: "piece", mode: "portion", servingsMade: "",
  kcal: "120", protein: "", carbs: "", fat: "", sugar: "", sodium: "", note: "",
};

describe("own foods", () => {
  it("keeps blank nutrients unknown, never zero", () => {
    const result = buildOwnFood(base);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.proteinG).toBeNull();
    expect(result.ok && result.value.kcal).toBe(120);
  });
  it("keeps a typed zero as a real zero", () => {
    const result = buildOwnFood({ ...base, fat: "0" });
    expect(result.ok && result.value.fatG).toBe(0);
  });
  it("divides a whole batch by the servings made", () => {
    const result = buildOwnFood({ ...base, mode: "batch", servingsMade: "8", kcal: "1600", protein: "40" });
    expect(result.ok && result.value.kcal).toBe(200);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.proteinG).toBe(5);
  });
  it("requires calories and sensible numbers", () => {
    expect(buildOwnFood({ ...base, kcal: "" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(buildOwnFood({ ...base, kcal: "-5" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(buildOwnFood({ ...base, mode: "batch", servingsMade: "0" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(buildOwnFood({ ...base, kcal: "abc" }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
  it("validates on the server side too", () => {
    expect(typeof validateOwnFoodInput({ name: "", unitKind: "count", servingName: "piece", kcal: 100 })).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof validateOwnFoodInput({ name: "Dosa", unitKind: "grams", servingName: "piece", kcal: 100 })).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof validateOwnFoodInput({ name: "Dosa", unitKind: "count", servingName: "piece", kcal: 99999 })).toBe("string");
  });
  it("parses blanks and commas", () => {
    expect(parseNumber("")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseNumber("1,5")).toBe(1.5);
    expect(parseNumber("x")).toBe("invalid");
  });
});

export const FOOD_SELECT =

  "food_servings(id,name,unit_class,grams)," +
  "food_nutrients(basis,serving_id,kcal,protein_g,carbs_g,fat_g,sat_fat_g,fibre_g,free_sugars_g,total_sugars_g,sodium_mg)";

New src/lib/server/own-foods.ts

ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { OWN_FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import type { OwnFoodInput } from "@/lib/own-food";

const nutrientValues = (input: OwnFoodInput) => ({
  kcal: input.kcal,
  protein_g: input.proteinG,
  carbs_g: input.carbsG,
  fat_g: input.fatG,
  total_sugars_g: input.sugarG,
  sodium_mg: input.sodiumMg,
});

export async function loadOwnFood(client: SupabaseClient, id: string) {
  const { data } = await client.from("foods").select(OWN_FOOD_SELECT).eq("id", id).single();
  return data ? (data as unknown as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
}

/** Creates the food, its searchable name, its portion and its nutrients. Returns the new id, or an error message. */
export async function createOwnFood(client: SupabaseClient, userId: string, input: OwnFoodInput): Promise<{ id: string } | string> {
  const { data: food, error } = await client
    .from("foods")
    .insert({ owner_id: userId, canonical_name: input.name, kind: "custom", serving_mode: input.unitKind, source_kind: "user_entered", user_note: input.note })
    .select("id")
    .single();
  if (error || !food) return error?.message ?? "Could not create the food.";
  const id = food.id as string;

  const fail = async (message: string) => {
    await client.from("foods").delete().eq("id", id); // removes the half-made food
    return message;
  };

  const alias = await client.from("food_aliases").insert({ food_id: id, alias: input.name, language: "en", script: "latin", alias_type: "user", verified: true, match_quality: "exact" });
  if (alias.error) return fail(alias.error.message);
  const serving = await client.from("food_servings").insert({ food_id: id, name: input.servingName, unit_class: input.unitKind, source: "user" }).select("id").single();
  if (serving.error || !serving.data) return fail(serving.error?.message ?? "Could not create the portion.");
  const nutrients = await client.from("food_nutrients").insert({ food_id: id, basis: "per_serving", serving_id: serving.data.id, ...nutrientValues(input) });
  if (nutrients.error) return fail(nutrients.error.message);
  return { id };
}

/** Edits an own food. Past diary entries keep the numbers they were saved with. */
export async function updateOwnFood(client: SupabaseClient, foodId: string, input: OwnFoodInput): Promise<string | null> {
  const food = await client.from("foods").update({ canonical_name: input.name, serving_mode: input.unitKind, user_note: input.note }).eq("id", foodId);
  if (food.error) return food.error.message;

  const removed = await client.from("food_aliases").delete().eq("food_id", foodId).eq("alias_type", "user");
  if (removed.error) return removed.error.message;
  const alias = await client.from("food_aliases").insert({ food_id: foodId, alias: input.name, language: "en", script: "latin", alias_type: "user", verified: true, match_quality: "exact" });
  if (alias.error) return alias.error.message;

  const serving = await client.from("food_servings").select("id").eq("food_id", foodId).limit(1).single();
  if (serving.error || !serving.data) return serving.error?.message ?? "This food has no portion to update.";
  const servingUpdate = await client.from("food_servings").update({ name: input.servingName, unit_class: input.unitKind }).eq("id", serving.data.id);
  if (servingUpdate.error) return servingUpdate.error.message;

  const nutrients = await client.from("food_nutrients").update(nutrientValues(input)).eq("food_id", foodId).eq("basis", "per_serving");
  return nutrients.error ? nutrients.error.message : null;
}
API routes (replace or new)

Replace src/app/api/foods/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { validateOwnFoodInput } from "@/lib/own-food";
import { createOwnFood, loadOwnFood, updateOwnFood } from "@/lib/server/own-foods";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const bad = (message: string, status = 400) => NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status });
async function readBody(request: Request): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null> {
  try {
    return (await request.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const body = await readBody(request);
  if (!body) return bad("Invalid food.");
  const input = validateOwnFoodInput(body);
  if (typeof input === "string") return bad(input);
  const created = await createOwnFood(auth.client, auth.user.id, input);
  if (typeof created === "string") return bad(created);
  return NextResponse.json({ id: created.id, food: await loadOwnFood(auth.client, created.id) }, { status: 201 });
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const body = await readBody(request);
  if (!body || typeof body.id !== "string") return bad("Invalid food.");
  const input = validateOwnFoodInput(body);
  if (typeof input === "string") return bad(input);
  const { data: owned } = await auth.client.from("foods").select("id").eq("id", body.id).eq("owner_id", auth.user.id).maybeSingle();
  if (!owned) return bad("Food not found.", 404);
  const failure = await updateOwnFood(auth.client, body.id, input);
  if (failure) return bad(failure);
  return NextResponse.json({ id: body.id, food: await loadOwnFood(auth.client, body.id) });
}

/** "Delete" archives the food: it leaves search, but past diary entries are untouched. */
export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const id = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("id");
  if (!id) return bad("A food id is required.");
  const { error } = await auth.client.from("foods").update({ archived_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", id).eq("owner_id", auth.user.id);
  return error ? bad(error.message) : new NextResponse(null, { status: 204 });
}

New src/app/api/foods/mine/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { OWN_FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const { data, error } = await auth.client
    .from("foods")
    .select(OWN_FOOD_SELECT)
    .eq("owner_id", auth.user.id)
    .eq("kind", "custom")
    .is("archived_at", null)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  return NextResponse.json({ foods: data ?? [] });
}

Replace src/app/api/foods/quick/route.ts (hides archived foods)

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const [recentResult, favouriteResult] = await Promise.all([
    auth.client.from("food_logs").select("food_id,quantity,unit").not("food_id", "is", null).order("consumed_at", { ascending: false }).limit(60),
    auth.client.from("favourite_foods").select("food_id").order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(20),
  ]);
  if (recentResult.error || favouriteResult.error) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Could not load your quick list." }, { status: 400 });

  const recentByFood = new Map<string, { quantity: number; unit: "serving" | "g" }>();
  for (const row of recentResult.data ?? []) {
    const id = row.food_id as string;
    if (recentByFood.size >= 12 && !recentByFood.has(id)) continue;
    if (!recentByFood.has(id)) recentByFood.set(id, { quantity: Number(row.quantity), unit: row.unit === "g" ? "g" : "serving" });
  }
  const favouriteIds = (favouriteResult.data ?? []).map((row) => row.food_id as string);
  const ids = [...new Set([...recentByFood.keys(), ...favouriteIds])];
  if (!ids.length) return NextResponse.json({ favourites: [], recents: [] });

  const { data: foods, error } = await auth.client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).in("id", ids);
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  const byId = new Map(
    ((foods ?? []) as unknown as { id: string; archived_at?: string | null }[]).filter((food) => !food.archived_at).map((food) => [food.id, food]),
  );
  const recents = [...recentByFood.entries()].flatMap(([id, last]) => (byId.get(id) ? [{ food: byId.get(id), ...last }] : [])).slice(0, 8);
  return NextResponse.json({
    favourites: favouriteIds.flatMap((id) => (byId.get(id) ? [{ food: byId.get(id) }] : [])),
    recents,
  });
}
Components

New src/components/food/PortionPicker.tsx (shared by Add Food and the edit sheet)

tsx
"use client";

import {
  availableUnits, formatQuantity, minQuantity, parseQuantity, previewPortion, quantityChips, quantityStep, servingNameFor,
  type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit,
} from "@/lib/units";

type Props = {
  food: FoodSummary;
  unit: PortionUnit;
  amount: string;
  onUnitChange: (unit: PortionUnit) => void;
  onAmountChange: (amount: string) => void;
};

export function PortionPicker({ food, unit, amount, onUnitChange, onAmountChange }: Props) {
  const units = availableUnits(food);
  const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
  const preview = quantity ? previewPortion(food, unit, quantity) : null;

  function nudge(direction: 1 | -1) {
    const current = parseQuantity(amount) ?? 0;
    onAmountChange(String(Math.max(minQuantity(unit), Math.round((current + direction * quantityStep(unit)) * 100) / 100)));
  }

  if (units.length === 0) return <p className="nutrition-note">This food has no usable nutrition data, so it can’t be logged.</p>;

  return (
    <>
      {units.length > 1 && (
        <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Unit">
          {units.map((option) => (
            <button
              key={option}
              className={`ax-chip ${unit === option ? "selected" : ""}`}
              aria-pressed={unit === option}
              onClick={() => {
                onUnitChange(option);
                onAmountChange(option === "g" ? "100" : "1");
              }}
            >
              {option === "g" ? "grams" : servingNameFor(food)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{unit === "g" ? "Amount (g)" : `How many ${servingNameFor(food)}?`}</legend>
        <div className="ax-chips">
          {quantityChips(unit).map((value) => (
            <button key={value} className={`ax-chip ${quantity === value ? "selected" : ""}`} onClick={() => onAmountChange(String(value))}>
              {unit === "g" ? `${value} g` : formatQuantity(value)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="quantity-stepper">
          <button onClick={() => nudge(-1)} aria-label="Decrease amount">−</button>
          <input value={amount} inputMode="decimal" onChange={(event) => onAmountChange(event.target.value)} aria-label="Amount" />
          <button onClick={() => nudge(1)} aria-label="Increase amount">+</button>
        </div>
      </fieldset>
      {preview?.ok ? (
        <section className="nutrition-preview" aria-live="polite">
          <h3>Nutrition for this portion</h3>
          <div className="nutrition-grid">
            {([["Calories", preview.nutrients.kcal, "kcal"], ["Protein", preview.nutrients.proteinG, "g"], ["Carbs", preview.nutrients.carbsG, "g"], ["Fat", preview.nutrients.fatG, "g"]] as const).map(([name, value, suffix]) => (
              <div key={name}><span>{name}</span><strong>{value === null ? "—" : `${Math.round(value * 10) / 10}${suffix}`}</strong></div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {preview.nutrients.kcal === null && <p className="muted">Calories aren’t available for this food. It is logged as unknown, not zero.</p>}
        </section>
      ) : (
        <p className="nutrition-note">{quantity ? "Nutrition isn’t available for this amount." : "Enter an amount above zero."}</p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

New src/components/food/OwnFoodForm.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { buildOwnFood, type OwnFoodFormValues } from "@/lib/own-food";
import { servingNameFor, type FoodSummary } from "@/lib/units";

export type OwnFoodRecord = FoodSummary & { user_note?: string | null };

const SERVING_SUGGESTIONS = {
  count: ["piece", "idli", "dosa", "roti", "vada", "laddu"],
  household: ["portion", "bowl", "katori", "cup", "plate", "glass", "spoon"],
} as const;

const text = (value: number | null | undefined) => (value === null || value === undefined ? "" : String(value));

function initialValues(food: OwnFoodRecord | undefined, name: string): OwnFoodFormValues {
  const row = food?.food_nutrients?.find((item) => item.basis === "per_serving");
  return {
    name: food?.canonical_name ?? name,
    unitKind: food?.serving_mode === "count" ? "count" : "household",
    servingName: food ? servingNameFor(food) : "portion",
    mode: "portion",
    servingsMade: "",
    kcal: text(row?.kcal),
    protein: text(row?.protein_g),
    carbs: text(row?.carbs_g),
    fat: text(row?.fat_g),
    sugar: text(row?.total_sugars_g),
    sodium: text(row?.sodium_mg),
    note: food?.user_note ?? "",
  };
}

type Props = { food?: OwnFoodRecord; initialName?: string; onSaved: (food: OwnFoodRecord) => void; onCancel?: () => void };

export function OwnFoodForm({ food, initialName = "", onSaved, onCancel }: Props) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<OwnFoodFormValues>(() => initialValues(food, initialName));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const set = <K extends keyof OwnFoodFormValues>(key: K, value: OwnFoodFormValues[K]) => setValues((old) => ({ ...old, [key]: value }));
  const batch = values.mode === "batch";
  const suffix = batch ? " (whole batch)" : " (per portion)";

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const built = buildOwnFood(values);
    if (!built.ok) {
      setError(built.error);
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const data = await apiFetch<{ food: OwnFoodRecord }>("/api/foods", { method: food ? "PATCH" : "POST", json: food ? { id: food.id, ...built.value } : built.value });
      onSaved(data.food);
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Your food couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className="form-card" onSubmit={submit}>
      <label>Food name
        <input value={values.name} onChange={(event) => set("name", event.target.value)} maxLength={120} />
      </label>

      <div>
        <p className="muted">How do you count it?</p>
        <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="How you count it">
          <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${values.unitKind === "count" ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={values.unitKind === "count"} onClick={() => { set("unitKind", "count"); set("servingName", "piece"); }}>By number (idli, dosa)</button>
          <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${values.unitKind === "household" ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={values.unitKind === "household"} onClick={() => { set("unitKind", "household"); set("servingName", "portion"); }}>By portion (curry, rice)</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <label>One portion is called
        <input list="own-serving-names" value={values.servingName} onChange={(event) => set("servingName", event.target.value)} maxLength={40} />
        <datalist id="own-serving-names">{SERVING_SUGGESTIONS[values.unitKind].map((name) => <option key={name} value={name} />)}</datalist>
      </label>

      <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="What you know">
        <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${!batch ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={!batch} onClick={() => set("mode", "portion")}>I know one portion</button>
        <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${batch ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={batch} onClick={() => set("mode", "batch")}>I know the whole batch</button>
      </div>
      {batch && (
        <label>Servings the batch makes
          <input inputMode="decimal" value={values.servingsMade} onChange={(event) => set("servingsMade", event.target.value)} placeholder="For example, 8" />
        </label>
      )}

      <label>Calories{suffix} (required)
        <input inputMode="decimal" value={values.kcal} onChange={(event) => set("kcal", event.target.value)} />
      </label>
      <p className="muted">Everything below is optional. Leave a field blank if you don’t know it; blank stays unknown, never zero.</p>
      <label>Protein (g){suffix}<input inputMode="decimal" value={values.protein} onChange={(event) => set("protein", event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label>Carbs (g){suffix}<input inputMode="decimal" value={values.carbs} onChange={(event) => set("carbs", event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label>Fat (g){suffix}<input inputMode="decimal" value={values.fat} onChange={(event) => set("fat", event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label>Sugar (g){suffix}<input inputMode="decimal" value={values.sugar} onChange={(event) => set("sugar", event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label>Sodium (mg){suffix}<input inputMode="decimal" value={values.sodium} onChange={(event) => set("sodium", event.target.value)} /></label>
      <label>Where the numbers came from (optional)
        <input value={values.note} onChange={(event) => set("note", event.target.value)} maxLength={500} placeholder="For example, packet label or my own calculation" />
      </label>

      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <div className="install-actions">
        <button className="button primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving…" : food ? "Save changes" : "Save and choose amount"}</button>
        {onCancel && <button type="button" className="button secondary" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

New src/components/dashboard/IntakeRing.tsx

tsx
import type { IntakeState } from "@/lib/day-status";

type Props = { fraction: number; state: IntakeState; label: string; sublabel: string };

export function IntakeRing({ fraction, state, label, sublabel }: Props) {
  const radius = 54;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fraction));
  return (
    <svg className="tx-ring" viewBox="0 0 132 132" role="img" aria-label={`${label}. ${sublabel}`}>
      <circle className="tx-ring-track" cx="66" cy="66" r={radius} fill="none" strokeWidth="12" />
      {clamped > 0 && (
        <circle
          className={`tx-ring-fill ${state}`}
          cx="66" cy="66" r={radius} fill="none" strokeWidth="12" strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeDasharray={`${clamped * circumference} ${circumference}`}
          transform="rotate(-90 66 66)"
        />
      )}
      <text className="tx-ring-text" x="66" y="66" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="22" fontWeight="800">{label}</text>
      <text className="tx-ring-sub" x="66" y="86" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="11">{sublabel}</text>
    </svg>
  );
}

New src/components/dashboard/EditEntrySheet.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { PortionPicker } from "@/components/food/PortionPicker";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localTime, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "@/lib/dates";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { MEALS, type Meal } from "@/lib/meals";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { availableUnits, parseQuantity, type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit } from "@/lib/units";

export type EditableLog = {
  id: string;
  food_id: string | null;
  name_snapshot: string;
  quantity: number;
  unit: string;
  meal_type: string;
  consumed_at: string;
  local_date: string;
};

type Props = { log: EditableLog; timeZone: string; onClose: () => void; onSaved: () => void; onRemove: () => void };

export function EditEntrySheet({ log, timeZone, onClose, onSaved, onRemove }: Props) {
  const [food, setFood] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(Boolean(log.food_id));
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<PortionUnit>(log.unit === "g" ? "g" : "serving");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(String(Number(log.quantity)));
  const [meal, setMeal] = useState<Meal>((MEALS as readonly string[]).includes(log.meal_type) ? (log.meal_type as Meal) : "snack");
  const [date, setDate] = useState(log.local_date);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(localTime(timeZone, new Date(log.consumed_at)));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!log.food_id) return;
    supabase()
      .from("foods")
      .select(FOOD_SELECT)
      .eq("id", log.food_id)
      .single()
      .then(({ data }) => {
        setFood(data ? (data as unknown as FoodSummary) : null);
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, [log.food_id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => event.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
  const canSave = Boolean(food) && availableUnits(food ?? ({} as FoodSummary)).length > 0 && quantity !== null && Boolean(date) && Boolean(time);

  async function save() {
    if (!food || quantity === null) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/logs", {
        method: "PATCH",
        json: { id: log.id, quantity, unit, mealType: meal, consumedAt: zonedDateTimeToInstant(date, time, timeZone).toISOString() },
      });
      onSaved();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Your changes couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="ax-sheet-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="ax-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`Edit ${log.name_snapshot}`} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="ax-sheet-head">
          <h2>{log.name_snapshot}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /></div>
        ) : food ? (
          <PortionPicker food={food} unit={unit} amount={amount} onUnitChange={setUnit} onAmountChange={setAmount} />
        ) : (
          <p className="nutrition-note">This food is no longer in the catalogue, so its amount can’t be changed. You can still remove the entry.</p>
        )}

        {food && (
          <>
            <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Meal">
              {MEALS.map((item) => (
                <button key={item} className={`ax-chip ${meal === item ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={meal === item} onClick={() => setMeal(item)}>{item}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="ax-when-fields">
              <label>Date<input type="date" value={date} onChange={(event) => setDate(event.target.value)} /></label>
              <label>Time<input type="time" value={time} onChange={(event) => setTime(event.target.value)} /></label>
            </div>
            <p className="muted">Saving re-calculates this entry from the food’s current values. Other days are not touched.</p>
          </>
        )}

        {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
        <div className="install-actions">
          {food && <button className="button primary" disabled={!canSave || saving} onClick={() => void save()}>{saving ? "Saving…" : "Save changes"}</button>}
          <button className="button secondary" onClick={onRemove}>Remove entry</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Replace src/components/dashboard/TodayDashboard.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { EditEntrySheet, type EditableLog } from "@/components/dashboard/EditEntrySheet";
import { IntakeRing } from "@/components/dashboard/IntakeRing";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { CapabilityState } from "@/components/RouteStates";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { describeIntake } from "@/lib/day-status";
import { localDate, shiftDate } from "@/lib/dates";
import { MEALS } from "@/lib/meals";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { finalKcal, finalValue, sumKnown } from "@/lib/totals";
import { useUndoableDelete } from "@/lib/use-undo-delete";
import { formatQuantity } from "@/lib/units";

type Log = EditableLog & { food_log_nutrients: unknown };
const SELECT = "id,food_id,name_snapshot,quantity,unit,meal_type,consumed_at,local_date,food_log_nutrients(final_totals)";
const DATE_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

const quantityText = (log: Log) => (log.unit === "g" ? `${formatQuantity(Number(log.quantity))} g` : `${formatQuantity(Number(log.quantity))} ${log.unit}`);
const weekday = (date: string) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-IN", { weekday: "short", timeZone: "UTC" }).format(new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`));
const kcalText = (total: ReturnType<typeof sumKnown>) => (total.total === null ? "Calories unknown" : `${total.partial ? "≥ " : ""}${Math.round(total.total)} kcal`);

export function TodayDashboard() {
  const session = useSession();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";
  const db = useMemo(supabase, []);
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const today = localDate(timeZone);
  const paramDate = params.get("date");
  const [date, setDate] = useState(() => (paramDate && DATE_PATTERN.test(paramDate) && paramDate <= today ? paramDate : today));
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<Log[]>([]);
  const [intakeGoal, setIntakeGoal] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [activityGoal, setActivityGoal] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [previousCount, setPreviousCount] = useState(0);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"loading" | "ready" | "error">("loading");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Log | null>(null);
  const [copying, setCopying] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setStatus("loading");
    const [goalsResult, logsResult, previousResult] = await Promise.all([
      db.from("goal_versions").select("kind,value").lte("starts_at", date).or(`ends_at.is.null,ends_at.gt.${date}`),
      db.from("food_logs").select(SELECT).eq("local_date", date).order("consumed_at"),
      db.from("food_logs").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("local_date", shiftDate(date, -1)),
    ]);
    if (goalsResult.error || logsResult.error) {
      setStatus("error");
      return;
    }
    const goals = (goalsResult.data ?? []) as { kind: string; value: number }[];
    const find = (kind: string) => {
      const row = goals.find((goal) => goal.kind === kind);
      return row ? Number(row.value) : null;
    };
    setIntakeGoal(find("intake_kcal"));
    setActivityGoal(find("activity_kcal"));
    setLogs((logsResult.data ?? []) as unknown as Log[]);
    setPreviousCount(previousResult.count ?? 0);
    setStatus("ready");
  }, [db, date]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  const { pending, start: startRemove, undo } = useUndoableDelete<Log>(async (log) => {
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/logs?id=${encodeURIComponent(log.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    } catch {
      setError("That entry couldn’t be removed.");
      void load();
    }
  });

  function remove(log: Log) {
    setEditing(null);
    setLogs((old) => old.filter((item) => item.id !== log.id));
    startRemove(log);
  }
  function restore() {
    const item = undo();
    if (item) setLogs((old) => [...old, item].sort((a, b) => a.consumed_at.localeCompare(b.consumed_at)));
  }

  async function copyPrevious() {
    setCopying(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/logs/copy-yesterday", { method: "POST", json: { fromDate: shiftDate(date, -1), toDate: date } });
      await load();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Could not copy those entries.");
    } finally {
      setCopying(false);
    }
  }

  const kcal = sumKnown(logs.map((log) => finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients)));
  const view = describeIntake(kcal, intakeGoal);
  const fraction = intakeGoal && kcal.total !== null ? kcal.total / intakeGoal : 0;
  const ringLabel = kcal.total === null ? "?" : `${kcal.partial ? "≥" : ""}${Math.round(kcal.total).toLocaleString("en-IN")}`;
  const ringSub = kcal.total === null ? "unknown" : intakeGoal ? `of ${intakeGoal.toLocaleString("en-IN")} kcal` : "kcal";
  const macro = (key: "proteinG" | "carbsG" | "fatG") => sumKnown(logs.map((log) => finalValue(log.food_log_nutrients, key)));
  const macroText = (total: ReturnType<typeof sumKnown>) => (total.total === null ? "—" : `${total.partial ? "≥ " : ""}${Math.round(total.total)} g`);

  const stripEnd = shiftDate(date, 3) < today ? shiftDate(date, 3) : today;
  const stripDays = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => shiftDate(stripEnd, index - 6));

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Daily dashboard" title={date === today ? "Today" : date} action={<Link className="button primary" href={`/add?date=${date}`}>Add food</Link>} />

      <div className="tx-strip" role="group" aria-label="Choose a day">
        {stripDays.map((day) => (
          <button key={day} className={`tx-day ${day === date ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={day === date} onClick={() => setDate(day)}>
            <small>{weekday(day)}</small>
            <b>{Number(day.slice(8))}</b>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="date-row">
        <label htmlFor="today-date">Jump to a date</label>
        <input id="today-date" type="date" value={date} max={today} onChange={(event) => event.target.value && setDate(event.target.value)} />
      </div>

      {status === "loading" ? (
        <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /><div /></div>
      ) : status === "error" ? (
        <CapabilityState title="This day could not load" detail="Your diary was not changed. Check your connection and try again." action="Try again" href="/today" />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="tx-summary">
            <IntakeRing fraction={fraction} state={view.state} label={ringLabel} sublabel={ringSub} />
            <div>
              <p className="eyebrow">Intake</p>
              <p className="tx-status"><span aria-hidden="true">{view.icon} </span>{view.text}</p>
              {kcal.partial && <p className="tx-status">{kcal.unknownCount} entr{kcal.unknownCount === 1 ? "y has" : "ies have"} unknown calories.</p>}
              {activityGoal !== null && <p className="tx-status">Activity target: {activityGoal} kcal</p>}
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="tx-macros" aria-label="Macros">
            <div className="tx-macro"><span>Protein</span><strong>{macroText(macro("proteinG"))}</strong></div>
            <div className="tx-macro"><span>Carbs</span><strong>{macroText(macro("carbsG"))}</strong></div>
            <div className="tx-macro"><span>Fat</span><strong>{macroText(macro("fatG"))}</strong></div>
          </section>

          {intakeGoal === null && activityGoal === null && (
            <section className="state-card">
              <h2>Set your daily targets</h2>
              <p>Add a food target and an activity target. Both are optional and personal.</p>
              <Link className="button secondary" href="/goals">Set targets</Link>
            </section>
          )}
          {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}

          {logs.length === 0 && previousCount > 0 && (
            <button className="button secondary" disabled={copying} onClick={() => void copyPrevious()}>
              {copying ? "Copying…" : `Copy ${previousCount} entr${previousCount === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} from ${shiftDate(date, -1)}`}
            </button>
          )}

          {MEALS.map((meal) => {
            const entries = logs.filter((log) => log.meal_type === meal);
            const subtotal = sumKnown(entries.map((log) => finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients)));
            return (
              <section className="tx-meal" key={meal}>
                <div className="tx-meal-head">
                  <div>
                    <h3>{meal}</h3>
                    <small>{entries.length === 0 ? "Nothing yet" : kcalText(subtotal)}</small>
                  </div>
                  <Link className="tx-add" href={`/add?meal=${meal}&date=${date}`} aria-label={`Add food to ${meal}`}>+</Link>
                </div>
                {entries.map((log) => {
                  const entryKcal = finalKcal(log.food_log_nutrients);
                  return (
                    <button className="tx-entry" key={log.id} onClick={() => setEditing(log)}>
                      <span><b>{log.name_snapshot}</b><small>{quantityText(log)}</small></span>
                      <strong>{entryKcal === null ? "Calories unknown" : `${Math.round(entryKcal)} kcal`}</strong>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </section>
            );
          })}
        </>
      )}

      {editing && (
        <EditEntrySheet
          key={editing.id}
          log={editing}
          timeZone={timeZone}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSaved={() => { setEditing(null); void load(); }}
          onRemove={() => remove(editing)}
        />
      )}
      {pending && (
        <div className="tx-undo" role="status">
          <span>{pending.name_snapshot} removed.</span>
          <button className="button secondary" onClick={restore}>Undo</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

Replace src/components/food/AddFoodExperience.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { OwnFoodForm } from "@/components/food/OwnFoodForm";
import { PortionPicker } from "@/components/food/PortionPicker";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localDate, localTime, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "@/lib/dates";
import { MEALS, mealForHour, type Meal } from "@/lib/meals";
import { availableUnits, defaultKcalLabel, formatQuantity, parseQuantity, servingNameFor, type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit } from "@/lib/units";

type Recent = { food: FoodSummary; quantity: number; unit: PortionUnit };
type Quick = { favourites: { food: FoodSummary }[]; recents: Recent[] };
const EMPTY_QUICK: Quick = { favourites: [], recents: [] };
const DATE_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

export function AddFoodExperience() {
  const session = useSession();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<FoodSummary[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [quick, setQuick] = useState<Quick>(EMPTY_QUICK);
  const [food, setFood] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<PortionUnit>("serving");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("1");
  const [meal, setMeal] = useState<Meal>("breakfast");
  const [date, setDate] = useState("");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [showWhen, setShowWhen] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [lastLogged, setLastLogged] = useState<{ id: string; name: string; meal: Meal; date: string } | null>(null);
  const [own, setOwn] = useState(false);

  const today = date ? localDate(timeZone) : "";

  // Defaults (or the meal and date passed in from Today) are set after mount so server and browser agree.
  useEffect(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const todayDate = localDate(timeZone, now);
    const dateParam = params.get("date");
    const mealParam = params.get("meal");
    const useParamDate = Boolean(dateParam && DATE_PATTERN.test(dateParam) && dateParam <= todayDate);
    setDate(useParamDate ? (dateParam as string) : todayDate);
    setTime(localTime(timeZone, now));
    setMeal(MEALS.includes(mealParam as Meal) ? (mealParam as Meal) : mealForHour(Number(localTime(timeZone, now).slice(0, 2))));
  }, [timeZone, params]);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick").then(setQuick).catch(() => setQuick(EMPTY_QUICK));
  }, [lastLogged, own]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setSearching(false);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      setSearching(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch<{ foods: FoodSummary[] }>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`, { signal: controller.signal });
        setResults(data.foods);
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setError("We couldn’t search right now. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setSearching(false);
      }
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query]);

  const favouriteIds = new Set(quick.favourites.map((item) => item.food.id));

  function choose(next: FoodSummary, preset?: { unit: PortionUnit; quantity: number }) {
    const units = availableUnits(next);
    const usePreset = preset && units.includes(preset.unit);
    const startUnit = usePreset ? preset.unit : units[0] ?? "serving";
    setFood(next);
    setUnit(startUnit);
    setAmount(String(usePreset ? preset.quantity : startUnit === "g" ? 100 : 1));
    setError("");
    setLastLogged(null);
  }

  async function logEntry(target: FoodSummary, logUnit: PortionUnit, quantity: number): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!date || !time) return false;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const id = crypto.randomUUID();
      const consumedAt = zonedDateTimeToInstant(date, time, timeZone);
      await apiFetch("/api/logs", { method: "POST", json: { id, foodId: target.id, quantity, unit: logUnit, mealType: meal, consumedAt: consumedAt.toISOString() } });
      setLastLogged({ id, name: target.canonical_name, meal, date });
      return true;
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That entry couldn’t be saved. Your choices are still here.");
      return false;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveFromSheet() {
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    if (!food || quantity === null) return;
    if (await logEntry(food, unit, quantity)) {
      setFood(null);
      setQuery("");
    }
  }

  async function undo() {
    if (!lastLogged) return;
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/logs?id=${encodeURIComponent(lastLogged.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setLastLogged(null);
    } catch {
      setError("That entry couldn’t be removed. You can remove it from Today.");
    }
  }

  async function toggleFavourite(target: FoodSummary) {
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/favourites", { method: "POST", json: { foodId: target.id, favourite: !favouriteIds.has(target.id) } });
      setQuick(await apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick"));
    } catch {
      setError("That favourite couldn’t be saved.");
    }
  }

  const whenBar = (
    <section className="ax-when" aria-label="When did you have it?">
      <div className="ax-chips">
        {MEALS.map((item) => (
          <button key={item} className={`ax-chip ${meal === item ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={meal === item} onClick={() => setMeal(item)}>{item}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="muted">
        {date === today ? "Today" : date} at {time}{" "}
        <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setShowWhen(!showWhen)}>{showWhen ? "Done" : "Change date or time"}</button>
      </p>
      {showWhen && (
        <div className="ax-when-fields">
          <label>Date<input type="date" value={date} max={today} onChange={(event) => setDate(event.target.value)} /></label>
          <label>Time<input type="time" value={time} onChange={(event) => setTime(event.target.value)} /></label>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );

  if (own) {
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Your own food" title="Add a food that isn’t listed" description="Add what you know. Anything you leave blank stays unknown." />
        <OwnFoodForm
          initialName={query.trim()}
          onCancel={() => setOwn(false)}
          onSaved={(saved) => {
            setOwn(false);
            choose(saved);
          }}
        />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (food) {
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    const favourite = favouriteIds.has(food.id);
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="How much?" />
        <section className="portion-editor">
          <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setFood(null)}>← Change food</button>
          <div className="selected-food-heading">
            <div>
              <p className="eyebrow">Selected food</p>
              <h2>{food.canonical_name}</h2>
              {food.match_quality === "approximate" && <p className="ax-note">Closest match, not the same dish.</p>}
            </div>
            <button className="favourite-button" aria-pressed={favourite} onClick={() => void toggleFavourite(food)}>{favourite ? "★ Saved" : "☆ Save"}</button>
          </div>
          <PortionPicker food={food} unit={unit} amount={amount} onUnitChange={setUnit} onAmountChange={setAmount} />
          {availableUnits(food).length > 0 && (
            <>
              {whenBar}
              {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
              <button className="button primary full-width" disabled={saving || quantity === null || !date || !time} onClick={() => void saveFromSheet()}>
                {saving ? "Adding…" : `Add to ${meal}`}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </section>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="What did you eat?" description="Search dishes or ingredients, then choose the portion." />
      {lastLogged && (
        <div className="post-save" role="status">
          <p>{lastLogged.name} added to {lastLogged.meal}.</p>
          <Link href={`/today?date=${lastLogged.date}`}>View day</Link>
          <button onClick={() => void undo()}>Undo</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {whenBar}

      <section className="search-panel">
        <label htmlFor="food-search">Search dishes or ingredients</label>
        <div className="search-input">
          <input id="food-search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Try poha, dosa, idli…" autoComplete="off" />
          <button onClick={() => setQuery("")} aria-label="Clear search">×</button>
        </div>
        <p className="search-status" role="status">{searching ? "Searching…" : query.trim().length >= 2 ? `${results.length} options found` : ""}</p>

        {query.trim().length >= 2 ? (
          <>
            {!searching && !results.length && !error && <p className="muted">We couldn’t find that dish. Try another name, or add your own below.</p>}
            <div className="search-results">
              {results.map((result) => {
                const aliasDiffers = result.matched_alias && result.matched_alias.toLowerCase() !== result.canonical_name.toLowerCase();
                return (
                  <button className="search-result" key={result.id} onClick={() => choose(result)}>
                    <span>
                      <b>{result.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(result)}{result.owner_id ? " · Your food" : ""}</small>
                      {(aliasDiffers || result.match_quality === "approximate") && (
                        <small className="ax-note">
                          {aliasDiffers && `Matched “${result.matched_alias}”`}
                          {aliasDiffers && result.match_quality === "approximate" && " · "}
                          {result.match_quality === "approximate" && "Closest match, not the same dish"}
                        </small>
                      )}
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {quick.recents.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Recent</h2>
                {quick.recents.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food, { unit: item.unit, quantity: item.quantity })}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>Last: {formatQuantity(item.quantity)} {item.unit === "g" ? "g" : servingNameFor(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                    <button className="button secondary" disabled={saving || !date} onClick={() => void logEntry(item.food, item.unit, item.quantity)}>Log again</button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
            {quick.favourites.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Favourites</h2>
                {quick.favourites.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food)}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        <button className="text-link quiet-link" onClick={() => setOwn(true)}>Add a food that isn’t listed</button>
        <p className="muted"><Link href="/my-foods">Manage my foods</Link></p>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}
