New src/app/(app)/my-foods/page.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { OwnFoodForm, type OwnFoodRecord } from "@/components/food/OwnFoodForm";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { defaultKcalLabel } from "@/lib/units";

export default function MyFoodsPage() {
  const [foods, setFoods] = useState<OwnFoodRecord[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"loading" | "ready" | "error">("loading");
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [archiving, setArchiving] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const data = await apiFetch<{ foods: OwnFoodRecord[] }>("/api/foods/mine");
      setFoods(data.foods);
      setState("ready");
    } catch {
      setState("error");
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  async function archive(food: OwnFoodRecord) {
    if (archiving !== food.id) {
      setArchiving(food.id);
      return;
    }
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/foods?id=${encodeURIComponent(food.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setMessage(`${food.canonical_name} was archived. Past diary entries keep their numbers.`);
      setArchiving(null);
      await load();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That food couldn’t be archived.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Your own foods" title="My foods" description="Foods you added yourself. Changing one never changes past diary entries." action={<Link className="button primary" href="/add">Add food</Link>} />
      {message && <p className="success-note" role="status">{message}</p>}
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {state === "loading" ? (
        <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /></div>
      ) : state === "error" ? (
        <section className="state-card"><h2>Your foods didn’t load</h2><p>Check your connection and try again.</p><button className="button primary" onClick={() => void load()}>Try again</button></section>
      ) : foods.length === 0 ? (
        <section className="state-card">
          <h2>No foods of your own yet</h2>
          <p>When you can’t find a dish, add it from the Add screen and it will appear here.</p>
          <Link className="button primary" href="/add">Add food</Link>
        </section>
      ) : (
        foods.map((food) =>
          editingId === food.id ? (
            <OwnFoodForm
              key={food.id}
              food={food}
              onCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
              onSaved={() => { setEditingId(null); setMessage("Saved."); void load(); }}
            />
          ) : (
            <section className="tx-meal" key={food.id}>
              <div className="tx-meal-head">
                <div>
                  <h3>{food.canonical_name}</h3>
                  <small>{defaultKcalLabel(food)}</small>
                </div>
                <div className="install-actions">
                  <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => { setMessage(""); setEditingId(food.id); }}>Edit</button>
                  <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => void archive(food)}>{archiving === food.id ? "Tap again to archive" : "Archive"}</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>
          ),
        )
      )}
    </>
  );
}

}

.tx-status { margin: 4px 0 0; color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.4; }

P4 checks

Today shows a 7-day strip, an intake ring with a status line, a Protein/Carbs/Fat row, and four meal cards. Each meal card has its own + that opens Add with that meal and day already chosen.
Tap an entry to open the edit sheet. Change the amount or meal and save, and the totals update. Change the date, and the entry moves to that day.
Tap “Remove entry”. It disappears at once, and an Undo bar shows for about 6 seconds. Undo brings it back. If you let it time out, it stays gone after a reload.
Add something with a missing calorie value. The ring shows “≥” and the line says some entries have unknown calories, not “at your target”.
After adding from a backdated day, the green bar’s “View day” link opens that day.
Old /log links go to Today.

P5 checks

In Add, search something that doesn’t exist, tap “Add a food that isn’t listed”, and enter a name and calories only. After saving, you land directly on “How much?” for that food. Log it.
Search for it by name, and it now appears with “Your food”.
Try “I know the whole batch”: 1600 kcal for 8 servings should log 200 kcal per serving.
Leave protein blank and log it. Today’s Protein shows “—”, not 0 g.
Profile, then My foods. Edit the calories and save. Entries you already logged keep their old numbers. Archive the food and it disappears from search, but past entries stay.

Good, now p6, p7

Before writing P6 and P7, I’m checking the exact adjustment-preset and product tables, so the code matches what’s in your database.

I found two issues in the existing code, and P6/P7 include the fixes. Neither is a new bug from P4/P5.

Favourites and delete account (existing 204 bug): both routes return a JSON body with status 204, which makes the server throw. So the favourite button can show an error even though it saved, and Delete account would succeed and then show “failed”.
Product tables (RLS): the product_details and product_barcodes tables have read-only security rules. Saving a scanned product would be refused until P7’s SQL adds write rules for your own products.

None of this is tested, since I can’t run your app from here.

Two scope calls:

Estimate presets: I’m not shipping a UI for them. The adjustment_presets table is empty, and your own rule is that each preset needs a written source. The Estimate badge is built in, ready for when presets exist. A good source for them later is the calorie difference between related INDB records (for example Poha and Vegetable poha).
Measured adjustments: you pick any food that has per-100 g data, enter grams, and choose Added or Left out. The numbers are calculated from published values, so no USDA import is needed.
Fixes (replace both files)

src/app/api/favourites/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const { foodId, favourite } = (await request.json()) as { foodId?: string; favourite?: boolean };
  if (!foodId) return NextResponse.json({ error: "foodId is required" }, { status: 400 });
  const query = favourite
    ? auth.client.from("favourite_foods").upsert({ owner_id: auth.user.id, food_id: foodId })
    : auth.client.from("favourite_foods").delete().eq("owner_id", auth.user.id).eq("food_id", foodId);
  const { error } = await query;
  return error ? NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 }) : new NextResponse(null, { status: 204 });
}

src/app/api/account/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const serviceKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  if (!serviceKey) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Account deletion is not configured on this deployment." }, { status: 501 });
  const admin = createClient(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!, serviceKey, { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } });
  const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(auth.user.id);
  return error ? NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 }) : new NextResponse(null, { status: 204 });
}
Database: run in the Supabase SQL editor

Also save as supabase/migrations/202611100021_adjustments_products.sql.

sql
-- 1. Replace an entry's adjustments and its final totals in one transaction.
create or replace function public.replace_log_adjustments(
  p_log_id uuid, p_adjustments jsonb, p_final_totals jsonb, p_completeness jsonb
) returns integer
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
declare new_version integer;
begin
  select snapshot_version + 1 into new_version from food_log_nutrients where log_id = p_log_id;
  if new_version is null then
    raise exception 'Entry not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  delete from food_log_adjustments where log_id = p_log_id;
  insert into food_log_adjustments
    (log_id, kind, accuracy_class, label, nutrient_changes, assumption, position, basis, scales_with_quantity, snapshot_version)
  select p_log_id, a->>'kind', a->>'accuracy_class', a->>'label', coalesce(a->'nutrient_changes', '{}'::jsonb),
         a->>'assumption', (ord - 1)::int, coalesce(a->>'basis', 'per_entry'),
         coalesce((a->>'scales_with_quantity')::boolean, false), new_version
  from jsonb_array_elements(p_adjustments) with ordinality as t(a, ord);
  update food_log_nutrients
     set final_totals = p_final_totals, completeness = p_completeness, snapshot_version = new_version
   where log_id = p_log_id;
  return new_version;
end
$$;

-- 2. People may save packaged products and barcodes for foods they own.
create policy "own product details write" on public.product_details for all
  using (exists (select 1 from public.foods f where f.id = food_id and f.owner_id = auth.uid()))
  with check (exists (select 1 from public.foods f where f.id = food_id and f.owner_id = auth.uid()));

create policy "own product barcodes write" on public.product_barcodes for all
  using (owner_id = auth.uid())
  with check (owner_id = auth.uid() and exists (select 1 from public.foods f where f.id = food_id and f.owner_id = auth.uid()));

New src/lib/adjustments.ts

ts
import type { Adjustment, AdjustmentKind, NutrientKey, Nutrients } from "@/lib/nutrition";

export const ADJUSTABLE: { key: NutrientKey; label: string; unit: string; max: number }[] = [
  { key: "kcal", label: "Calories", unit: "kcal", max: 5000 },
  { key: "proteinG", label: "Protein", unit: "g", max: 1000 },
  { key: "carbsG", label: "Carbs", unit: "g", max: 1000 },
  { key: "fatG", label: "Fat", unit: "g", max: 1000 },
  { key: "totalSugarsG", label: "Sugar", unit: "g", max: 1000 },
  { key: "sodiumMg", label: "Sodium", unit: "mg", max: 20000 },
];
export const MAX_ADJUSTMENTS = 12;
const KINDS: AdjustmentKind[] = ["measured_ingredient", "estimate_preset", "percent_of_base", "manual_delta", "set_unknown_value"];
const CLASSES = ["measured", "estimate", "manual"];
const round2 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
export const labelOf = (key: string) => ADJUSTABLE.find((item) => item.key === key)?.label ?? key;

export function badgeFor(adjustment: Adjustment): { text: "Measured" | "Estimate" | "Manual"; icon: string } {
  if (adjustment.accuracyClass === "measured") return { text: "Measured", icon: "⚖" };
  if (adjustment.accuracyClass === "estimate") return { text: "Estimate", icon: "≈" };
  return { text: "Manual", icon: "✎" };
}

/** "+40 kcal, +4.5 g fat" */
export function describeChange(changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>>): string {
  const parts = ADJUSTABLE.flatMap(({ key, label, unit }) => {
    const value = changes[key];
    if (value === undefined) return [];
    const sign = value > 0 ? "+" : value < 0 ? "−" : "";
    const amount = Math.abs(round2(value));
    return [unit === "kcal" ? `${sign}${amount} kcal` : `${sign}${amount} ${unit} ${label.toLowerCase()}`];
  });
  return parts.join(", ") || "No change";
}

export function completenessNotes(completeness: unknown): string[] {
  if (!completeness || typeof completeness !== "object") return [];
  const notes: string[] = [];
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(completeness as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    const flag = value as { notFullyAdjusted?: boolean; direction?: string } | null;
    if (flag && typeof flag === "object" && flag.notFullyAdjusted) {
      notes.push(`${labelOf(key)} ${flag.direction === "at_most" ? "may be lower" : "may be higher"} than shown: its starting value is unknown.`);
    }
  }
  return notes;
}

/** Calculates an ingredient's effect from its published per-100 g values. Unknown values are left out and listed. */
export function measuredFromFood(args: { name: string; per100g: Nutrients; grams: number; direction: 1 | -1; perUnit: boolean }): { adjustment: Adjustment; missing: string[] } | string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(args.grams) || args.grams <= 0 || args.grams > 2000) return "Enter an amount above 0 and up to 2000 g.";
  const changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>> = {};
  const missing: string[] = [];
  for (const { key, label } of ADJUSTABLE) {
    const value = args.per100g[key];
    if (value === null) missing.push(label.toLowerCase());
    else changes[key] = round2((args.direction * value * args.grams) / 100);
  }
  if (Object.keys(changes).length === 0) return "This food has no nutrition data to calculate from.";
  const assumption =
    `Calculated from the published per-100 g values for ${args.name}.` +
    (missing.length ? ` No data for ${missing.join(", ")}, so those totals are not adjusted.` : "");
  return {
    adjustment: {
      kind: "measured_ingredient",
      label: `${args.direction > 0 ? "+" : "−"} ${args.grams} g ${args.name}`,
      changes,
      basis: args.perUnit ? "per_unit" : "per_entry",
      accuracyClass: "measured",
      assumption,
    },
    missing,
  };
}

function readValues(values: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, string>>, direction: 1 | -1, only?: NutrientKey[]): Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>> | string {
  const changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>> = {};
  for (const { key, label, max } of ADJUSTABLE) {
    if (only && !only.includes(key)) continue;
    const text = (values[key] ?? "").trim().replace(",", ".");
    if (text === "") continue;
    const value = Number(text);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > max) return `${label} must be a number from 0 to ${max}.`;
    changes[key] = round2(direction * value);
  }
  return Object.keys(changes).length === 0 ? "Enter at least one value." : changes;
}

export type ManualForm = { label: string; direction: 1 | -1; values: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, string>>; perUnit: boolean };
export function manualFromForm(form: ManualForm): Adjustment | string {
  const label = form.label.trim();
  if (!label || label.length > 80) return "Describe the change in a few words.";
  const changes = readValues(form.values, form.direction);
  if (typeof changes === "string") return changes;
  return { kind: "manual_delta", label, changes, basis: form.perUnit ? "per_unit" : "per_entry", accuracyClass: "manual", assumption: "Entered by you." };
}

/** Fills only nutrients whose starting value is unknown. Known values are never overwritten. */
export function fillUnknownFromForm(values: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, string>>): Adjustment | string {
  const changes = readValues(values, 1);
  if (typeof changes === "string") return changes;
  return { kind: "set_unknown_value", label: "Your values for unknown nutrients", changes, basis: "per_entry", accuracyClass: "manual", assumption: "Entered by you." };
}

export function validateAdjustments(raw: unknown): Adjustment[] | string {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return "Adjustments must be a list.";
  if (raw.length > MAX_ADJUSTMENTS) return `You can add up to ${MAX_ADJUSTMENTS} adjustments to one entry.`;
  const result: Adjustment[] = [];
  for (const item of raw) {
    const entry = item as Record<string, unknown> | null;
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return "Invalid adjustment.";
    const label = typeof entry.label === "string" ? entry.label.trim() : "";
    if (!label || label.length > 120) return "Each adjustment needs a short label.";
    if (!KINDS.includes(entry.kind as AdjustmentKind)) return "Unknown adjustment type.";
    const accuracy = (entry.accuracyClass ?? "manual") as string;
    if (!CLASSES.includes(accuracy)) return "Unknown accuracy class.";
    if (typeof entry.changes !== "object" || entry.changes === null) return "Invalid changes.";
    const changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>> = {};
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entry.changes as Record<string, unknown>)) {
      const limit = ADJUSTABLE.find((candidate) => candidate.key === key);
      if (!limit) return `${key} cannot be adjusted.`;
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || Math.abs(value) > limit.max * 2) return `Invalid value for ${limit.label}.`;
      changes[key as NutrientKey] = value;
    }
    if (Object.keys(changes).length === 0) return "An adjustment needs at least one change.";
    const assumption = typeof entry.assumption === "string" && entry.assumption.trim() ? entry.assumption.trim().slice(0, 300) : undefined;
    result.push({
      kind: entry.kind as AdjustmentKind,
      label,
      changes,
      basis: entry.basis === "per_unit" ? "per_unit" : "per_entry",
      accuracyClass: accuracy as Adjustment["accuracyClass"],
      scalesWithQuantity: entry.scalesWithQuantity === true,
      assumption,
    });
  }
  return result;
}

export type AdjustmentRow = {
  kind: AdjustmentKind;
  accuracy_class: "measured" | "estimate" | "manual";
  label: string;
  nutrient_changes: Partial<Record<NutrientKey, number>>;
  assumption: string | null;
  basis: "per_entry" | "per_unit";
  scales_with_quantity: boolean;
};
export const rowToAdjustment = (row: AdjustmentRow): Adjustment => ({
  kind: row.kind, label: row.label, changes: row.nutrient_changes, basis: row.basis,
  scalesWithQuantity: row.scales_with_quantity, accuracyClass: row.accuracy_class, assumption: row.assumption ?? undefined,
});
export const adjustmentToRow = (adjustment: Adjustment) => ({
  kind: adjustment.kind,
  accuracy_class: adjustment.accuracyClass ?? "manual",
  label: adjustment.label,
  nutrient_changes: adjustment.changes,
  assumption: adjustment.assumption ?? null,
  basis: adjustment.basis ?? "per_entry",
  scales_with_quantity: adjustment.scalesWithQuantity ?? false,
});

New src/lib/adjustments.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { describeChange, fillUnknownFromForm, manualFromForm, measuredFromFood, validateAdjustments } from "./adjustments";
import { emptyNutrients } from "./nutrition";

// Fixture numbers only exercise the rules; they are not nutrition data.
const ghee = { ...emptyNutrients(), kcal: 900, fatG: 100, proteinG: 0, carbsG: 0 };

describe("adjustments", () => {
  it("calculates a measured ingredient from per-100 g values and lists what it lacks", () => {
    const result = measuredFromFood({ name: "Ghee", per100g: ghee, grams: 5, direction: 1, perUnit: false });
    if (typeof result === "string") throw new Error(result);
    expect(result.adjustment.changes).toMatchObject({ kcal: 45, fatG: 5, proteinG: 0 });
    expect(result.adjustment.changes.sodiumMg).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result.missing).toContain("sodium");
    expect(result.adjustment.assumption).toContain("No data for");
  });
  it("makes the change negative when something is left out", () => {
    const result = measuredFromFood({ name: "Ghee", per100g: ghee, grams: 10, direction: -1, perUnit: true });
    if (typeof result === "string") throw new Error(result);
    expect(result.adjustment.changes.kcal).toBe(-90);
    expect(result.adjustment.basis).toBe("per_unit");
  });
  it("rejects nonsense amounts", () => {
    expect(typeof measuredFromFood({ name: "Ghee", per100g: ghee, grams: 0, direction: 1, perUnit: false })).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof measuredFromFood({ name: "Ghee", per100g: emptyNutrients(), grams: 5, direction: 1, perUnit: false })).toBe("string");
  });
  it("builds manual and fill-unknown adjustments, blank meaning no change", () => {
    const manual = manualFromForm({ label: "Extra ghee, my guess", direction: 1, values: { kcal: "80", fatG: "" }, perUnit: false });
    expect(typeof manual === "string" ? manual : manual.changes).toEqual({ kcal: 80 });
    expect(typeof manualFromForm({ label: "", direction: 1, values: { kcal: "5" }, perUnit: false })).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof manualFromForm({ label: "x", direction: 1, values: {}, perUnit: false })).toBe("string");
    const fill = fillUnknownFromForm({ sodiumMg: "300" });
    expect(typeof fill === "string" ? fill : fill.kind).toBe("set_unknown_value");
  });
  it("validates adjustments on the server side", () => {
    expect(typeof validateAdjustments("nope")).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof validateAdjustments([{ kind: "manual_delta", label: "x", changes: { fibreG: 2 } }])).toBe("string");
    expect(typeof validateAdjustments([{ kind: "manual_delta", label: "x", changes: { kcal: 1e9 } }])).toBe("string");
    const ok = validateAdjustments([{ kind: "manual_delta", label: "Extra", changes: { kcal: 50 } }]);
    expect(Array.isArray(ok) && ok[0].accuracyClass).toBe("manual");
  });
  it("describes changes plainly", () => {
    expect(describeChange({ kcal: 45, fatG: 5, sodiumMg: -20 })).toBe("+45 kcal, +5 g fat, −20 mg sodium");
  });
});
P6: API routes

Replace src/app/api/logs/adjustments/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { adjustmentToRow, validateAdjustments } from "@/lib/adjustments";
import { calculateAdjusted, type Nutrients } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { completenessOf } from "@/lib/server/logging";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const bad = (message: string, status = 400) => NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status });

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  let body: { logId?: unknown; adjustments?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return bad("Invalid adjustment request.");
  }
  if (typeof body.logId !== "string") return bad("Invalid adjustment request.");
  const adjustments = validateAdjustments(body.adjustments);
  if (typeof adjustments === "string") return bad(adjustments);

  const { data: log } = await auth.client.from("food_logs").select("quantity,food_log_nutrients(base_snapshot)").eq("id", body.logId).single();
  const embed = log ? (Array.isArray(log.food_log_nutrients) ? log.food_log_nutrients[0] : log.food_log_nutrients) : null;
  const base = embed?.base_snapshot as Nutrients | undefined;
  if (!log || !base) return bad("Entry not found.", 404);

  const result = calculateAdjusted(base, Number(log.quantity), adjustments);
  const completeness = { ...completenessOf(result.totals), ...result.completeness };
  const { error } = await auth.client.rpc("replace_log_adjustments", {
    p_log_id: body.logId,
    p_adjustments: adjustments.map(adjustmentToRow),
    p_final_totals: result.totals,
    p_completeness: completeness,
  });
  if (error) return bad(error.message);
  return NextResponse.json({ totals: result.totals, completeness });
}

Replace src/app/api/logs/tags/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const PERMITTED = new Set(["junk", "homemade", "sugary_junk", "fried", "sweet", "sugary_drink", "packaged_snack", "high_sodium"]);
const bad = (message: string) => NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 400 });

export async function PUT(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  let body: { logId?: unknown; tags?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return bad("Invalid tags.");
  }
  const tags = body.tags;
  if (typeof body.logId !== "string" || !Array.isArray(tags) || tags.length > 8 || tags.some((tag) => typeof tag !== "string" || !PERMITTED.has(tag))) return bad("Invalid tags.");
  const unique = [...new Set(tags as string[])];

  if (unique.length) {
    const { error } = await auth.client.from("food_log_tags").upsert(unique.map((tag) => ({ log_id: body.logId, tag, source: "user" })), { onConflict: "log_id,tag" });
    if (error) return bad(error.message);
  }
  let remove = auth.client.from("food_log_tags").delete().eq("log_id", body.logId as string);
  if (unique.length) remove = remove.not("tag", "in", `(${unique.join(",")})`);
  const { error: removeError } = await remove;
  if (removeError) return bad(removeError.message);
  return NextResponse.json({ tags: unique });
}

Replace src/app/api/versions/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { validateAdjustments } from "@/lib/adjustments";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const bad = (message: string, status = 400) => NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status });

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  let body: { baseFoodId?: unknown; name?: unknown; adjustments?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return bad("Invalid version.");
  }
  const name = typeof body.name === "string" ? body.name.trim() : "";
  if (typeof body.baseFoodId !== "string" || !name || name.length > 120) return bad("Give the version a name of up to 120 characters.");
  const adjustments = validateAdjustments(body.adjustments);
  if (typeof adjustments === "string") return bad(adjustments);
  if (adjustments.length === 0) return bad("Add at least one adjustment before saving a version.");
  const { data, error } = await auth.client.from("my_versions").insert({ owner_id: auth.user.id, base_food_id: body.baseFoodId, name, adjustments }).select("id").single();
  if (error?.code === "23505") return bad("You already have a version with that name.", 409);
  if (error || !data) return bad(error?.message ?? "Could not save the version.");
  return NextResponse.json({ id: data.id }, { status: 201 });
}

export async function DELETE(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return bad("Unauthorized", 401);
  const id = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("id");
  if (!id) return bad("A version id is required.");
  const { error } = await auth.client.from("my_versions").delete().eq("id", id).eq("owner_id", auth.user.id);
  return error ? bad(error.message) : new NextResponse(null, { status: 204 });
}

Replace src/app/api/search/route.ts (adds My versions)

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { validateAdjustments } from "@/lib/adjustments";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

type Hit = { found_food_id: string; matched_alias: string; match_quality: "exact" | "approximate" };
type VersionRow = { id: string; name: string; adjustments: unknown; base_food_id: string };

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const query = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("q")?.trim();
  if (!query || query.length < 2 || query.length > 80) return NextResponse.json({ foods: [], versions: [] });

  const escaped = query.replace(/[\\%_]/g, "\\$&");
  const [hitResult, versionResult] = await Promise.all([
    auth.client.rpc("search_foods", { q: query, max_results: 30 }),
    auth.client.from("my_versions").select("id,name,adjustments,base_food_id").ilike("name", `%${escaped}%`).limit(5),
  ]);
  if (hitResult.error) return NextResponse.json({ error: hitResult.error.message }, { status: 400 });
  const hits = (hitResult.data ?? []) as Hit[];
  const versionRows = (versionResult.data ?? []) as VersionRow[];

  const ids = [...new Set([...hits.map((hit) => hit.found_food_id), ...versionRows.map((row) => row.base_food_id)])];
  if (!ids.length) return NextResponse.json({ foods: [], versions: [] });
  const { data: foods, error } = await auth.client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).in("id", ids);
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  const byId = new Map(((foods ?? []) as unknown as { id: string }[]).map((food) => [food.id, food]));

  return NextResponse.json({
    foods: hits.flatMap((hit) => {
      const food = byId.get(hit.found_food_id);
      return food ? [{ ...food, matched_alias: hit.matched_alias, match_quality: hit.match_quality }] : [];
    }),
    versions: versionRows.flatMap((row) => {
      const food = byId.get(row.base_food_id);
      const adjustments = validateAdjustments(row.adjustments);
      return food && Array.isArray(adjustments) ? [{ id: row.id, name: row.name, adjustments, food }] : [];
    }),
  });
}
P6: components

Replace src/components/food/PortionPicker.tsx (can preview a saved version’s changes)

tsx
"use client";

import { calculateAdjusted, type Adjustment } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import {
  availableUnits, formatQuantity, minQuantity, parseQuantity, previewPortion, quantityChips, quantityStep, servingNameFor,
  type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit,
} from "@/lib/units";

type Props = {
  food: FoodSummary;
  unit: PortionUnit;
  amount: string;
  adjustments?: Adjustment[];
  onUnitChange: (unit: PortionUnit) => void;
  onAmountChange: (amount: string) => void;
};

export function PortionPicker({ food, unit, amount, adjustments, onUnitChange, onAmountChange }: Props) {
  const units = availableUnits(food);
  const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
  const base = quantity ? previewPortion(food, unit, quantity) : null;
  const shown = base?.ok ? (adjustments?.length ? calculateAdjusted(base.nutrients, quantity as number, adjustments).totals : base.nutrients) : null;

  function nudge(direction: 1 | -1) {
    const current = parseQuantity(amount) ?? 0;
    onAmountChange(String(Math.max(minQuantity(unit), Math.round((current + direction * quantityStep(unit)) * 100) / 100)));
  }

  if (units.length === 0) return <p className="nutrition-note">This food has no usable nutrition data, so it can’t be logged.</p>;

  return (
    <>
      {units.length > 1 && (
        <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Unit">
          {units.map((option) => (
            <button
              key={option}
              className={`ax-chip ${unit === option ? "selected" : ""}`}
              aria-pressed={unit === option}
              onClick={() => {
                onUnitChange(option);
                onAmountChange(option === "g" ? "100" : "1");
              }}
            >
              {option === "g" ? "grams" : servingNameFor(food)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <fieldset>
        <legend>{unit === "g" ? "Amount (g)" : `How many ${servingNameFor(food)}?`}</legend>
        <div className="ax-chips">
          {quantityChips(unit).map((value) => (
            <button key={value} className={`ax-chip ${quantity === value ? "selected" : ""}`} onClick={() => onAmountChange(String(value))}>
              {unit === "g" ? `${value} g` : formatQuantity(value)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="quantity-stepper">
          <button onClick={() => nudge(-1)} aria-label="Decrease amount">−</button>
          <input value={amount} inputMode="decimal" onChange={(event) => onAmountChange(event.target.value)} aria-label="Amount" />
          <button onClick={() => nudge(1)} aria-label="Increase amount">+</button>
        </div>
      </fieldset>
      {shown ? (
        <section className="nutrition-preview" aria-live="polite">
          <h3>Nutrition for this portion{adjustments?.length ? " (with your version)" : ""}</h3>
          <div className="nutrition-grid">
            {([["Calories", shown.kcal, "kcal"], ["Protein", shown.proteinG, "g"], ["Carbs", shown.carbsG, "g"], ["Fat", shown.fatG, "g"]] as const).map(([name, value, suffix]) => (
              <div key={name}><span>{name}</span><strong>{value === null ? "—" : `${Math.round(value * 10) / 10}${suffix}`}</strong></div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {shown.kcal === null && <p className="muted">Calories aren’t available for this food. It is logged as unknown, not zero.</p>}
        </section>
      ) : (
        <p className="nutrition-note">{quantity ? "Nutrition isn’t available for this amount." : "Enter an amount above zero."}</p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

New src/components/dashboard/AdjustSheet.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  ADJUSTABLE, badgeFor, completenessNotes, describeChange, fillUnknownFromForm, manualFromForm, measuredFromFood, rowToAdjustment,
  type AdjustmentRow,
} from "@/lib/adjustments";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import type { Adjustment, NutrientKey, Nutrients } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { rowToNutrients, type FoodSummary } from "@/lib/units";

type Mode = "list" | "ingredient" | "manual" | "fill";
type Loaded = { foodId: string | null; name: string; unit: string; base: Nutrients; totals: Nutrients; completeness: unknown };
type Props = { logId: string; startMode?: Mode; onClose: (changed: boolean) => void };

const fmt = (value: number | null) => (value === null ? "—" : String(Math.round(value * 10) / 10));

export function AdjustSheet({ logId, startMode = "list", onClose }: Props) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<Loaded | null>(null);
  const [adjustments, setAdjustments] = useState<Adjustment[]>([]);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>(startMode);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const changed = useRef(false);

  // ingredient mode
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<FoodSummary[]>([]);
  const [ingredient, setIngredient] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [grams, setGrams] = useState("10");
  const [direction, setDirection] = useState<1 | -1>(1);
  const [perUnit, setPerUnit] = useState(false);
  // manual and fill modes
  const [label, setLabel] = useState("");
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Partial<Record<NutrientKey, string>>>({});
  const [versionName, setVersionName] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const db = supabase();
    void (async () => {
      const [logResult, nutrientResult, rowResult] = await Promise.all([
        db.from("food_logs").select("food_id,name_snapshot,unit").eq("id", logId).single(),
        db.from("food_log_nutrients").select("base_snapshot,final_totals,completeness").eq("log_id", logId).single(),
        db.from("food_log_adjustments").select("kind,accuracy_class,label,nutrient_changes,assumption,basis,scales_with_quantity").eq("log_id", logId).order("position"),
      ]);
      if (logResult.error || nutrientResult.error || !logResult.data || !nutrientResult.data) {
        setError("This entry couldn’t be loaded.");
        return;
      }
      setLoaded({
        foodId: logResult.data.food_id as string | null,
        name: logResult.data.name_snapshot as string,
        unit: logResult.data.unit as string,
        base: nutrientResult.data.base_snapshot as Nutrients,
        totals: nutrientResult.data.final_totals as Nutrients,
        completeness: nutrientResult.data.completeness,
      });
      setAdjustments(((rowResult.data ?? []) as AdjustmentRow[]).map(rowToAdjustment));
    })();
  }, [logId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    if (mode !== "ingredient" || trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      apiFetch<{ foods: FoodSummary[] }>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`, { signal: controller.signal })
        .then((data) => setResults(data.foods.filter((food) => food.serving_mode !== "count" && food.food_nutrients?.some((row) => row.basis === "per_100g"))))
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query, mode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => event.key === "Escape" && onClose(changed.current);
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  function openMode(next: Mode) {
    setMode(next);
    setError("");
    setMessage("");
    setLabel("");
    setValues({});
  }

  async function persist(next: Adjustment[]) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await apiFetch<{ totals: Nutrients; completeness: unknown }>("/api/logs/adjustments", { method: "POST", json: { logId, adjustments: next } });
      setAdjustments(next);
      setLoaded((old) => (old ? { ...old, totals: result.totals, completeness: result.completeness } : old));
      changed.current = true;
      setIngredient(null);
      setQuery("");
      setMode("list");
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That change couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function addIngredient() {
    const row = ingredient?.food_nutrients?.find((item) => item.basis === "per_100g");
    if (!ingredient || !row) return;
    const built = measuredFromFood({ name: ingredient.canonical_name, per100g: rowToNutrients(row), grams: Number(grams.replace(",", ".")), direction, perUnit });
    if (typeof built === "string") return setError(built);
    void persist([...adjustments, built.adjustment]);
  }
  function addManual() {
    const built = manualFromForm({ label, direction, values, perUnit });
    if (typeof built === "string") return setError(built);
    void persist([...adjustments, built]);
  }
  function addFill() {
    const built = fillUnknownFromForm(values);
    if (typeof built === "string") return setError(built);
    void persist([...adjustments, built]);
  }
  async function saveVersion() {
    if (!loaded?.foodId) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/versions", { method: "POST", json: { baseFoodId: loaded.foodId, name: versionName, adjustments } });
      setMessage(`Saved as “${versionName.trim()}”. Search for it when you add food.`);
      setVersionName("");
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That version couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const unknownKeys = loaded ? ADJUSTABLE.filter(({ key }) => loaded.base[key] === null) : [];
  const ingredientRow = ingredient?.food_nutrients?.find((item) => item.basis === "per_100g");
  const preview = ingredient && ingredientRow ? measuredFromFood({ name: ingredient.canonical_name, per100g: rowToNutrients(ingredientRow), grams: Number(grams.replace(",", ".")), direction, perUnit }) : null;
  const eachAllowed = loaded ? loaded.unit !== "g" : false;
  const valueFields = (keys: { key: NutrientKey; label: string; unit: string }[]) =>
    keys.map(({ key, label: name, unit }) => (
      <label key={key}>{name} ({unit})
        <input inputMode="decimal" value={values[key] ?? ""} onChange={(event) => setValues((old) => ({ ...old, [key]: event.target.value }))} />
      </label>
    ));
  const directionChips = (
    <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Add or remove">
      <button className={`ax-chip ${direction === 1 ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={direction === 1} onClick={() => setDirection(1)}>Added</button>
      <button className={`ax-chip ${direction === -1 ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={direction === -1} onClick={() => setDirection(-1)}>Left out</button>
    </div>
  );
  const perUnitToggle = eachAllowed && (
    <label className="ax-check"><input type="checkbox" checked={perUnit} onChange={(event) => setPerUnit(event.target.checked)} /> Applies to each {loaded?.unit}, not just once</label>
  );

  return (
    <div className="ax-sheet-backdrop ax-top" onClick={() => onClose(changed.current)}>
      <div className="ax-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Adjust this entry" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="ax-sheet-head">
          <h2>Adjust {loaded?.name ?? "entry"}</h2>
          <button onClick={() => onClose(changed.current)} aria-label="Close">×</button>
        </div>

        {!loaded ? (
          error ? <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /></div>
        ) : mode === "list" ? (
          <>
            <div className="ax-totals">
              <div className="tx-macro"><span>Published values</span><strong>{fmt(loaded.base.kcal)} kcal</strong></div>
              <div className="tx-macro"><span>With your changes</span><strong>{fmt(loaded.totals.kcal)} kcal</strong></div>
            </div>
            {completenessNotes(loaded.completeness).map((note) => <p className="ax-note" key={note}>{note}</p>)}
            {adjustments.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="muted">No changes yet. The published values are used as they are.</p>
            ) : (
              adjustments.map((adjustment, index) => {
                const badge = badgeFor(adjustment);
                return (
                  <div className="ax-adj-row" key={`${adjustment.label}-${index}`}>
                    <span><span className={`ax-badge ${badge.text.toLowerCase()}`}><span aria-hidden="true">{badge.icon}</span> {badge.text}</span> <b>{adjustment.label}</b></span>
                    <small>{describeChange(adjustment.changes)}{adjustment.basis === "per_unit" ? ` per ${loaded.unit}` : ""}</small>
                    {adjustment.assumption && <small>{adjustment.assumption}</small>}
                    <button className="text-link" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void persist(adjustments.filter((_, position) => position !== index))}>Remove</button>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
            <div className="ax-stack">
              <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => openMode("ingredient")}>Add or leave out an ingredient</button>
              <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => openMode("manual")}>Enter my own change</button>
              {unknownKeys.length > 0 && <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => openMode("fill")}>Fill in unknown values</button>}
            </div>
            {adjustments.length > 0 && loaded.foodId && (
              <div className="ax-stack">
                <label>Save these changes as My Version
                  <input value={versionName} onChange={(event) => setVersionName(event.target.value)} maxLength={120} placeholder="For example, Poha with extra ghee" />
                </label>
                <button className="button secondary" disabled={busy || !versionName.trim()} onClick={() => void saveVersion()}>Save My Version</button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        ) : mode === "ingredient" ? (
          <>
            <button className="text-link" onClick={() => openMode("list")}>← Back</button>
            {!ingredient ? (
              <>
                <label>Search for an ingredient
                  <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Try ghee, oil, sugar, curd…" autoComplete="off" />
                </label>
                <div className="search-results">
                  {results.map((food) => (
                    <button className="search-result" key={food.id} onClick={() => setIngredient(food)}>
                      <span><b>{food.canonical_name}</b></span><span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
                {query.trim().length >= 2 && results.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Nothing with per-100 g data matches. You can enter your own change instead.</p>}
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <h3>{ingredient.canonical_name}</h3>
                {directionChips}
                <label>Amount (g)<input inputMode="decimal" value={grams} onChange={(event) => setGrams(event.target.value)} /></label>
                {perUnitToggle}
                {preview && typeof preview !== "string" ? (
                  <>
                    <p><span className="ax-badge measured"><span aria-hidden="true">⚖</span> Measured</span> {describeChange(preview.adjustment.changes)}</p>
                    {preview.missing.length > 0 && <p className="ax-note">No data for {preview.missing.join(", ")}; those totals are not adjusted.</p>}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  typeof preview === "string" && <p className="muted">{preview}</p>
                )}
                <div className="install-actions">
                  <button className="button primary" disabled={busy} onClick={addIngredient}>{busy ? "Saving…" : "Add to this entry"}</button>
                  <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setIngredient(null)}>Choose another</button>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </>
        ) : mode === "manual" ? (
          <>
            <button className="text-link" onClick={() => openMode("list")}>← Back</button>
            <p className="muted">Use this when you know roughly what changed. It is labelled Manual, so it is never mistaken for a measured value.</p>
            <label>What changed?<input value={label} onChange={(event) => setLabel(event.target.value)} maxLength={80} placeholder="For example, extra ghee on top" /></label>
            {directionChips}
            {valueFields(ADJUSTABLE)}
            {perUnitToggle}
            <button className="button primary" disabled={busy} onClick={addManual}>{busy ? "Saving…" : "Add to this entry"}</button>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <button className="text-link" onClick={() => openMode("list")}>← Back</button>
            <p className="muted">These values are unknown for this food. Fill in only what you know. Values that are already known are never overwritten.</p>
            {valueFields(unknownKeys)}
            <button className="button primary" disabled={busy} onClick={addFill}>{busy ? "Saving…" : "Save my values"}</button>
          </>
        )}

        {message && <p className="success-note" role="status">{message}</p>}
        {error && loaded && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Replace src/components/dashboard/EditEntrySheet.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AdjustSheet } from "@/components/dashboard/AdjustSheet";
import { PortionPicker } from "@/components/food/PortionPicker";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localTime, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "@/lib/dates";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { MEALS, type Meal } from "@/lib/meals";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { USER_TAGS } from "@/lib/tags";
import { availableUnits, parseQuantity, type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit } from "@/lib/units";

export type EditableLog = {
  id: string;
  food_id: string | null;
  name_snapshot: string;
  quantity: number;
  unit: string;
  meal_type: string;
  consumed_at: string;
  local_date: string;
};

type Props = { log: EditableLog; timeZone: string; onClose: () => void; onSaved: () => void; onRemove: () => void };

export function EditEntrySheet({ log, timeZone, onClose, onSaved, onRemove }: Props) {
  const [food, setFood] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(Boolean(log.food_id));
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<PortionUnit>(log.unit === "g" ? "g" : "serving");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(String(Number(log.quantity)));
  const [meal, setMeal] = useState<Meal>((MEALS as readonly string[]).includes(log.meal_type) ? (log.meal_type as Meal) : "snack");
  const [date, setDate] = useState(log.local_date);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(localTime(timeZone, new Date(log.consumed_at)));
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [adjusting, setAdjusting] = useState<null | "list" | "fill">(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const changed = useRef(false);

  const close = () => (changed.current ? onSaved() : onClose());

  useEffect(() => {
    const db = supabase();
    if (log.food_id) {
      db.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).eq("id", log.food_id).single().then(({ data }) => {
        setFood(data ? (data as unknown as FoodSummary) : null);
        setLoading(false);
      });
    }
    db.from("food_log_tags").select("tag").eq("log_id", log.id).then(({ data }) => setTags((data ?? []).map((row) => row.tag as string)));
  }, [log.food_id, log.id]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => event.key === "Escape" && !adjusting && (changed.current ? onSaved() : onClose());
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [adjusting, onClose, onSaved]);

  const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
  const canSave = Boolean(food) && availableUnits(food ?? ({} as FoodSummary)).length > 0 && quantity !== null && Boolean(date) && Boolean(time);

  async function save() {
    if (!food || quantity === null) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/logs", {
        method: "PATCH",
        json: { id: log.id, quantity, unit, mealType: meal, consumedAt: zonedDateTimeToInstant(date, time, timeZone).toISOString() },
      });
      onSaved();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "Your changes couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function toggleTag(tag: string) {
    const next = tags.includes(tag) ? tags.filter((item) => item !== tag) : [...tags, tag];
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/logs/tags", { method: "PUT", json: { logId: log.id, tags: next } });
      setTags(next);
      changed.current = true;
    } catch {
      setError("That tag couldn’t be saved.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="ax-sheet-backdrop" onClick={close}>
      <div className="ax-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`Edit ${log.name_snapshot}`} onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="ax-sheet-head">
          <h2>{log.name_snapshot}</h2>
          <button onClick={close} aria-label="Close">×</button>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /></div>
        ) : food ? (
          <PortionPicker food={food} unit={unit} amount={amount} onUnitChange={setUnit} onAmountChange={setAmount} />
        ) : (
          <p className="nutrition-note">This food is no longer in the catalogue, so its amount can’t be changed. You can still adjust, tag or remove the entry.</p>
        )}

        {food && (
          <>
            <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Meal">
              {MEALS.map((item) => (
                <button key={item} className={`ax-chip ${meal === item ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={meal === item} onClick={() => setMeal(item)}>{item}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="ax-when-fields">
              <label>Date<input type="date" value={date} onChange={(event) => setDate(event.target.value)} /></label>
              <label>Time<input type="time" value={time} onChange={(event) => setTime(event.target.value)} /></label>
            </div>
            <p className="muted">Saving re-calculates this entry from the food’s current values, keeping any adjustments.</p>
          </>
        )}

        <div>
          <p className="muted">Tags (they never change calories)</p>
          <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Tags">
            {USER_TAGS.map(([value, name]) => (
              <button key={value} className={`ax-chip ${tags.includes(value) ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={tags.includes(value)} onClick={() => void toggleTag(value)}>{name}</button>
            ))}
          </div>
          {(tags.includes("junk") || tags.includes("sugary_junk")) && (
            <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setAdjusting("fill")}>Add sugar or sodium if you know them</button>
          )}
        </div>

        {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
        <div className="install-actions">
          {food && <button className="button primary" disabled={!canSave || saving} onClick={() => void save()}>{saving ? "Saving…" : "Save changes"}</button>}
          <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setAdjusting("list")}>Adjust this entry</button>
          <button className="button secondary" onClick={onRemove}>Remove entry</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {adjusting && (
        <AdjustSheet
          logId={log.id}
          startMode={adjusting}
          onClose={(didChange) => {
            if (didChange) changed.current = true;
            setAdjusting(null);
            if (didChange) onSaved();
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

Closing the Adjust sheet after a change reloads Today, so the new totals show straight away.

New src/components/food/MyVersionsSection.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { describeChange } from "@/lib/adjustments";
import type { Adjustment } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

type Version = { id: string; name: string; base: string; adjustments: Adjustment[] };
type Row = { id: string; name: string; adjustments: Adjustment[]; foods: { canonical_name: string } | { canonical_name: string }[] | null };

export function MyVersionsSection() {
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<Version[]>([]);
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase().from("my_versions").select("id,name,adjustments,foods(canonical_name)").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
    setVersions(
      ((data ?? []) as unknown as Row[]).map((row) => {
        const food = Array.isArray(row.foods) ? row.foods[0] : row.foods;
        return { id: row.id, name: row.name, base: food?.canonical_name ?? "a removed food", adjustments: row.adjustments ?? [] };
      }),
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  async function remove(version: Version) {
    if (confirming !== version.id) {
      setConfirming(version.id);
      return;
    }
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/versions?id=${encodeURIComponent(version.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setConfirming(null);
      await load();
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That version couldn’t be deleted.");
    }
  }

  if (versions.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <section className="ax-section">
      <h2>My versions</h2>
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {versions.map((version) => (
        <div className="tx-meal" key={version.id}>
          <div className="tx-meal-head">
            <div>
              <h3>{version.name}</h3>
              <small>Based on {version.base}: {version.adjustments.map((item) => `${item.label} (${describeChange(item.changes)})`).join("; ")}</small>
            </div>
            <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => void remove(version)}>{confirming === version.id ? "Tap again to delete" : "Delete"}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

Deleting a version never changes entries you already logged with it.

P7: barcode

New src/lib/off.ts

ts
export type Per100g = {
  kcal: number | null; proteinG: number | null; carbsG: number | null; fatG: number | null;
  satFatG: number | null; fibreG: number | null; sugarG: number | null; sodiumMg: number | null;
};
export type ParsedProduct = { barcode: string; name: string; brand: string | null; servingGrams: number | null; per100g: Per100g };

/** EAN-8, UPC-A (12), EAN-13 and ITF-14 are all digits only. */
export const isValidBarcode = (code: string) => /^\d{8,14}$/.test(code);
const round2 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;

/** Reads an Open Food Facts product response. Anything missing stays null, never zero. */
export function parseOffProduct(barcode: string, json: unknown): ParsedProduct | null {
  const root = json as { status?: number; product?: Record<string, unknown> } | null;
  if (!root || root.status === 0 || !root.product) return null;
  const product = root.product;
  const nutriments = (product.nutriments ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const text = (value: unknown) => (typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null);
  const number = (key: string): number | null => {
    const raw = nutriments[key];
    const value = typeof raw === "number" ? raw : typeof raw === "string" && raw.trim() !== "" ? Number(raw) : NaN;
    return Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 ? value : null;
  };
  const name = text(product.product_name) ?? text(product.product_name_en) ?? text(product.generic_name);
  if (!name) return null;

  const kcalDirect = number("energy-kcal_100g");
  const kilojoules = number("energy_100g");
  const kcal = kcalDirect !== null ? kcalDirect : kilojoules !== null ? kilojoules / 4.184 : null;
  const sodiumGrams = number("sodium_100g");
  const saltGrams = number("salt_100g");
  const sodiumMg = sodiumGrams !== null ? sodiumGrams * 1000 : saltGrams !== null ? saltGrams * 400 : null; // salt is 40% sodium
  const round = (value: number | null) => (value === null ? null : round2(value));

  const servingRaw = Number(product.serving_quantity);
  const unit = text(product.serving_quantity_unit);
  const servingGrams = Number.isFinite(servingRaw) && servingRaw > 0 && (unit === null || unit === "g") ? servingRaw : null;

  return {
    barcode,
    name,
    brand: text(product.brands)?.split(",")[0].trim() ?? null,
    servingGrams,
    per100g: {
      kcal: round(kcal), proteinG: round(number("proteins_100g")), carbsG: round(number("carbohydrates_100g")), fatG: round(number("fat_100g")),
      satFatG: round(number("saturated-fat_100g")), fibreG: round(number("fiber_100g")), sugarG: round(number("sugars_100g")), sodiumMg: round(sodiumMg),
    },
  };
}

New src/lib/off.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isValidBarcode, parseOffProduct } from "./off";

// Fixture values only exercise parsing and conversion; they are not nutrition data.
const fixture = {
  status: 1,
  product: {
    product_name: "Test biscuit", brands: "Brand A, Brand B", serving_quantity: "25",
    nutriments: { "energy-kcal_100g": 400, proteins_100g: 6, carbohydrates_100g: 70, fat_100g: 10, "saturated-fat_100g": 4, sugars_100g: 20, salt_100g: 1 },
  },
};

describe("Open Food Facts parsing", () => {
  it("reads values and keeps missing ones null, not zero", () => {
    const parsed = parseOffProduct("8901234567890", fixture);
    expect(parsed?.name).toBe("Test biscuit");
    expect(parsed?.brand).toBe("Brand A");
    expect(parsed?.servingGrams).toBe(25);
    expect(parsed?.per100g.kcal).toBe(400);
    expect(parsed?.per100g.fibreG).toBeNull();
  });
  it("converts salt to sodium and kilojoules to kcal", () => {
    expect(parseOffProduct("8901234567890", fixture)?.per100g.sodiumMg).toBe(400);
    const kj = parseOffProduct("8901234567890", { status: 1, product: { product_name: "X", nutriments: { energy_100g: 418.4 } } });
    expect(kj?.per100g.kcal).toBe(100);
  });
  it("returns null for unknown products or ones without a name", () => {
    expect(parseOffProduct("1", { status: 0 })).toBeNull();
    expect(parseOffProduct("1", { status: 1, product: { nutriments: {} } })).toBeNull();
  });
  it("accepts only digit barcodes of valid length", () => {
    expect(isValidBarcode("8901234567890")).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidBarcode("12345")).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidBarcode("89012345678a0")).toBe(false);
  });
});

New src/lib/product.ts

ts
import { isValidBarcode, type Per100g } from "@/lib/off";

export type Per = Per100g;
export type ProductInput = {
  barcode: string | null;
  name: string;
  brand: string | null;
  source: "off" | "manual";
  servingName: string | null;
  servingGrams: number | null;
  per100g: Per | null;
  perServing: Per | null;
  per100gDerived: boolean;
  perServingDerived: boolean;
  note: string | null;
};
export type ProductFormValues = {
  barcode: string; name: string; brand: string; source: "off" | "manual"; basis: "per100g" | "perServing";
  servingName: string; servingGrams: string;
  kcal: string; protein: string; carbs: string; fat: string; satFat: string; fibre: string; sugar: string; sodium: string; note: string;
};

const FIELDS: { key: keyof Per; form: keyof ProductFormValues; label: string; max: number }[] = [
  { key: "kcal", form: "kcal", label: "Calories", max: 5000 },
  { key: "proteinG", form: "protein", label: "Protein", max: 1000 },
  { key: "carbsG", form: "carbs", label: "Carbs", max: 1000 },
  { key: "fatG", form: "fat", label: "Fat", max: 1000 },
  { key: "satFatG", form: "satFat", label: "Saturated fat", max: 1000 },
  { key: "fibreG", form: "fibre", label: "Fibre", max: 1000 },
  { key: "sugarG", form: "sugar", label: "Sugar", max: 1000 },
  { key: "sodiumMg", form: "sodium", label: "Sodium", max: 40000 },
];
const round2 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
const scale = (per: Per, factor: number): Per =>
  Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(per).map(([key, value]) => [key, value === null ? null : round2((value as number) * factor)])) as Per;

function readPer(raw: unknown): Per | string | null {
  if (raw === null || raw === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof raw !== "object") return "Invalid nutrition values.";
  const source = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  const out = {} as Per;
  for (const { key, label, max } of FIELDS) {
    const value = source[key];
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") out[key] = null;
    else if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > max) return `${label} must be a number from 0 to ${max}.`;
    else out[key] = value;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Validates a product on the server. Returns an error message or the clean values. */
export function validateProductInput(raw: Record<string, unknown>): ProductInput | string {
  const name = typeof raw.name === "string" ? raw.name.trim() : "";
  if (!name || name.length > 120) return "Enter a product name of up to 120 characters.";
  const barcode = typeof raw.barcode === "string" && raw.barcode ? raw.barcode : null;
  if (barcode && !isValidBarcode(barcode)) return "That barcode isn’t valid.";
  const brand = typeof raw.brand === "string" && raw.brand.trim() ? raw.brand.trim().slice(0, 80) : null;
  const note = typeof raw.note === "string" && raw.note.trim() ? raw.note.trim().slice(0, 500) : null;
  const servingGrams = raw.servingGrams === null || raw.servingGrams === undefined ? null : Number(raw.servingGrams);
  if (servingGrams !== null && (!Number.isFinite(servingGrams) || servingGrams <= 0 || servingGrams > 5000)) return "Serving size must be above 0 and up to 5000 g.";
  const per100g = readPer(raw.per100g);
  const perServing = readPer(raw.perServing);
  if (typeof per100g === "string") return per100g;
  if (typeof perServing === "string") return perServing;
  if (!per100g && !perServing) return "Enter the nutrition values from the label.";
  if ((per100g && per100g.kcal === null) || (perServing && perServing.kcal === null)) return "Calories are required.";
  const servingName = typeof raw.servingName === "string" && raw.servingName.trim() ? raw.servingName.trim().slice(0, 40) : null;
  if (perServing && !servingName) return "Name the serving, such as serving or biscuit.";
  return {
    barcode, name, brand, note, servingGrams, per100g, perServing, servingName: perServing ? servingName : servingGrams ? servingName ?? "serving" : null,
    source: raw.source === "off" ? "off" : "manual",
    per100gDerived: raw.per100gDerived === true,
    perServingDerived: raw.perServingDerived === true,
  };
}

/** Turns the form into a product. Values that are worked out from the other basis are flagged as derived. */
export function buildProduct(form: ProductFormValues): { ok: true; value: ProductInput } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const values = {} as Per;
  for (const { key, form: formKey, label, max } of FIELDS) {
    const text = String(form[formKey]).trim().replace(",", ".");
    if (text === "") {
      values[key] = null;
      continue;
    }
    const value = Number(text);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > max) return { ok: false, error: `${label} must be a number from 0 to ${max}.` };
    values[key] = value;
  }
  if (values.kcal === null) return { ok: false, error: "Calories are required." };

  const gramsText = form.servingGrams.trim().replace(",", ".");
  const grams = gramsText === "" ? null : Number(gramsText);
  if (grams !== null && (!Number.isFinite(grams) || grams <= 0 || grams > 5000)) return { ok: false, error: "Serving size must be above 0 and up to 5000 g." };

  const servingName = form.servingName.trim() || "serving";
  let per100g: Per | null = null;
  let perServing: Per | null = null;
  let per100gDerived = false;
  let perServingDerived = false;
  if (form.basis === "per100g") {
    per100g = values;
    if (grams) {
      perServing = scale(values, grams / 100);
      perServingDerived = true;
    }
  } else {
    perServing = values;
    if (grams) {
      per100g = scale(values, 100 / grams);
      per100gDerived = true;
    }
  }
  const checked = validateProductInput({
    barcode: form.barcode || null, name: form.name, brand: form.brand, source: form.source, servingName: perServing ? servingName : null,
    servingGrams: grams, per100g, perServing, per100gDerived, perServingDerived,
    note: form.note || (form.source === "off" ? `Open Food Facts, barcode ${form.barcode}. Checked by you.` : "Entered from the product label."),
  });
  return typeof checked === "string" ? { ok: false, error: checked } : { ok: true, value: checked };
}

New src/lib/product.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildProduct, type ProductFormValues } from "./product";

// Fixture numbers only exercise the rules; they are not nutrition data.
const form: ProductFormValues = {
  barcode: "8901234567890", name: "Test biscuit", brand: "", source: "manual", basis: "per100g", servingName: "biscuit", servingGrams: "",
  kcal: "400", protein: "", carbs: "70", fat: "", satFat: "", fibre: "", sugar: "", sodium: "", note: "",
};

describe("packaged products", () => {
  it("keeps blank values unknown and a gram-only food when there is no serving size", () => {
    const result = buildProduct(form);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.per100g?.proteinG).toBeNull();
    expect(result.ok && result.value.perServing).toBeNull();
  });
  it("derives the per-serving values from per-100 g and flags them", () => {
    const result = buildProduct({ ...form, servingGrams: "25" });
    expect(result.ok && result.value.perServing?.kcal).toBe(100);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.perServingDerived).toBe(true);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.perServing?.proteinG).toBeNull();
  });
  it("derives per-100 g from per-serving values when the serving size is known", () => {
    const result = buildProduct({ ...form, basis: "perServing", kcal: "100", carbs: "17.5", servingGrams: "25" });
    expect(result.ok && result.value.per100g?.kcal).toBe(400);
    expect(result.ok && result.value.per100gDerived).toBe(true);
  });
  it("needs calories and a sane barcode", () => {
    expect(buildProduct({ ...form, kcal: "" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(buildProduct({ ...form, barcode: "12ab" }).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(buildProduct({ ...form, name: " " }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

New src/lib/server/products.ts

ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import type { Per, ProductInput } from "@/lib/product";

const nutrientColumns = (per: Per) => ({
  kcal: per.kcal, protein_g: per.proteinG, carbs_g: per.carbsG, fat_g: per.fatG,
  sat_fat_g: per.satFatG, fibre_g: per.fibreG, total_sugars_g: per.sugarG, sodium_mg: per.sodiumMg,
});

export async function loadFoodSummary(client: SupabaseClient, id: string) {
  const { data } = await client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).eq("id", id).single();
  return data ? (data as unknown as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
}

/** One packaged-food record: the food, its names, serving, nutrients, product details and barcode. */
export async function createProduct(client: SupabaseClient, userId: string, input: ProductInput): Promise<{ id: string } | string> {
  const servingMode = input.per100g && !input.perServing ? "grams_only" : "household";
  const { data: food, error } = await client
    .from("foods")
    .insert({ owner_id: userId, canonical_name: input.name, kind: "packaged", serving_mode: servingMode, source_kind: "label", user_note: input.note })
    .select("id")
    .single();
  if (error || !food) return error?.message ?? "Could not create the product.";
  const id = food.id as string;
  const fail = async (message: string) => {
    await client.from("foods").delete().eq("id", id); // removes everything attached to the half-made product
    return message;
  };

  const names = [input.name, ...(input.brand ? [`${input.brand} ${input.name}`] : [])];
  const aliases = await client.from("food_aliases").insert(names.map((alias) => ({ food_id: id, alias, language: "en", script: "latin", alias_type: "user", verified: true, match_quality: "exact" })));
  if (aliases.error) return fail(aliases.error.message);

  let servingId: string | null = null;
  if (input.servingName) {
    const serving = await client
      .from("food_servings")
      .insert({ food_id: id, name: input.servingName, unit_class: "household", grams: input.servingGrams, derived: input.perServingDerived, source: input.source === "off" ? "open_food_facts" : "user" })
      .select("id")
      .single();
    if (serving.error || !serving.data) return fail(serving.error?.message ?? "Could not create the serving.");
    servingId = serving.data.id as string;
  }

  const rows = [
    ...(input.per100g ? [{ food_id: id, basis: "per_100g", serving_id: null, confidence: input.per100gDerived ? "derived" : "label", ...nutrientColumns(input.per100g) }] : []),
    ...(input.perServing && servingId ? [{ food_id: id, basis: "per_serving", serving_id: servingId, confidence: input.perServingDerived ? "derived" : "label", ...nutrientColumns(input.perServing) }] : []),
  ];
  const nutrients = await client.from("food_nutrients").insert(rows);
  if (nutrients.error) return fail(nutrients.error.message);

  const details = await client.from("product_details").insert({
    food_id: id, brand: input.brand, provider: input.source === "off" ? "Open Food Facts" : "manual", provider_record_id: input.barcode,
    last_lookup: new Date().toISOString(), lookup_status: input.source === "off" ? "found" : "entered_by_user",
  });
  if (details.error) return fail(details.error.message);

  if (input.barcode) {
    const barcode = await client.from("product_barcodes").insert({ barcode: input.barcode, food_id: id, owner_id: userId });
    if (barcode.error) return fail(barcode.error.code === "23505" ? "You already saved a product with that barcode." : barcode.error.message);
  }
  return { id };
}

New src/app/api/products/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { validateProductInput } from "@/lib/product";
import { createProduct, loadFoodSummary } from "@/lib/server/products";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try {
    body = (await request.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid product." }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const input = validateProductInput(body);
  if (typeof input === "string") return NextResponse.json({ error: input }, { status: 400 });
  const created = await createProduct(auth.client, auth.user.id, input);
  if (typeof created === "string") return NextResponse.json({ error: created }, { status: 400 });
  return NextResponse.json({ id: created.id, food: await loadFoodSummary(auth.client, created.id) }, { status: 201 });
}

New src/app/api/products/lookup/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { FOOD_SELECT } from "@/lib/food-select";
import { isValidBarcode, parseOffProduct } from "@/lib/off";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const FIELDS = "product_name,product_name_en,generic_name,brands,nutriments,serving_quantity,serving_quantity_unit";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  const barcode = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("barcode")?.trim() ?? "";
  if (!isValidBarcode(barcode)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "That doesn’t look like a valid barcode." }, { status: 400 });

  // 1. Already saved by you: no outside lookup needed.
  const { data: link } = await auth.client.from("product_barcodes").select("food_id").eq("barcode", barcode).limit(1).maybeSingle();
  if (link) {
    const { data: food } = await auth.client.from("foods").select(FOOD_SELECT).eq("id", link.food_id).single();
    if (food) return NextResponse.json({ source: "saved", food });
  }

  // 2. Open Food Facts. Responses are cached for a day so the same barcode is never fetched twice.
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`https://world.openfoodfacts.org/api/v2/product/${barcode}.json?fields=${FIELDS}`, {
      headers: { "User-Agent": "Ahara/0.1 (personal nutrition tracker)" },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
      next: { revalidate: 86400 },
    });
    if (response.status === 429) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Open Food Facts is busy right now. Try again in a minute, or enter the label values yourself." }, { status: 429 });
    if (response.ok) {
      const product = parseOffProduct(barcode, await response.json());
      if (product) return NextResponse.json({ source: "off", product });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ source: "none", barcode });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ source: "none", barcode, unreachable: true });
  }
}

New src/components/food/BarcodeScanner.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { isValidBarcode } from "@/lib/off";

type DetectedCode = { rawValue: string };
type DetectorLike = { detect: (source: CanvasImageSource) => Promise<DetectedCode[]> };
type DetectorConstructor = new (options?: { formats?: string[] }) => DetectorLike;

type Props = { onDetected: (code: string) => void; onUnavailable: (reason: string) => void };

export function BarcodeScanner({ onDetected, onUnavailable }: Props) {
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const detectedRef = useRef(onDetected);
  const unavailableRef = useRef(onUnavailable);

  useEffect(() => {
    detectedRef.current = onDetected;
    unavailableRef.current = onUnavailable;
  }, [onDetected, onUnavailable]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const Detector = (window as unknown as { BarcodeDetector?: DetectorConstructor }).BarcodeDetector;
    if (!Detector || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
      unavailableRef.current("This browser can’t scan barcodes. Type the number instead.");
      return;
    }
    let detector: DetectorLike;
    try {
      detector = new Detector({ formats: ["ean_13", "ean_8", "upc_a", "upc_e"] });
    } catch {
      detector = new Detector();
    }
    let stopped = false;
    let timer: number | undefined;
    let stream: MediaStream | null = null;

    navigator.mediaDevices
      .getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: { ideal: "environment" } }, audio: false })
      .then((media) => {
        if (stopped) {
          media.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
          return;
        }
        stream = media;
        const video = videoRef.current;
        if (!video) return;
        video.srcObject = media;
        void video.play();
        const scan = async () => {
          if (stopped) return;
          try {
            const codes = await detector.detect(video);
            const hit = codes.find((code) => isValidBarcode(code.rawValue));
            if (hit) {
              detectedRef.current(hit.rawValue);
              return;
            }
          } catch {
            /* a frame that can't be read is simply skipped */
          }
          timer = window.setTimeout(scan, 300);
        };
        void scan();
      })
      .catch(() => unavailableRef.current("Camera access was blocked. You can type the barcode instead."));

    return () => {
      stopped = true;
      if (timer) window.clearTimeout(timer);
      stream?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="scan-box">
      <video ref={videoRef} playsInline muted aria-label="Camera view" />
      <p className="muted">Point the camera at the barcode and hold steady.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

New src/components/food/ProductForm.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import type { ParsedProduct } from "@/lib/off";
import { buildProduct, type ProductFormValues } from "@/lib/product";
import type { FoodSummary } from "@/lib/units";

export type ProductPrefill = { barcode: string | null; source: "off" | "manual"; product?: ParsedProduct };

const text = (value: number | null | undefined) => (value === null || value === undefined ? "" : String(value));

function initial(prefill: ProductPrefill): ProductFormValues {
  const per = prefill.product?.per100g;
  return {
    barcode: prefill.barcode ?? "",
    name: prefill.product?.name ?? "",
    brand: prefill.product?.brand ?? "",
    source: prefill.source,
    basis: "per100g",
    servingName: "serving",
    servingGrams: text(prefill.product?.servingGrams),
    kcal: text(per?.kcal), protein: text(per?.proteinG), carbs: text(per?.carbsG), fat: text(per?.fatG),
    satFat: text(per?.satFatG), fibre: text(per?.fibreG), sugar: text(per?.sugarG), sodium: text(per?.sodiumMg),
    note: "",
  };
}

type Props = { prefill: ProductPrefill; onSaved: (food: FoodSummary) => void; onCancel: () => void };

export function ProductForm({ prefill, onSaved, onCancel }: Props) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<ProductFormValues>(() => initial(prefill));
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const set = <K extends keyof ProductFormValues>(key: K, value: ProductFormValues[K]) => setValues((old) => ({ ...old, [key]: value }));
  const per100 = values.basis === "per100g";
  const unit = per100 ? "per 100 g" : "per serving";

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
