    event.preventDefault();
    const built = buildProduct(values);
    if (!built.ok) return setError(built.error);
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const data = await apiFetch<{ food: FoodSummary }>("/api/products", { method: "POST", json: built.value });
      onSaved(data.food);
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "The product couldn’t be saved.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const field = (label: string, key: keyof ProductFormValues) => (
    <label key={key}>{label} ({unit})
      <input inputMode="decimal" value={values[key] as string} onChange={(event) => set(key, event.target.value as never)} />
    </label>
  );

  return (
    <form className="ax-stack" onSubmit={submit}>
      {prefill.source === "off" ? (
        <p className="muted">Found on Open Food Facts. These values come from volunteers, so check them against the pack before saving.</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="muted">Enter the values printed on the pack. Leave blank anything you can’t see; blank stays unknown, never zero.</p>
      )}
      {prefill.barcode && <p className="muted">Barcode {prefill.barcode}</p>}
      <label>Product name<input value={values.name} onChange={(event) => set("name", event.target.value)} maxLength={120} /></label>
      <label>Brand (optional)<input value={values.brand} onChange={(event) => set("brand", event.target.value)} maxLength={80} /></label>

      <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="What the label shows">
        <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${per100 ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={per100} onClick={() => set("basis", "per100g")}>Values per 100 g</button>
        <button type="button" className={`ax-chip ${!per100 ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={!per100} onClick={() => set("basis", "perServing")}>Values per serving</button>
      </div>
      {!per100 && <label>One serving is called<input value={values.servingName} onChange={(event) => set("servingName", event.target.value)} maxLength={40} placeholder="serving, biscuit, packet…" /></label>}
      <label>One serving weighs (g){per100 ? ", if the pack says" : ", to also log by grams"}
        <input inputMode="decimal" value={values.servingGrams} onChange={(event) => set("servingGrams", event.target.value)} />
      </label>

      {field("Calories (required)", "kcal")}
      {field("Protein (g)", "protein")}
      {field("Carbs (g)", "carbs")}
      {field("Fat (g)", "fat")}
      {field("Saturated fat (g)", "satFat")}
      {field("Fibre (g)", "fibre")}
      {field("Sugar (g)", "sugar")}
      {field("Sodium (mg)", "sodium")}
      <label>Note (optional)<input value={values.note} onChange={(event) => set("note", event.target.value)} maxLength={500} /></label>

      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <div className="install-actions">
        <button className="button primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving…" : "Save and choose amount"}</button>
        <button type="button" className="button secondary" onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

New src/components/food/BarcodeFlow.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { BarcodeScanner } from "@/components/food/BarcodeScanner";
import { ProductForm, type ProductPrefill } from "@/components/food/ProductForm";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { isValidBarcode, type ParsedProduct } from "@/lib/off";
import type { FoodSummary } from "@/lib/units";

type Lookup =
  | { source: "saved"; food: FoodSummary }
  | { source: "off"; product: ParsedProduct }
  | { source: "none"; barcode: string; unreachable?: boolean };
type Step = "scan" | "looking" | "manual" | "form";

export function BarcodeFlow({ onFood, onClose }: { onFood: (food: FoodSummary) => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("scan");
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [prefill, setPrefill] = useState<ProductPrefill | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => event.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  const lookup = useCallback(
    async (code: string) => {
      setStep("looking");
      setMessage("");
      try {
        const result = await apiFetch<Lookup>(`/api/products/lookup?barcode=${encodeURIComponent(code)}`);
        if (result.source === "saved") return onFood(result.food);
        if (result.source === "off") setPrefill({ barcode: code, source: "off", product: result.product });
        else {
          setPrefill({ barcode: code, source: "manual" });
          setMessage(result.unreachable ? "Couldn’t reach Open Food Facts. Enter the label values yourself." : "This product isn’t on Open Food Facts. Enter the label values yourself.");
        }
        setStep("form");
      } catch (caught) {
        setMessage(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "The lookup failed.");
        setStep("manual"); // never go back to scanning automatically, or the same barcode would be looked up again and again
      }
    },
    [onFood],
  );

  function submitTyped(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const code = typed.replace(/\s/g, "");
    if (!isValidBarcode(code)) return setMessage("A barcode is 8 to 14 digits, with no letters.");
    void lookup(code);
  }

  return (
    <div className="ax-sheet-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="ax-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Scan a barcode" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="ax-sheet-head">
          <h2>Scan a barcode</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button>
        </div>

        {step === "scan" && (
          <>
            <BarcodeScanner
              onDetected={(code) => void lookup(code)}
              onUnavailable={(reason) => {
                setMessage(reason);
                setStep("manual");
              }}
            />
            <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setStep("manual")}>Type the barcode instead</button>
          </>
        )}
        {step === "looking" && <p className="muted" role="status">Looking up this product…</p>}
        {step === "manual" && (
          <form className="ax-stack" onSubmit={submitTyped}>
            <label>Barcode number
              <input inputMode="numeric" value={typed} onChange={(event) => setTyped(event.target.value)} placeholder="Digits under the barcode" autoComplete="off" />
            </label>
            <div className="install-actions">
              <button className="button primary">Look up</button>
              <button type="button" className="button secondary" onClick={() => { setMessage(""); setStep("scan"); }}>Use camera</button>
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="text-link" onClick={() => { setPrefill({ barcode: null, source: "manual" }); setMessage(""); setStep("form"); }}>
              Add a packaged food without a barcode
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
        {step === "form" && prefill && <ProductForm prefill={prefill} onSaved={onFood} onCancel={onClose} />}

        {message && step !== "looking" && <p className={step === "form" ? "muted" : "inline-error"} role="status">{message}</p>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Replace src/components/food/AddFoodExperience.tsx (adds My versions, barcode, and the Adjust link)

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell/PageHeader";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import { BarcodeFlow } from "@/components/food/BarcodeFlow";
import { OwnFoodForm } from "@/components/food/OwnFoodForm";
import { PortionPicker } from "@/components/food/PortionPicker";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { localDate, localTime, zonedDateTimeToInstant } from "@/lib/dates";
import { MEALS, mealForHour, type Meal } from "@/lib/meals";
import type { Adjustment } from "@/lib/nutrition";
import { availableUnits, defaultKcalLabel, formatQuantity, parseQuantity, servingNameFor, type FoodSummary, type PortionUnit } from "@/lib/units";

type Recent = { food: FoodSummary; quantity: number; unit: PortionUnit };
type Quick = { favourites: { food: FoodSummary }[]; recents: Recent[] };
type SearchVersion = { id: string; name: string; adjustments: Adjustment[]; food: FoodSummary };
type Logged = { id: string; name: string; meal: Meal; date: string; warning?: string };
const EMPTY_QUICK: Quick = { favourites: [], recents: [] };
const DATE_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

export function AddFoodExperience() {
  const session = useSession();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [results, setResults] = useState<FoodSummary[]>([]);
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<SearchVersion[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [quick, setQuick] = useState<Quick>(EMPTY_QUICK);
  const [food, setFood] = useState<FoodSummary | null>(null);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState<SearchVersion | null>(null);
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<PortionUnit>("serving");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("1");
  const [meal, setMeal] = useState<Meal>("breakfast");
  const [date, setDate] = useState("");
  const [time, setTime] = useState("");
  const [showWhen, setShowWhen] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [lastLogged, setLastLogged] = useState<Logged | null>(null);
  const [own, setOwn] = useState(false);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);

  const today = date ? localDate(timeZone) : "";

  useEffect(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const todayDate = localDate(timeZone, now);
    const dateParam = params.get("date");
    const mealParam = params.get("meal");
    const useParamDate = Boolean(dateParam && DATE_PATTERN.test(dateParam) && dateParam <= todayDate);
    setDate(useParamDate ? (dateParam as string) : todayDate);
    setTime(localTime(timeZone, now));
    setMeal(MEALS.includes(mealParam as Meal) ? (mealParam as Meal) : mealForHour(Number(localTime(timeZone, now).slice(0, 2))));
  }, [timeZone, params]);

  useEffect(() => {
    apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick").then(setQuick).catch(() => setQuick(EMPTY_QUICK));
  }, [lastLogged, own]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 2) {
      setResults([]);
      setVersions([]);
      setSearching(false);
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      setSearching(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const data = await apiFetch<{ foods: FoodSummary[]; versions?: SearchVersion[] }>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`, { signal: controller.signal });
        setResults(data.foods);
        setVersions(data.versions ?? []);
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setError("We couldn’t search right now. Please try again.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setSearching(false);
      }
    }, 250);
    return () => {
      controller.abort();
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [query]);

  const favouriteIds = new Set(quick.favourites.map((item) => item.food.id));

  function choose(next: FoodSummary, preset?: { unit: PortionUnit; quantity: number }, chosenVersion?: SearchVersion) {
    const units = availableUnits(next);
    const usePreset = preset && units.includes(preset.unit);
    const startUnit = usePreset ? preset.unit : units[0] ?? "serving";
    setFood(next);
    setVersion(chosenVersion ?? null);
    setUnit(startUnit);
    setAmount(String(usePreset ? preset.quantity : startUnit === "g" ? 100 : 1));
    setError("");
    setLastLogged(null);
  }

  async function logEntry(target: FoodSummary, logUnit: PortionUnit, quantity: number, withVersion?: SearchVersion | null): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!date || !time) return false;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const id = crypto.randomUUID();
      const consumedAt = zonedDateTimeToInstant(date, time, timeZone);
      await apiFetch("/api/logs", { method: "POST", json: { id, foodId: target.id, quantity, unit: logUnit, mealType: meal, consumedAt: consumedAt.toISOString() } });
      let warning: string | undefined;
      if (withVersion) {
        try {
          await apiFetch("/api/logs/adjustments", { method: "POST", json: { logId: id, adjustments: withVersion.adjustments } });
        } catch {
          warning = "Logged, but your version’s changes couldn’t be applied. Open the entry from Today to adjust it.";
        }
      }
      setLastLogged({ id, name: withVersion ? withVersion.name : target.canonical_name, meal, date, warning });
      return true;
    } catch (caught) {
      setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : "That entry couldn’t be saved. Your choices are still here.");
      return false;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveFromSheet() {
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    if (!food || quantity === null) return;
    if (await logEntry(food, unit, quantity, version)) {
      setFood(null);
      setVersion(null);
      setQuery("");
    }
  }

  async function undo() {
    if (!lastLogged) return;
    try {
      await apiFetch(`/api/logs?id=${encodeURIComponent(lastLogged.id)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      setLastLogged(null);
    } catch {
      setError("That entry couldn’t be removed. You can remove it from Today.");
    }
  }

  async function toggleFavourite(target: FoodSummary) {
    try {
      await apiFetch("/api/favourites", { method: "POST", json: { foodId: target.id, favourite: !favouriteIds.has(target.id) } });
      setQuick(await apiFetch<Quick>("/api/foods/quick"));
    } catch {
      setError("That favourite couldn’t be saved.");
    }
  }

  const whenBar = (
    <section className="ax-when" aria-label="When did you have it?">
      <div className="ax-chips">
        {MEALS.map((item) => (
          <button key={item} className={`ax-chip ${meal === item ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={meal === item} onClick={() => setMeal(item)}>{item}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="muted">
        {date === today ? "Today" : date} at {time}{" "}
        <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setShowWhen(!showWhen)}>{showWhen ? "Done" : "Change date or time"}</button>
      </p>
      {showWhen && (
        <div className="ax-when-fields">
          <label>Date<input type="date" value={date} max={today} onChange={(event) => setDate(event.target.value)} /></label>
          <label>Time<input type="time" value={time} onChange={(event) => setTime(event.target.value)} /></label>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );

  if (own) {
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Your own food" title="Add a food that isn’t listed" description="Add what you know. Anything you leave blank stays unknown." />
        <OwnFoodForm initialName={query.trim()} onCancel={() => setOwn(false)} onSaved={(saved) => { setOwn(false); choose(saved); }} />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (food) {
    const quantity = parseQuantity(amount);
    const favourite = favouriteIds.has(food.id);
    return (
      <>
        <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="How much?" />
        <section className="portion-editor">
          <button className="text-link" onClick={() => { setFood(null); setVersion(null); }}>← Change food</button>
          <div className="selected-food-heading">
            <div>
              <p className="eyebrow">{version ? "My version" : "Selected food"}</p>
              <h2>{version ? version.name : food.canonical_name}</h2>
              {version && (
                <p className="muted">
                  Based on {food.canonical_name}, with your saved changes.{" "}
                  <button className="text-link" onClick={() => setVersion(null)}>Use without them</button>
                </p>
              )}
              {food.match_quality === "approximate" && <p className="ax-note">Closest match, not the same dish.</p>}
            </div>
            <button className="favourite-button" aria-pressed={favourite} onClick={() => void toggleFavourite(food)}>{favourite ? "★ Saved" : "☆ Save"}</button>
          </div>
          <PortionPicker food={food} unit={unit} amount={amount} adjustments={version?.adjustments} onUnitChange={setUnit} onAmountChange={setAmount} />
          {availableUnits(food).length > 0 && (
            <>
              {whenBar}
              {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
              <button className="button primary full-width" disabled={saving || quantity === null || !date || !time} onClick={() => void saveFromSheet()}>
                {saving ? "Adding…" : `Add to ${meal}`}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </section>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Food diary" title="What did you eat?" description="Search dishes or ingredients, scan a pack, then choose the portion." />
      {lastLogged && (
        <div className="post-save" role="status">
          <p>{lastLogged.name} added to {lastLogged.meal}.</p>
          {lastLogged.warning && <p className="ax-note">{lastLogged.warning}</p>}
          <Link href={`/today?date=${lastLogged.date}`}>View day</Link>
          <Link href={`/today?date=${lastLogged.date}&edit=${lastLogged.id}`}>Adjust</Link>
          <button onClick={() => void undo()}>Undo</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {whenBar}

      <section className="search-panel">
        <label htmlFor="food-search">Search dishes or ingredients</label>
        <div className="search-input">
          <input id="food-search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Try poha, dosa, idli…" autoComplete="off" />
          <button onClick={() => setQuery("")} aria-label="Clear search">×</button>
        </div>
        <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setScanning(true)}>Scan a barcode</button>
        <p className="search-status" role="status">{searching ? "Searching…" : query.trim().length >= 2 ? `${results.length + versions.length} options found` : ""}</p>

        {query.trim().length >= 2 ? (
          <>
            {versions.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>My versions</h2>
                <div className="search-results">
                  {versions.map((item) => (
                    <button className="search-result" key={item.id} onClick={() => choose(item.food, undefined, item)}>
                      <span>
                        <b>{item.name}</b>
                        <small>Based on {item.food.canonical_name} · {item.adjustments.map((adjustment) => adjustment.label).join(", ")}</small>
                      </span>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </section>
            )}
            {!searching && !results.length && !versions.length && !error && <p className="muted">We couldn’t find that dish. Try another name, scan the pack, or add your own below.</p>}
            <div className="search-results">
              {results.map((result) => {
                const aliasDiffers = result.matched_alias && result.matched_alias.toLowerCase() !== result.canonical_name.toLowerCase();
                return (
                  <button className="search-result" key={result.id} onClick={() => choose(result)}>
                    <span>
                      <b>{result.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(result)}{result.owner_id ? " · Your food" : ""}</small>
                      {(aliasDiffers || result.match_quality === "approximate") && (
                        <small className="ax-note">
                          {aliasDiffers && `Matched “${result.matched_alias}”`}
                          {aliasDiffers && result.match_quality === "approximate" && " · "}
                          {result.match_quality === "approximate" && "Closest match, not the same dish"}
                        </small>
                      )}
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {quick.recents.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Recent</h2>
                {quick.recents.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food, { unit: item.unit, quantity: item.quantity })}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>Last: {formatQuantity(item.quantity)} {item.unit === "g" ? "g" : servingNameFor(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                    <button className="button secondary" disabled={saving || !date} onClick={() => void logEntry(item.food, item.unit, item.quantity)}>Log again</button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
            {quick.favourites.length > 0 && (
              <section className="ax-section">
                <h2>Favourites</h2>
                {quick.favourites.map((item) => (
                  <div className="ax-quick-row" key={item.food.id}>
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => choose(item.food)}>
                      <b>{item.food.canonical_name}</b>
                      <small>{defaultKcalLabel(item.food)}</small>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </section>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        <button className="text-link quiet-link" onClick={() => setOwn(true)}>Add a food that isn’t listed</button>
        <p className="muted"><Link href="/my-foods">Manage my foods</Link></p>
      </section>

      {scanning && <BarcodeFlow onClose={() => setScanning(false)} onFood={(scanned) => { setScanning(false); choose(scanned); }} />}
    </>
  );
}
Edits to existing files

src/components/dashboard/TodayDashboard.tsx: four small edits.

P6 checks

In Today, tap an entry, then “Adjust this entry”. Choose “Add or leave out an ingredient”, search “ghee”, set 5 g, and tap Add. The entry shows a Measured badge, the “+X kcal” line, and the new total. Today’s row now says “Adjusted”.
Choose “Left out” for the same ingredient and the numbers go down. Remove an adjustment and it returns to the published values.
Try “Enter my own change”. It is labelled Manual.
On an entry with a missing nutrient, “Fill in unknown values” lets you set it. Known values are never overwritten.
Change the amount of an adjusted entry and save. The adjustments are kept and recalculated.
Save the adjusted entry as a My Version, then search for its name in Add. It appears under “My versions”, and logging it applies the same changes.
Tag an entry Junk or Homemade. The tag appears on its row in Today.
The favourite star and Delete account no longer give false errors.

P7 checks (on your phone, over HTTPS)

In Add, tap “Scan a barcode” and allow the camera. Scan a packaged item. If it’s on Open Food Facts, you get a form pre-filled with the label values. Check them against the pack, then save. You land on “How much?”.
Scan the same pack again. It opens straight to “How much?” with no outside lookup.
Scan something unknown. The form opens blank and tells you to enter the label values.
Deny the camera, or use a browser without scanning. You get the “Type the barcode” box instead.
A pack with a serving size lets you log it by serving or by grams. One without is grams-only.
Search the product’s name later and it appears with “Your food”.

Compacted conversation

P8,p9 now

I couldn’t read the full column lists of the old activity and tracker tables from here, and they have never been run end to end. The P8 migration therefore drops and recreates them with a known shape. It stops with an error if any of them already holds rows, so it can’t delete data silently. Nothing here has been run.

P8 scope:

Activity page with steps, workouts, an activity bar toward your target, and trackers (count, amount, yes/no, measurement).
Activity is never netted against intake.
A workout without calories counts as unknown, not zero.
Writes go straight from the browser under row-level security, with ids generated on the device so a replay can’t double-save.

P9 scope:

Insights with Week, Month and Year views, logged-days coverage, and partial-day handling.
A meal breakdown, a tag breakdown and top foods.
The goal that applied on each day is used, not today’s goal.
Database: run in the Supabase SQL editor

Also save as supabase/migrations/202611140022_activity_trackers.sql.

sql
do $$
begin
  if exists (select 1 from public.daily_steps) or exists (select 1 from public.activities)
     or exists (select 1 from public.trackers) or exists (select 1 from public.tracker_entries) then
    raise exception 'Activity or tracker tables already hold data. Stop here and tell Claude.';
  end if;
end $$;

drop table if exists public.tracker_entries, public.tracker_target_versions, public.trackers,
                     public.activities, public.daily_steps cascade;

create table public.daily_steps (
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  local_date date not null,
  steps integer not null check (steps between 0 and 200000),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (owner_id, local_date)
);

create table public.activities (
  id uuid primary key,
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  local_date date not null,
  name text not null check (char_length(name) between 1 and 80),
  duration_min integer not null check (duration_min between 1 and 1440),
  kcal numeric check (kcal is null or kcal between 0 and 10000),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index activities_owner_date on public.activities (owner_id, local_date);

create table public.trackers (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null check (char_length(name) between 1 and 60),
  kind text not null check (kind in ('counter', 'amount', 'yes_no', 'measurement')),
  unit text check (unit is null or char_length(unit) <= 20),
  step numeric check (step is null or step > 0),
  target numeric check (target is null or target > 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create unique index trackers_active_name on public.trackers (owner_id, lower(name)) where archived_at is null;

create table public.tracker_entries (
  id uuid primary key,
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
  tracker_id uuid not null references public.trackers(id) on delete cascade,
  local_date date not null,
  value numeric not null check (value >= 0 and value <= 1000000),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index tracker_entries_owner_date on public.tracker_entries (owner_id, local_date);

alter table public.daily_steps enable row level security;
alter table public.activities enable row level security;
alter table public.trackers enable row level security;
alter table public.tracker_entries enable row level security;

create policy "own steps" on public.daily_steps for all using (owner_id = auth.uid()) with check (owner_id = auth.uid());
create policy "own activities" on public.activities for all using (owner_id = auth.uid()) with check (owner_id = auth.uid());
create policy "own trackers" on public.trackers for all using (owner_id = auth.uid()) with check (owner_id = auth.uid());
create policy "own tracker entries" on public.tracker_entries for all using (owner_id = auth.uid())
  with check (owner_id = auth.uid() and exists (select 1 from public.trackers t where t.id = tracker_id and t.owner_id = auth.uid()));

If a src/app/api/activity, api/steps or api/trackers folder exists in the repo, delete it. It targets the old tables.

P8: logic

New src/lib/activity.ts

ts
export type Workout = { id: string; name: string; duration_min: number; kcal: number | null };
export type TrackerKind = "counter" | "amount" | "yes_no" | "measurement";
export type Tracker = { id: string; name: string; kind: TrackerKind; unit: string | null; step: number | null; target: number | null };
export type TrackerEntry = { id: string; tracker_id: string; value: number; created_at: string };

export const KIND_LABELS: Record<TrackerKind, string> = {
  counter: "Count (cups, tablets…)",
  amount: "Amount (ml, g…)",
  yes_no: "Yes or no each day",
  measurement: "Measurement (weight…)",
};

const round1 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
export const formatNumber = (value: number) => String(round1(value));

/** Workout calories. A workout without calories is unknown, never zero. */
export function sumWorkoutKcal(workouts: Pick<Workout, "kcal">[]) {
  const known = workouts.filter((workout) => workout.kcal !== null);
  return {
    total: known.reduce((sum, workout) => sum + (workout.kcal as number), 0),
    unknownCount: workouts.length - known.length,
    knownCount: known.length,
  };
}

/** Neutral wording. Activity is never netted against intake. */
export function describeActivity(workouts: Pick<Workout, "kcal">[], goal: number | null): string {
  if (workouts.length === 0) return goal === null ? "Nothing logged yet." : `Nothing logged yet. Your activity target is ${Math.round(goal)} kcal.`;
  const { total, unknownCount, knownCount } = sumWorkoutKcal(workouts);
  if (knownCount === 0) return "Workouts logged without calories, so there is no total to compare.";
  const caveat = unknownCount > 0 ? ` (at least: ${unknownCount} workout${unknownCount === 1 ? " has" : "s have"} no calories)` : "";
  const shown = Math.round(total);
  if (goal === null) return `${shown} kcal from workouts${caveat}.`;
  return shown >= goal
    ? `${shown} kcal from workouts${caveat}. Your target of ${Math.round(goal)} kcal is reached.`
    : `${shown} of ${Math.round(goal)} kcal from workouts${caveat}.`;
}

export function trackerSummary(tracker: Tracker, entries: Pick<TrackerEntry, "value">[]): string {
  const unit = tracker.unit ? ` ${tracker.unit}` : "";
  if (tracker.kind === "yes_no") return entries.length > 0 ? "Done today" : "Not marked yet";
  if (tracker.kind === "measurement") {
    return entries.length > 0 ? `Latest: ${formatNumber(entries[entries.length - 1].value)}${unit}` : "No reading yet";
  }
  const total = entries.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.value, 0);
  const target = tracker.target ? ` of ${formatNumber(tracker.target)}` : "";
  return `${formatNumber(total)}${target}${unit}`;
}

export function trackerProgress(tracker: Tracker, entries: Pick<TrackerEntry, "value">[]): number | null {
  if (!tracker.target || (tracker.kind !== "counter" && tracker.kind !== "amount")) return null;
  const total = entries.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.value, 0);
  return Math.min(100, Math.round((total / tracker.target) * 100));
}

export function parseAmount(text: string, max: number): number | null {
  const cleaned = text.trim().replace(",", ".");
  if (cleaned === "") return null;
  const value = Number(cleaned);
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 && value <= max ? value : null;
}

New src/lib/activity.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { describeActivity, parseAmount, sumWorkoutKcal, trackerProgress, trackerSummary, type Tracker } from "./activity";

const tracker = (over: Partial<Tracker>): Tracker => ({ id: "t", name: "Water", kind: "amount", unit: "ml", step: 250, target: 2000, ...over });

describe("activity", () => {
  it("treats a workout without calories as unknown, not zero", () => {
    expect(sumWorkoutKcal([{ kcal: 200 }, { kcal: null }])).toEqual({ total: 200, unknownCount: 1, knownCount: 1 });
    expect(describeActivity([{ kcal: null }], 300)).toContain("without calories");
  });
  it("says at least when some workouts have no calories", () => {
    expect(describeActivity([{ kcal: 100 }, { kcal: null }], 300)).toContain("at least");
  });
  it("uses neutral wording and never mentions intake", () => {
    expect(describeActivity([{ kcal: 350 }], 300)).toContain("reached");
    expect(describeActivity([{ kcal: 100 }], 300)).toBe("100 of 300 kcal from workouts.");
    expect(describeActivity([], null)).toBe("Nothing logged yet.");
  });
  it("summarises trackers by kind", () => {
    expect(trackerSummary(tracker({}), [{ value: 250 }, { value: 250 }])).toBe("500 of 2000 ml");
    expect(trackerSummary(tracker({ kind: "yes_no" }), [])).toBe("Not marked yet");
    expect(trackerSummary(tracker({ kind: "measurement", unit: "kg" }), [{ value: 70 }, { value: 69.5 }])).toBe("Latest: 69.5 kg");
    expect(trackerProgress(tracker({}), [{ value: 5000 }])).toBe(100);
    expect(trackerProgress(tracker({ kind: "yes_no" }), [])).toBeNull();
  });
  it("parses amounts strictly", () => {
    expect(parseAmount("1,5", 10)).toBe(1.5);
    expect(parseAmount("", 10)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseAmount("-1", 10)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseAmount("abc", 10)).toBeNull();
    expect(parseAmount("11", 10)).toBeNull();
  });
});

New src/components/activity/ActivityExperience.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useSession } from "@/components/app-shell/SessionProvider";
import {
  KIND_LABELS, describeActivity, parseAmount, trackerProgress, trackerSummary,
  type Tracker, type TrackerEntry, type TrackerKind, type Workout,
} from "@/lib/activity";
import { localDate } from "@/lib/dates";
import { addDays } from "@/lib/insights";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

const DUPLICATE = "23505"; // the same client id arriving twice is a replay, not an error
const DATE_PATTERN = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

export function ActivityExperience() {
  const session = useSession();
  const timeZone = session?.timeZone ?? "Asia/Kolkata";
  const userId = session?.userId;
  const today = localDate(timeZone);

  const [date, setDate] = useState(today);
  const [goal, setGoal] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [steps, setSteps] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [stepsText, setStepsText] = useState("");
  const [workouts, setWorkouts] = useState<Workout[]>([]);
  const [trackers, setTrackers] = useState<Tracker[]>([]);
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<TrackerEntry[]>([]);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [wName, setWName] = useState("");
  const [wMinutes, setWMinutes] = useState("");
  const [wKcal, setWKcal] = useState("");
  const [showTrackerForm, setShowTrackerForm] = useState(false);
  const [confirmArchive, setConfirmArchive] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [readings, setReadings] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const db = supabase();
    const [goalRes, stepsRes, workoutRes, trackerRes, entryRes] = await Promise.all([
      db.from("goal_versions").select("value").eq("kind", "activity_kcal").lte("starts_at", date)
        .or(`ends_at.is.null,ends_at.gte.${date}`).order("starts_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1),
      db.from("daily_steps").select("steps").eq("local_date", date).maybeSingle(),
      db.from("activities").select("id,name,duration_min,kcal").eq("local_date", date).order("created_at"),
      db.from("trackers").select("id,name,kind,unit,step,target").is("archived_at", null).order("created_at"),
      db.from("tracker_entries").select("id,tracker_id,value,created_at").eq("local_date", date).order("created_at"),
    ]);
    const failed = [goalRes, stepsRes, workoutRes, trackerRes, entryRes].find((result) => result.error);
    if (failed) setError("Some of this couldn’t be loaded. Pull to refresh or try again.");
    else setError("");
    const goalValue = (goalRes.data as { value: number }[] | null)?.[0]?.value;
    setGoal(goalValue === undefined ? null : Number(goalValue));
    const stepValue = (stepsRes.data as { steps: number } | null)?.steps ?? null;
    setSteps(stepValue);
    setStepsText(stepValue === null ? "" : String(stepValue));
    setWorkouts(((workoutRes.data ?? []) as Workout[]).map((row) => ({ ...row, kcal: row.kcal === null ? null : Number(row.kcal) })));
    setTrackers(((trackerRes.data ?? []) as Tracker[]).map((row) => ({ ...row, step: row.step === null ? null : Number(row.step), target: row.target === null ? null : Number(row.target) })));
    setEntries(((entryRes.data ?? []) as TrackerEntry[]).map((row) => ({ ...row, value: Number(row.value) })));
    setReady(true);
  }, [date]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [load]);

  async function run(action: () => PromiseLike<{ error: { code?: string; message: string } | null }>, failure: string) {
    setError("");
    const { error: failed } = await action();
    if (failed && failed.code !== DUPLICATE) {
      setError(failure);
      return false;
    }
    await load();
    return true;
  }

  async function saveSteps() {
    if (!userId) return;
    if (stepsText.trim() === "") {
      await run(() => supabase().from("daily_steps").delete().eq("local_date", date), "Steps couldn’t be cleared.");
      return;
    }
    const value = parseAmount(stepsText, 200000);
    if (value === null || !Number.isInteger(value)) return setError("Steps must be a whole number from 0 to 200000.");
    await run(() => supabase().from("daily_steps").upsert({ owner_id: userId, local_date: date, steps: value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: "owner_id,local_date" }), "Steps couldn’t be saved.");
  }

  async function addWorkout(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!userId) return;
    const name = wName.trim();
    const minutes = parseAmount(wMinutes, 1440);
    const kcal = wKcal.trim() === "" ? null : parseAmount(wKcal, 10000);
    if (!name || name.length > 80) return setError("Name the workout in a few words.");
    if (minutes === null || minutes < 1 || !Number.isInteger(minutes)) return setError("Duration must be a whole number of minutes.");
    if (wKcal.trim() !== "" && kcal === null) return setError("Calories must be a number from 0 to 10000, or leave it blank.");
    const ok = await run(() => supabase().from("activities").insert({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), owner_id: userId, local_date: date, name, duration_min: minutes, kcal }), "The workout couldn’t be saved.");
    if (ok) {
      setWName("");
      setWMinutes("");
      setWKcal("");
    }
  }

  const removeWorkout = (id: string) => run(() => supabase().from("activities").delete().eq("id", id), "That workout couldn’t be removed.");

  async function addEntry(tracker: Tracker, value: number) {
    if (!userId) return;
    await run(() => supabase().from("tracker_entries").insert({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), owner_id: userId, tracker_id: tracker.id, local_date: date, value }), "That entry couldn’t be saved.");
  }
  const removeEntry = (id: string) => run(() => supabase().from("tracker_entries").delete().eq("id", id), "That entry couldn’t be removed.");

  async function archive(tracker: Tracker) {
    if (confirmArchive !== tracker.id) return setConfirmArchive(tracker.id);
    setConfirmArchive(null);
    await run(() => supabase().from("trackers").update({ archived_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", tracker.id), "The tracker couldn’t be archived.");
  }

  const kcalTotal = workouts.reduce((sum, workout) => sum + (workout.kcal ?? 0), 0);
  const barPercent = goal && workouts.some((workout) => workout.kcal !== null) ? Math.min(100, Math.round((kcalTotal / goal) * 100)) : 0;
  const dateLabel = date === today ? "Today" : date;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="ax-daynav">
        <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setDate(addDays(date, -1))} aria-label="Previous day">‹</button>
        <strong>{dateLabel}</strong>
        <button className="button secondary" disabled={date >= today} onClick={() => setDate(addDays(date, 1))} aria-label="Next day">›</button>
      </div>
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {!ready ? (
        <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /><div /></div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <section className="ax-section">
            <h2>Workouts</h2>
            <div className="ax-bar" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={barPercent} aria-label="Workout calories toward your activity target">
              <span style={{ width: `${barPercent}%` }} />
            </div>
            <p className="muted">{describeActivity(workouts, goal)}</p>
            {goal === null && <p className="muted">No activity target set. <Link href="/goals">Set one in Goals</Link>, or leave it off.</p>}
            {workouts.map((workout) => (
              <div className="ax-quick-row" key={workout.id}>
                <span>
                  <b>{workout.name}</b>
                  <small>{workout.duration_min} min · {workout.kcal === null ? "calories not entered" : `${Math.round(workout.kcal)} kcal`}</small>
                </span>
                <button className="text-link" onClick={() => void removeWorkout(workout.id)}>Remove</button>
              </div>
            ))}
            <form className="ax-stack" onSubmit={addWorkout}>
              <label>Workout<input value={wName} onChange={(event) => setWName(event.target.value)} maxLength={80} placeholder="Walk, cricket, yoga…" /></label>
              <div className="ax-when-fields">
                <label>Minutes<input inputMode="numeric" value={wMinutes} onChange={(event) => setWMinutes(event.target.value)} /></label>
                <label>Calories (optional)<input inputMode="decimal" value={wKcal} onChange={(event) => setWKcal(event.target.value)} /></label>
              </div>
              <button className="button primary">Add workout</button>
            </form>
          </section>

          <section className="ax-section">
            <h2>Steps</h2>
            <p className="muted">{steps === null ? "Not entered. This stays unknown, not zero." : `${steps.toLocaleString("en-IN")} steps`}</p>
            <div className="ax-quick-row">
              <input inputMode="numeric" aria-label="Steps" value={stepsText} onChange={(event) => setStepsText(event.target.value)} placeholder="Steps for the day" />
              <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => void saveSteps()}>Save steps</button>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="ax-section">
            <h2>My trackers</h2>
            {trackers.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Track anything you like each day: water, tea, a vitamin, weight.</p>}
            {trackers.map((tracker) => {
              const mine = entries.filter((entry) => entry.tracker_id === tracker.id);
              const progress = trackerProgress(tracker, mine);
              const unit = tracker.unit ? ` ${tracker.unit}` : "";
              return (
                <div className="tx-meal" key={tracker.id}>
                  <div className="tx-meal-head">
                    <div>
                      <h3>{tracker.name}</h3>
                      <small>{trackerSummary(tracker, mine)}</small>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {progress !== null && <div className="ax-bar" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={progress} aria-label={`${tracker.name} progress`}><span style={{ width: `${progress}%` }} /></div>}
                  <div className="install-actions">
                    {(tracker.kind === "counter" || tracker.kind === "amount") && tracker.step && (
                      <button className="button primary" onClick={() => void addEntry(tracker, tracker.step as number)}>+{tracker.step}{unit}</button>
                    )}
                    {tracker.kind === "yes_no" && (
                      mine.length === 0
                        ? <button className="button primary" onClick={() => void addEntry(tracker, 1)}>Mark done</button>
                        : <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => void removeEntry(mine[mine.length - 1].id)}>Done ✓ (tap to undo)</button>
                    )}
                    {tracker.kind === "measurement" && (
                      <>
                        <input inputMode="decimal" aria-label={`${tracker.name} reading`} value={readings[tracker.id] ?? ""} onChange={(event) => setReadings((old) => ({ ...old, [tracker.id]: event.target.value }))} placeholder={`Reading${unit ? ` (${tracker.unit})` : ""}`} />
                        <button className="button primary" onClick={async () => {
                          const value = parseAmount(readings[tracker.id] ?? "", 1000000);
                          if (value === null) return setError("Enter a reading as a number.");
                          await addEntry(tracker, value);
                          setReadings((old) => ({ ...old, [tracker.id]: "" }));
                        }}>Save reading</button>
                      </>
                    )}
                    {tracker.kind !== "yes_no" && mine.length > 0 && (
                      <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => void removeEntry(mine[mine.length - 1].id)}>Undo last</button>
                    )}
                    <button className="text-link" onClick={() => void archive(tracker)}>{confirmArchive === tracker.id ? "Tap again to archive" : "Archive"}</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
            {showTrackerForm ? (
              <TrackerForm userId={userId} onDone={async (created) => { setShowTrackerForm(false); if (created) await load(); }} />
            ) : (
              <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setShowTrackerForm(true)}>Add a tracker</button>
            )}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function TrackerForm({ userId, onDone }: { userId?: string; onDone: (created: boolean) => void }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<TrackerKind>("counter");
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState("");
  const [step, setStep] = useState("1");
  const [target, setTarget] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const accumulates = kind === "counter" || kind === "amount";

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!userId) return;
    const cleanName = name.trim();
    if (!cleanName || cleanName.length > 60) return setError("Give the tracker a name of up to 60 characters.");
    const stepValue = accumulates ? parseAmount(step, 1000000) : null;
    if (accumulates && (stepValue === null || stepValue <= 0)) return setError("Each tap must add an amount above 0.");
    const targetValue = accumulates && target.trim() !== "" ? parseAmount(target, 1000000) : null;
    if (accumulates && target.trim() !== "" && (targetValue === null || targetValue <= 0)) return setError("The daily target must be above 0, or leave it blank.");
    setSaving(true);
    const { error: failed } = await supabase().from("trackers").insert({
      owner_id: userId, name: cleanName, kind, unit: unit.trim() || null, step: stepValue, target: targetValue,
    });
    setSaving(false);
    if (failed) return setError(failed.code === DUPLICATE ? "You already have a tracker with that name." : "The tracker couldn’t be saved.");
    onDone(true);
  }

  return (
    <form className="ax-stack" onSubmit={submit}>
      <label>Name<input value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} maxLength={60} placeholder="Water, tea, vitamin D, weight…" /></label>
      <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Type">
        {(Object.keys(KIND_LABELS) as TrackerKind[]).map((option) => (
          <button type="button" key={option} className={`ax-chip ${kind === option ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={kind === option} onClick={() => { setKind(option); setStep(option === "amount" ? "250" : "1"); }}>
            {KIND_LABELS[option]}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {kind !== "yes_no" && <label>Unit (optional)<input value={unit} onChange={(event) => setUnit(event.target.value)} maxLength={20} placeholder={kind === "amount" ? "ml" : kind === "measurement" ? "kg" : "cups"} /></label>}
      {accumulates && (
        <>
          <label>Each tap adds<input inputMode="decimal" value={step} onChange={(event) => setStep(event.target.value)} /></label>
          <label>Daily target (optional)<input inputMode="decimal" value={target} onChange={(event) => setTarget(event.target.value)} /></label>
        </>
      )}
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <div className="install-actions">
        <button className="button primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving…" : "Save tracker"}</button>
        <button type="button" className="button secondary" onClick={() => onDone(false)}>Cancel</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
P9: logic

New src/lib/insights.ts

ts
import { MEALS } from "@/lib/meals";

export type RangeKind = "week" | "month" | "year";
export type InsightLog = { local_date: string; meal_type: string; name_snapshot: string; kcal: number | null; tags: string[] };
export type GoalVersion = { value: number; starts_at: string; ends_at: string | null };
export type DayStatus = "none" | "nogoal" | "below" | "at" | "above" | "uncertain";
export type Bucket = { key: string; label: string; kcal: number | null; partial: boolean; goal: number | null; logged: boolean };
export type Insights = {
  range: RangeKind; start: string; end: string; label: string;
  elapsedDays: number; loggedDays: number;
  average: { kcal: number | null; partial: boolean };
  buckets: Bucket[];
  statusCounts: { below: number; at: number; above: number; uncertain: number };
  hasGoal: boolean;
  meals: { meal: string; kcal: number; entries: number }[];
  tags: { tag: string; entries: number; kcal: number }[];
  topFoods: { name: string; count: number; kcal: number | null }[];
  unknownEntries: number;
};

export const TOLERANCE_KCAL = 25;
const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
const MONTHS_LONG = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
const WEEKDAYS = ["S", "M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S"];
const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");
const toDate = (text: string) => {
  const [year, month, day] = text.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
};
const toText = (date: Date) => `${date.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad(date.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getUTCDate())}`;

export const isDateString = (text: string) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text) && toText(toDate(text)) === text;
export function addDays(text: string, days: number): string {
  const date = toDate(text);
  date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + days);
  return toText(date);
}

export function rangeBounds(range: RangeKind, anchor: string): { start: string; end: string } {
  const date = toDate(anchor);
  const year = date.getUTCFullYear();
  const month = date.getUTCMonth();
  if (range === "week") return { start: addDays(anchor, -6), end: anchor };
  if (range === "month") return { start: `${year}-${pad(month + 1)}-01`, end: toText(new Date(Date.UTC(year, month + 1, 0))) };
  return { start: `${year}-01-01`, end: `${year}-12-31` };
}

export function shiftAnchor(range: RangeKind, anchor: string, direction: 1 | -1): string {
  const date = toDate(anchor);
  if (range === "week") return addDays(anchor, 7 * direction);
  if (range === "month") return toText(new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth() + direction, 1)));
  return `${date.getUTCFullYear() + direction}-01-01`;
}

export function rangeLabel(range: RangeKind, start: string, end: string): string {
  const s = toDate(start);
  const e = toDate(end);
  if (range === "year") return String(s.getUTCFullYear());
  if (range === "month") return `${MONTHS_LONG[s.getUTCMonth()]} ${s.getUTCFullYear()}`;
  return `${s.getUTCDate()} ${MONTHS[s.getUTCMonth()]} – ${e.getUTCDate()} ${MONTHS[e.getUTCMonth()]}`;
}

/** The goal in force on a date, so a changed goal never rewrites older days. */
export function goalOn(goals: GoalVersion[], date: string): number | null {
  const match = goals
    .filter((goal) => goal.starts_at <= date && (goal.ends_at === null || goal.ends_at >= date))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.starts_at < b.starts_at ? 1 : -1))[0];
  return match ? Number(match.value) : null;
}

export function dayStatus(kcal: number | null, partial: boolean, goal: number | null, hasEntries: boolean): DayStatus {
  if (!hasEntries) return "none";
  if (goal === null) return "nogoal";
  if (kcal === null) return "uncertain";
  if (kcal > goal + TOLERANCE_KCAL) return "above"; // "at least" is already above, so this holds for partial days too
  if (partial) return "uncertain";
  return kcal < goal - TOLERANCE_KCAL ? "below" : "at";
}

const known = (values: (number | null)[]) => values.filter((value): value is number => value !== null);
const sum = (values: number[]) => values.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0);

export function buildInsights(args: { range: RangeKind; anchor: string; today: string; logs: InsightLog[]; goals: GoalVersion[] }): Insights {
  const { range, anchor, today, goals } = args;
  const { start, end } = rangeBounds(range, anchor);
  const logs = args.logs.filter((log) => log.local_date >= start && log.local_date <= end);

  const byDate = new Map<string, InsightLog[]>();
  for (const log of logs) byDate.set(log.local_date, [...(byDate.get(log.local_date) ?? []), log]);

  const days: { date: string; entries: number; kcal: number | null; partial: boolean; goal: number | null; status: DayStatus }[] = [];
  for (let date = start; date <= end; date = addDays(date, 1)) {
    const items = byDate.get(date) ?? [];
    const knownKcal = known(items.map((item) => item.kcal));
    const kcal = knownKcal.length > 0 ? sum(knownKcal) : null; // a day with only unknown calories has no total
    const partial = items.some((item) => item.kcal === null);
    const goal = goalOn(goals, date);
    days.push({ date, entries: items.length, kcal, partial, goal, status: dayStatus(kcal, partial, goal, items.length > 0) });
  }

  const elapsed = days.filter((day) => day.date <= today);
  const logged = elapsed.filter((day) => day.entries > 0);
  const withTotals = logged.filter((day) => day.kcal !== null);
  const average = {
    kcal: withTotals.length > 0 ? Math.round(sum(withTotals.map((day) => day.kcal as number)) / withTotals.length) : null,
    partial: logged.some((day) => day.partial),
  };

  let buckets: Bucket[];
  if (range === "year") {
    buckets = MONTHS.map((label, index) => {
      const prefix = `${start.slice(0, 4)}-${pad(index + 1)}`;
      const month = days.filter((day) => day.date.startsWith(prefix));
      const totals = month.filter((day) => day.kcal !== null);
      return {
        key: prefix, label: label[0], kcal: totals.length > 0 ? Math.round(sum(totals.map((day) => day.kcal as number)) / totals.length) : null,
        partial: month.some((day) => day.partial), goal: goalOn(goals, month[month.length - 1].date), logged: month.some((day) => day.entries > 0),
      };
    });
  } else {
    buckets = days.map((day) => ({
      key: day.date,
      label: range === "week" ? WEEKDAYS[toDate(day.date).getUTCDay()] : String(Number(day.date.slice(8))),
      kcal: day.kcal === null ? null : Math.round(day.kcal), partial: day.partial, goal: day.goal, logged: day.entries > 0,
    }));
  }

  const statusCounts = { below: 0, at: 0, above: 0, uncertain: 0 };
  for (const day of elapsed) if (day.status in statusCounts) statusCounts[day.status as keyof typeof statusCounts] += 1;

  const mealMap = new Map<string, { kcal: number; entries: number }>();
  const tagMap = new Map<string, { entries: number; kcal: number }>();
  const foodMap = new Map<string, { count: number; kcals: number[] }>();
  for (const log of logs) {
    const meal = mealMap.get(log.meal_type) ?? { kcal: 0, entries: 0 };
    mealMap.set(log.meal_type, { kcal: meal.kcal + (log.kcal ?? 0), entries: meal.entries + 1 });
    for (const tag of log.tags) {
      const entry = tagMap.get(tag) ?? { entries: 0, kcal: 0 };
      tagMap.set(tag, { entries: entry.entries + 1, kcal: entry.kcal + (log.kcal ?? 0) });
    }
    const food = foodMap.get(log.name_snapshot) ?? { count: 0, kcals: [] };
    foodMap.set(log.name_snapshot, { count: food.count + 1, kcals: log.kcal === null ? food.kcals : [...food.kcals, log.kcal] });
  }

  const order = [...MEALS] as string[];
  return {
    range, start, end, label: rangeLabel(range, start, end),
    elapsedDays: elapsed.length, loggedDays: logged.length, average, buckets, statusCounts,
    hasGoal: days.some((day) => day.goal !== null),
    meals: [...mealMap.entries()].map(([meal, value]) => ({ meal, ...value })).sort((a, b) => (order.indexOf(a.meal) + 99) % 99 - (order.indexOf(b.meal) + 99) % 99),
    tags: [...tagMap.entries()].map(([tag, value]) => ({ tag, ...value })).sort((a, b) => b.entries - a.entries),
    topFoods: [...foodMap.entries()]
      .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, count: value.count, kcal: value.kcals.length > 0 ? Math.round(sum(value.kcals)) : null }))
      .sort((a, b) => (b.kcal ?? -1) - (a.kcal ?? -1) || b.count - a.count)
      .slice(0, 8),
    unknownEntries: logs.filter((log) => log.kcal === null).length,
  };
}

New src/lib/insights.test.ts

ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { addDays, buildInsights, dayStatus, goalOn, isDateString, rangeBounds, shiftAnchor, type InsightLog } from "./insights";

// Fixture numbers only exercise the rules; they are not nutrition data.
const log = (date: string, kcal: number | null, over: Partial<InsightLog> = {}): InsightLog => ({
  local_date: date, meal_type: "lunch", name_snapshot: "Test food", kcal, tags: [], ...over,
});
const goals = [{ value: 2000, starts_at: "2026-01-01", ends_at: null }];

describe("insight ranges", () => {
  it("computes week, month and year bounds", () => {
    expect(rangeBounds("week", "2026-11-14")).toEqual({ start: "2026-11-08", end: "2026-11-14" });
    expect(rangeBounds("month", "2026-02-10")).toEqual({ start: "2026-02-01", end: "2026-02-28" });
    expect(rangeBounds("year", "2026-06-01")).toEqual({ start: "2026-01-01", end: "2026-12-31" });
  });
  it("shifts across month and year edges", () => {
    expect(shiftAnchor("month", "2026-01-15", -1)).toBe("2025-12-01");
    expect(shiftAnchor("year", "2026-03-01", 1)).toBe("2027-01-01");
    expect(addDays("2026-03-01", -1)).toBe("2026-02-28");
  });
  it("validates date strings", () => {
    expect(isDateString("2026-02-30")).toBe(false);
    expect(isDateString("2026-11-14")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("goal history", () => {
  it("uses the goal in force on each day", () => {
    const history = [
      { value: 1800, starts_at: "2026-01-01", ends_at: "2026-06-30" },
      { value: 2000, starts_at: "2026-07-01", ends_at: null },
    ];
    expect(goalOn(history, "2026-03-01")).toBe(1800);
    expect(goalOn(history, "2026-08-01")).toBe(2000);
    expect(goalOn(history, "2025-12-31")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("day status", () => {
  it("never calls a partial day below or at the goal", () => {
    expect(dayStatus(1500, true, 2000, true)).toBe("uncertain");
    expect(dayStatus(1500, false, 2000, true)).toBe("below");
    expect(dayStatus(2010, false, 2000, true)).toBe("at");
    expect(dayStatus(2100, true, 2000, true)).toBe("above");
    expect(dayStatus(null, true, 2000, true)).toBe("uncertain");
    expect(dayStatus(null, false, 2000, false)).toBe("none");
    expect(dayStatus(1500, false, null, true)).toBe("nogoal");
  });
});

describe("buildInsights", () => {
  const base = { range: "week" as const, anchor: "2026-11-14", today: "2026-11-14", goals };
  it("counts coverage and averages logged days only", () => {
    const result = buildInsights({ ...base, logs: [log("2026-11-14", 1000), log("2026-11-13", 2000)] });
    expect(result.elapsedDays).toBe(7);
    expect(result.loggedDays).toBe(2);
    expect(result.average.kcal).toBe(1500);
    expect(result.average.partial).toBe(false);
  });
  it("keeps unknown calories unknown and flags partial days", () => {
    const result = buildInsights({ ...base, logs: [log("2026-11-14", 500), log("2026-11-14", null), log("2026-11-13", null)] });
    const today = result.buckets.find((bucket) => bucket.key === "2026-11-14");
    const yesterday = result.buckets.find((bucket) => bucket.key === "2026-11-13");
    expect(today).toMatchObject({ kcal: 500, partial: true });
    expect(yesterday?.kcal).toBeNull();
    expect(result.average.partial).toBe(true);
    expect(result.unknownEntries).toBe(2);
  });
  it("does not count future days toward coverage", () => {
    const result = buildInsights({ ...base, range: "month", logs: [] });
    expect(result.elapsedDays).toBe(14);
    expect(result.buckets).toHaveLength(30);
  });
  it("groups meals, tags and top foods without turning unknown into zero", () => {
    const result = buildInsights({
      ...base,
      logs: [
        log("2026-11-14", 300, { tags: ["junk"], name_snapshot: "Samosa" }),
        log("2026-11-13", null, { tags: ["junk"], name_snapshot: "Samosa" }),
        log("2026-11-13", 200, { meal_type: "breakfast", name_snapshot: "Idli" }),
      ],
    });
    expect(result.tags).toEqual([{ tag: "junk", entries: 2, kcal: 300 }]);
    expect(result.meals.map((item) => item.meal)).toEqual(["breakfast", "lunch"]);
    expect(result.topFoods[0]).toEqual({ name: "Samosa", count: 2, kcal: 300 });
  });
  it("builds twelve monthly bars for a year", () => {
    const result = buildInsights({ ...base, range: "year", logs: [log("2026-11-14", 1800)] });
    expect(result.buckets).toHaveLength(12);
    expect(result.buckets[10].kcal).toBe(1800);
    expect(result.buckets[0].kcal).toBeNull();
  });
});
P9: API and screen

Replace src/app/api/insights/route.ts

ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE, isValidTimeZone, localDate } from "@/lib/dates";
import { buildInsights, isDateString, rangeBounds, type GoalVersion, type InsightLog, type RangeKind } from "@/lib/insights";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/request";

const PAGE = 1000;
type Row = {
  local_date: string; meal_type: string; name_snapshot: string;
  food_log_nutrients: unknown; food_log_tags: { tag: string }[] | null;
};

function kcalOf(embed: unknown): number | null {
  const one = Array.isArray(embed) ? embed[0] : embed;
  const value = (one as { final_totals?: { kcal?: unknown } } | null | undefined)?.final_totals?.kcal;
  return typeof value === "number" ? value : null;
}

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const auth = await requireUser(request);
  if (!auth) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

  const params = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
  const range = params.get("range") as RangeKind;
  if (!["week", "month", "year"].includes(range)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Choose week, month or year." }, { status: 400 });

  const { data: profile } = await auth.client.from("profiles").select("timezone").eq("id", auth.user.id).maybeSingle();
  const zone = profile?.timezone && isValidTimeZone(profile.timezone) ? profile.timezone : DEFAULT_TIME_ZONE;
  const today = localDate(zone);
  const anchorParam = params.get("anchor");
  if (anchorParam && !isDateString(anchorParam)) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid date." }, { status: 400 });
  const anchor = anchorParam ?? today;
  const { start, end } = rangeBounds(range, anchor);

  const logs: InsightLog[] = [];
  for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE) {
    const { data, error } = await auth.client
      .from("food_logs")
      .select("local_date,meal_type,name_snapshot,food_log_nutrients(final_totals),food_log_tags(tag)")
      .gte("local_date", start).lte("local_date", end)
      .order("local_date").order("id")
      .range(from, from + PAGE - 1);
    if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
    const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as Row[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      logs.push({
        local_date: row.local_date, meal_type: row.meal_type, name_snapshot: row.name_snapshot,
        kcal: kcalOf(row.food_log_nutrients), tags: (row.food_log_tags ?? []).map((item) => item.tag),
      });
    }
    if (rows.length < PAGE) break;
  }

  const { data: goalRows } = await auth.client.from("goal_versions").select("value,starts_at,ends_at").eq("kind", "intake_kcal");
  const goals = ((goalRows ?? []) as GoalVersion[]).map((goal) => ({ ...goal, value: Number(goal.value) }));
  return NextResponse.json({ insights: buildInsights({ range, anchor, today, logs, goals }), today, anchor });
}

New src/components/insights/InsightsExperience.tsx

tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { apiFetch } from "@/lib/api";
import { shiftAnchor, type Insights, type RangeKind } from "@/lib/insights";
import { tagLabel } from "@/lib/tags";

const RANGES: [RangeKind, string][] = [["week", "Week"], ["month", "Month"], ["year", "Year"]];
const fmt = (value: number) => value.toLocaleString("en-IN");

export function InsightsExperience() {
  const [range, setRange] = useState<RangeKind>("week");
  const [anchor, setAnchor] = useState<string | null>(null); // null means today
  const [data, setData] = useState<{ insights: Insights; today: string; anchor: string } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setError("");
    const query = `/api/insights?range=${range}${anchor ? `&anchor=${anchor}` : ""}`;
    apiFetch<{ insights: Insights; today: string; anchor: string }>(query, { signal: controller.signal })
      .then(setData)
      .catch(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setError("Insights couldn’t be loaded. Please try again.");
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [range, anchor]);

  const insights = data?.insights;
  const atEnd = insights && data ? insights.end >= data.today : true;
  const max = insights ? Math.max(1, ...insights.buckets.map((bucket) => Math.max(bucket.kcal ?? 0, bucket.goal ?? 0))) : 1;
  const unit = range === "year" ? "average per logged day" : "per day";

  return (
    <>
      <div className="ax-chips" role="group" aria-label="Range">
        {RANGES.map(([value, label]) => (
          <button key={value} className={`ax-chip ${range === value ? "selected" : ""}`} aria-pressed={range === value} onClick={() => { setRange(value); setAnchor(null); }}>{label}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {error && <p className="inline-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}

      {!insights ? (
        !error && <div className="skeleton-stack"><div /><div /><div /></div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="ax-daynav">
            <button className="button secondary" onClick={() => setAnchor(shiftAnchor(range, data?.anchor ?? insights.end, -1))} aria-label="Earlier">‹</button>
            <strong>{insights.label}</strong>
            <button className="button secondary" disabled={atEnd} onClick={() => setAnchor(shiftAnchor(range, data?.anchor ?? insights.end, 1))} aria-label="Later">›</button>
          </div>

          <section className="ax-section">
            <h2>Coverage</h2>
            <p>Logged {insights.loggedDays} of {insights.elapsedDays} day{insights.elapsedDays === 1 ? "" : "s"}.</p>
            {insights.average.kcal === null ? (
              <p className="muted">No calorie totals yet for this period.</p>
            ) : (
              <p>
                Average on logged days: <strong>{insights.average.partial ? "at least " : ""}{fmt(insights.average.kcal)} kcal</strong>
                {insights.average.partial && <span className="muted"> (some entries have unknown calories)</span>}
              </p>
            )}
            {insights.unknownEntries > 0 && <p className="muted">{insights.unknownEntries} entr{insights.unknownEntries === 1 ? "y has" : "ies have"} unknown calories and {insights.unknownEntries === 1 ? "is" : "are"} left out of totals.</p>}
          </section>

          <section className="ax-section">
            <h2>Calories ({unit})</h2>
            <div className="ax-bars" role="img" aria-label={`Bar chart of calories ${unit} for ${insights.label}`}>
              {insights.buckets.map((bucket) => (
                <div className="ax-barcol" key={bucket.key} title={bucket.kcal === null ? (bucket.logged ? "Calories unknown" : "Not logged") : `${bucket.partial ? "At least " : ""}${fmt(bucket.kcal)} kcal`}>
                  <div className="ax-bartrack">
                    {bucket.goal !== null && <i className="ax-goaltick" style={{ bottom: `${(bucket.goal / max) * 100}%` }} />}
                    {bucket.kcal !== null && <span className={`ax-barfill ${bucket.partial ? "partial" : ""}`} style={{ height: `${Math.max(2, (bucket.kcal / max) * 100)}%` }} />}
                  </div>
                  <small>{bucket.label}</small>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <p className="muted">Hatched bars are at least that much, because some entries have unknown calories. Empty means nothing logged.{insights.hasGoal ? " The dash marks your goal for that day." : ""}</p>
          </section>

          {insights.hasGoal && (
            <section className="ax-section">
              <h2>Against your goal</h2>
              <div className="ax-totals">
                <div className="tx-macro"><span>Below</span><strong>{insights.statusCounts.below}</strong></div>
                <div className="tx-macro"><span>Around</span><strong>{insights.statusCounts.at}</strong></div>
                <div className="tx-macro"><span>Above</span><strong>{insights.statusCounts.above}</strong></div>
                <div className="tx-macro"><span>Can’t tell</span><strong>{insights.statusCounts.uncertain}</strong></div>
              </div>
              <p className="muted">“Around” means within 25 kcal of the goal. “Can’t tell” is a day with unknown calories that could fall either side. Days with a goal that applied at the time are compared to that goal.</p>
            </section>
          )}

          {insights.meals.length > 0 && (
            <section className="ax-section">
              <h2>By meal</h2>
              {insights.meals.map((meal) => (
                <div className="ax-quick-row" key={meal.meal}>
                  <b style={{ textTransform: "capitalize" }}>{meal.meal}</b>
                  <span>{fmt(Math.round(meal.kcal))} kcal · {meal.entries} entr{meal.entries === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </section>
          )}

          {insights.tags.length > 0 && (
            <section className="ax-section">
              <h2>Tags</h2>
              {insights.tags.map((tag) => (
                <div className="ax-quick-row" key={tag.tag}>
                  <b>{tagLabel(tag.tag)}</b>
                  <span>{tag.entries} entr{tag.entries === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} · {fmt(Math.round(tag.kcal))} kcal</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </section>
          )}

          {insights.topFoods.length > 0 && (
            <section className="ax-section">
              <h2>Most logged</h2>
              {insights.topFoods.map((food) => (
                <div className="ax-quick-row" key={food.name}>
                  <b>{food.name}</b>
                  <span>{food.count}× · {food.kcal === null ? "calories unknown" : `${fmt(food.kcal)} kcal`}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </section>
          )}

          {insights.loggedDays === 0 && <p className="muted">Nothing logged in this period yet.</p>}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

Run the SQL first. Send me any error text exactly.

P8 checks

On Activity, add a workout with 200 kcal and one with the calories blank. The line reads “200 kcal from workouts (at least: 1 workout has no calories)”. If you’ve set an activity target in Goals, the bar fills toward it.
Enter steps, then clear the box and save. It goes back to “Not entered”, not 0.
Add a tracker: “Water”, Amount, ml, each tap 250, target 2000. Tap +250 ml a few times and the bar moves. “Undo last” removes one tap.
Add a yes/no tracker (mark done, tap to undo) and a measurement tracker such as weight (it shows the latest reading).
Use the ‹ › buttons to go back a day. Entries are kept per day. › is disabled on today.
Archive a tracker (it takes two taps). It disappears from the list, and the old entries stay in the database.

P9 checks

Open Insights. Week shows Sun–Sat style bars, with “Logged X of 7 days”.
Log one entry with unknown calories on a day. That bar is hatched, the average says “at least”, and the note counts the unknown entry.
Switch to Month and Year, and use ‹ › to go back. › is disabled when the range reaches today.
With an intake goal set, the Below/Around/Above/Can’t tell counts add up to the logged days. A day with unknown calories that is still under the goal shows under “Can’t tell”.
Tag an entry Junk. It appears under Tags. “Most logged” lists foods by calories, with “calories unknown” where that applies.

Next up is P10: offline logging that replays safely, the “update available” notice, export and delete end to end, and the final release checklist.
